- «Uh, sure, how's this: think for yourself. I don't care for politics, and I'm big on letting people figure things out for themselves. The Wookiees have prophesies of deliverance if you ask them, but for now they follow without hesitation. That is their wish.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «Maddened with grief, perhaps, but not crazed. I helped him pass to the lower forests where only a Wookiee could follow. Some other matters will determine if you can follow. There is a barrier that... well, we'll talk of that more in a moment.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «It is a very unfortunate matter. It will end in shame and sorrow. You will find Rorworr down here. And more. I have witnessed a number of happenings, but I have left them for others to sort out.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «On the body of Rorworr, I know. His death will not go unnoticed. I have been wondering how this foolishness would play out. I am no weaponsmith, but someone in the village will be able to identify the owner of that bolt casing. They are very personal items.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «I wasn't aware I had to be. Look, events like these reveal much about the people involved. I'm well past that introspective tripe. I'm not saying that outside help isn't needed. I just... well... I didn't want to make anyone mad while I didn't have a way off the planet. We'll talk more about that momentarily.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «Yes, I could feel it as you walked near. And I don't sense the smell of death on you. You spared them? Interesting.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «Wasn't there? I suppose not, but the environment would say otherwise, if it could talk. No matter.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «If you imagined a meaning behind it, I suppose that's good, but I don't think such a trivial thing is any real measure of a person.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «You were here and it needed doing. Besides, dealing with the Czerka is dirty business. I hope you washed your hands.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «Yes, I heard the fuss of it. I told you I wanted a non-violent solution, and I had my reasons for it. You should have thought about that.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «Czerka Corporation will find the bodies and blame the Wookiees. This may cause more troops to be deployed. That means more disruption. I'm not pleased, but what's done is done and I'm not here to judge you. Hopefully you'll take care of that yourself.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «Well, perhaps I have a duty to point out what you should have learned from this. Or perhaps... no.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «You're pretty much right, really. Truth be known, I won't judge how you fared... as long as you can LIVE WITH YOURSELF!» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «Was that convincing? It's been a while since I've been in the company of someone that needed that kind of babysitting.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «I see. You are completely detached. Are you sure you're not a droid? It doesn't matter. A trivial thing like this is no measure of anyone.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «Oh, go bark up a tree. You'll get the same response out of me. Besides, I'm not about to get in the habit of grading you.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «A good teacher, and I'm not saying I am one, but a good teacher doesn't mash a book in a student's face shouting "learn this bit here!" If you can't look at the situation for yourself and see the logic behind my way of doing things, you're not the type to understand me anyway. Besides, you're a little old for that master/student fuss, aren't you? I know I am.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «Yes, yes, in the east past the poacher camp. I saw it when it was installed. It's a very effective barrier, but I know how to get past it.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «Yes, of course. Let's get moving. I noticed the old paths some time ago. You probably wouldn't have found them on your own.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «I know where you want to go. I can get you through to the old paths. This forest hides some strange things.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «There's also a Czerka repulsor field blocking the way, past the poacher camp in the east. I saw it when it was installed, so I know how to pass it.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «And on the other side... ancient answers. And perhaps a Wookiee you were seeking. I hope he's in the mood for visitors.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «There, you see? Beautifully subtle, isn't it? At least, compared to other Czerka equipment dumped down here. It's only been here a short while, or the Wookiees would have disabled it. They wouldn't have had an easy time of it, though.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «Jolee: Here we are again. Shall I bypass the field so we may pass?» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «There are others, each blocking similar points on certain paths. It is all very calculated. Very precise. It would have been effective if it hadn't relied on the creatures to be walking. Climbers don't have much trouble getting around it.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «You aren't going to ask about it? A repulsor field in the woods and you have no interest?» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «I think it was an attempt to block the more dangerous carnivores from wandering freely, but it's not very effective. It limits us non-climbers, but the creatures of Kashyyyk are very adaptable. You'll see why when we reach your goal.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «All right then, uh, how did it go...» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «I can manipulate it for a moment. Let me see... how did the Czerka engineers do it...» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- «Ahh, there we go. Now keep moving. These are the most dangerous depths of Kashyyyk. A few surprises wait for us, I'll wager.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]

**[K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]**
- «Well, a gifted few, perhaps.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]

**[K1/Woorwill's_Home]**
- «We're rather hairless, aren't we?» [K1/Woorwill's_Home]
- «*sigh* This will end in sadness. Don't say I didn't warn you.» [K1/Woorwill's_Home]

**[K1/Worrroznor's_Home]**
- «I urge you caution. This may seem like the right path to follow... but you do not know these people as I do.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- «A painful end, but perhaps it is the right thing to do. We shall see.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- «There was pain no matter how this ended. Perhaps this is for the best.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]

### Quote bank B: what others say about Jolee Bindo (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K1/Bastila]**
- Bastila: «Jolee, may I have a moment? There is something I wish to speak to you about.» [K1/Bastila] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Bastila: «I know you have... issues... with the Order. But you are a Jedi, Jolee. You command the Force. Without the guidance of the Council how can you avoid falling to the dark side?» [K1/Bastila] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K1/Black_Vulkar_Base_(Garage)]**
- Kandon Ark: «Excellent. Here – you might need these for this mission. They're access codes to Gadon's private chambers in the Bek base. Without them you won't be able to get at the old man. My guards will escort you to the main doors of our base. After you have killed Gadon return to the main doors and I will arrange for you to ride for the Vulkars in the swoop race. I'd suggest you don't come back until the job is done, or we might think you've decided to renegotiate on our offer. The Vulkars won't consider you a friend until Gadon is dead.» [K1/Black_Vulkar_Base_(Garage)]

**[K1/Carth]**
- Carth: «So, Jolee, you decided to leave your little hermitage in the forest and come help us stop the Sith. I guess you realized this was worth coming out of retirement for, huh?» [K1/Carth] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K1/Hangar]**
- Computer: «Sorting by identity. Three attempts by the Wookiee Freyyr, all denied. 152 attempts by human Jolee Bindo, all denied.» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K1/Jolee_Bindo]**
- Davin Kotras: «Ha! Jolee! Jolee Bindo? Why, you old freak! Last time I saw you, you were still wandering the galaxy in that old tug. I thought you were dead, old man.» [K1/Jolee_Bindo]

**[K1/Leviathan]**
- Sith Trooper: «I... I agree, Commander. After speaking with the old man I think we should question him away from the others.» [K1/Leviathan]

**[K1/Lower_Shadowlands]**
- Freyyr: «The old hermit saved me, I think. He clouded their minds for a moment. I have had a long time to hide... and regret.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]

**[K1/Prison_Block]**
- Bastila: «Well done, Jolee. I knew we could count on you. A Jedi never fails to get the task done!» [K1/Prison_Block]

**[K1/Sith_Academy]**
- Dustil Onasi: «I don't see the old man with you. Did he come to his senses and leave, finally? I knew he couldn't prove anything. I don't know why you're still here. Why don't you just follow him wherever he went? You and I have no business at all.» [K1/Sith_Academy]

**[K1/Star_Forge_System]**
- Carth: «Well, if Bastila is on the Star Forge like you think, Jolee, then we can't rescue her until we disable that disruptor field. The sooner we investigate that temple to the east the better. We can probably find the wreckage of a downed ship along the way. If we're lucky we can salvage some stabilizers from it to get off this planet.» [K1/Star_Forge_System]

**[K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]**
- Elora: «I was told that you're a friend of Jolee Bindo... and that Jolee has returned to the sector. Please, I must speak to him. It's very urgent!» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Elora: «My name is Elora. My husband Sunry and Jolee are old friends. Now Sunry needs Jolee's help.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Elora: «Someone is out to destroy my husband. I... I don't know who I can trust. I don't know you. But Jolee - he was always a true friend to Sunry.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Elora: «Please, I beg you to bring Jolee to speak to me about my husband. Sunry's life depends on it!» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Elora: «Jolee! It *is* you! I... need your help.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Elora: «It's horrible, Jolee! Sunry has been arrested! The Sith have accused him of murder!» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Elora: «It's all a mistake, Jolee! Sunry isn't a murderer - someone is trying to frame him.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «Jolee! What... what are you doing here?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «Just like old times, eh Jolee? You come swooping in out of nowhere to save my tail from the fire.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «Jolee? What's he doing here on Manaan? Never mind - I'm just glad he's here. So is this some kind of prison break? If it is we won't get very far before the Selkath cut us down I can tell ya.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «Jolee - coming to check in on an old man? I must confess, I... I would rather you were here to speak to the warden and begin my trial.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «You do whatever you have to, Jolee. I know what I did, but I also know what's at stake, here.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «Thank you, Jolee! I... I don't know what I would have done if you hadn't shown up!» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]

**[K1/Temple_Exterior]**
- Juhani: «Jolee speaks for both of us. You must enter the Temple, but we must go with you.» [K1/Temple_Exterior]

**[K1/Temple_Summit]**
- Carth: «Where are Juhani and Jolee Bindo? Are they okay? What happened inside that temple?» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Carth: «Where's Jolee Bindo? Is he okay? What happened inside that temple?» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Bastila: «Juhani and Jolee would not swear loyalty to the new ruler of the Sith. They died for their foolishness. Now swear loyalty to Darth Revan, or face your own death!» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Bastila: «Jolee would not swear loyalty to the new ruler of the Sith. He died for his foolishness. Now swear loyalty to Darth Revan, or face your own death!» [K1/Temple_Summit]

**[K1/Upper_Shadowlands]**
- Commander Dern: «Besides you? Besides any number of predators that can kill in seconds? I suppose there's old Bindo. He's got a camp here somewhere. I'd leave him alone, though. Anyone who'd live down here is just plain crazy. There's not much else. We've got a repulsor field to the east so not much passes through here now.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Commander Dern: «Is that old hermit Jolee trying to talk you into doing his dirty work? We've spent too much on setting up just to walk away. As long as the sonic emitters keep the preds away and I've got bodies to guard the perimeter, we're staying right here. I mean, if I can put up with my own worthless guards complaining about this place, Jolee whining through you is nothing.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Force Field Speaker: «[This is obviously one of the barriers to your progress that Jolee was speaking of. If he were here, he would allow you to pass it.]» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]

### Quote bank B2: pronoun references resolved from context (context-resolved, hand-reviewed scene-wide (each line read by hand against the whole conversation; anchor = the line that names the character))
- [context-resolved] Chuundar: «So, the insane outsider is real. Interesting. I trust you are removing him? I'd hate to see him come to harm.» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain] (anchor: I met the hermit Jolee Bindo down there....)
- [context-resolved] Chuundar: «Yes, better that he leave. It's hard to blame the Shadowlands for missing Wookiees when he lives down there.» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain] (anchor: I met the hermit Jolee Bindo down there....)

### Quote bank D: lines addressed to Jolee Bindo (Heuristic: lines in second person spoken directly before or after one of this character's lines on the same page)

**[K1/Ahto_West]**
- Nilko Bwaas: «Please - see if you can find the answers I seek. Find why the Republic is hiring so many mercenaries, find where they are going. Find why they do not return.» [K1/Ahto_West]
- Nilko Bwaas: «You must tell the Republic to stop this madness at once. Make them shut down the facility and recall their people before they do something we all regret.» [K1/Ahto_West]
- Mission: «I know you're supposed to save the galaxy and all, but could you try being less of a scummy core slime while you're doing it?» [K1/Ahto_West] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Zaalbar: «I am proud to call you my friend, {{s» [K1/Ahto_West]

**[K1/Anchorhead]**
- Juhani: «You should not act this way. You are treading a dangerous path I fear.» [K1/Anchorhead]
- Mission: «You know what? Sometimes you're nothing but a bully!» [K1/Anchorhead] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Bastila: «It troubles me to see you behaving this way. Such acts can lead to the dark side.» [K1/Anchorhead] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Bastila: «Thank you for helping this woman. It may not seem like such a great thing to you, but you are making a difference.» [K1/Anchorhead]
- Sharina Fizark: «For whatever it means, may the Force be with you. You've saved us.» [K1/Anchorhead]
- Sandcrawler Mechanic: «[Success] What, is this a mugging or are you begging? Look, here's a few creds, now leave me alone.» [K1/Anchorhead]
- Juhani: «You are a representative of the Jedi Order. When you behave this way you disgrace us all!» [K1/Anchorhead]
- Bastila: «*whisper* The Force is not a thing to be trifled with. Continue to use it frivolously and you may find yourself straying down the dark path.» [K1/Anchorhead]
- Carth: «I'm no Jedi, but are you really supposed to be using the Force for things like this?» [K1/Anchorhead]
- Carth: «What. You're joking, right? Just because he's small doesn't mean he's any more trustworthy than a Gamorrean, Bastila. Don't be naive.» [K1/Anchorhead]
- Mission: «Hey, why are you being so mean to this little guy? What did he ever do to you?» [K1/Anchorhead]

**[K1/Bastila]**
- Bastila: «I see you are quite adamant. No doubt you've had ample time to think on this during your long seclusion. I guess it was foolish of me to think I could sway your position so easily.» [K1/Bastila] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Mission: «Hey, Bastila. You ever just use the Force for fun? You know, a little jolt of the Force to trip some jerk who's ticking you off?» [K1/Bastila]

**[K1/Cantina]**
- Bastila: «Your sarcasm is not appreciated, Carth.» [K1/Cantina]
- Jawa Server: «The voices of Jawa under Iziz are not flawed. The ears of your kin are poor while we speak plain of our missing.» [K1/Cantina]

**[K1/Carth]**
- Carth: «Okay, old man, you lost me there. Are you trying to make a point?» [K1/Carth] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Mission: «Hey, Carth. You're a pilot for the Republic, right? You've been all over the galaxy I bet, right? So tell me, how would you rate Taris compared to other worlds you've seen?» [K1/Carth]

**[K1/Courtyard]**
- Carth: «Why did you do that? Sometimes... sometimes I just can't understand how you can be so cruel.» [K1/Courtyard]

**[K1/Czerka_Landing_Port]**
- Bastila: «[Force Persuade] You wish to tell us more about this agreement with the Wookiee leadership.» [K1/Czerka_Landing_Port]

**[K1/Czerka_Office]**
- Protocol Officer: «[Success] And I wouldn't want you to leave without this additional reward. Yes, of course I wouldn't.» [K1/Czerka_Office]

**[K1/Docking_Bay_(Tatooine)]**
- Customs Officer: «[Success] You know… I don't think you need to pay the fee. We'll let it go, this time.» [K1/Docking_Bay_(Tatooine)]

**[K1/Dreshdae]**
- Shaardan: «Let me pose a question to you. These hopefuls will never survive in the academy. A lesson must be taught, here, but I am at a loss as to what form it should take.» [K1/Dreshdae]
- Lashowe: «Those are very brave words for such an insignificant person. Do you not realize how many Sith are here in Dreshdae?» [K1/Dreshdae]
- Canderous: «Not enough to save your sorry self.» [K1/Dreshdae]

**[K1/Droid_Shop]**
- Mission: «Gee, that doesn't sound suspicious at all. Warehouse workers have a bad habit of paying off their debts with other people's property, you know.» [K1/Droid_Shop]
- Yuka Laka: «[Success] I believe you actually would tell people of my business. All right, but this is the last offer! 2500 credits!» [K1/Droid_Shop]
- Juhani: «*whisper* You should treat your power with respect, not use the Force on a whim like it is some toy!» [K1/Droid_Shop] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K1/East_Central]**
- Juhani: «I see you are more interested in winning than in trying to solve this case. I cannot say I approve of your actions here.» [K1/East_Central]
- Bastila: «I know you want to help Sunry, but we should not be manufacturing evidence like this.» [K1/East_Central]

**[K1/Eastern_Dune_Sea]**
- Carth: «There's an unwritten code of loyalty. You just don't turn on your allies like this. Any good soldier would tell you that.» [K1/Eastern_Dune_Sea] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Komad Fortuna: «I have spent many years following in the memory of my father. I can't step away from a battle like this. No words can express my distaste for you. If you intend to take what I worked for, you will have to fight for it.» [K1/Eastern_Dune_Sea]
- Komad Fortuna: «And I will warn you, no words will change my mind. Regardless of the circumstances, respect for the hunt governs me. I will die defending it.» [K1/Eastern_Dune_Sea]
- Mission: «Hey - it's one of those Star Maps, ain't it? Kind of weird finding it in the back of a krayt dragon's lair, don't you think?» [K1/Eastern_Dune_Sea]
- Juhani: «The Star Map. Even after all thousands of years it has been here,  it still has tremendous power. Can you not feel it?» [K1/Eastern_Dune_Sea]

**[K1/Ebon_Hawk]**
- Juhani: «I do not know how to help you with that, I am sorry.» [K1/Ebon_Hawk] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K1/Great_Walkway]**
- Gorwooken: «No, it cannot be you. You are too frail and old. The other is mad like a crazed tach.» [K1/Great_Walkway]

**[K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]**
- Zaalbar: «Then there is no other way. Chuundar! You have betrayed our people! You must pay!» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]
- Mission: «I... guess he *did* toss you out on your ear, Big Z. What kind of father is that?» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]
- Bastila: «Are you sure this is wise? To support a slaver feels... wrong.» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]
- Mission: «Look, Big Z may owe you a life-debt but you've got to cut him some slack!» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]
- Juhani: «You should not be so self-serving, lest you fall to the dark side.» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]
- Wookiee: «I don't know about that. You outsiders from away are always looking for strange things. It makes you crazy. Maybe you should search for that fool living in the Shadowlands. Our hunters say they see him from time to time and sometimes he helps them.» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]
- Chuundar: «Now I think you are just making things up. Nothing is older than the forest, not even we Wookiees.» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]
- Chuundar: «I might send someone to try and find this ancient computer, but I think you are mistaken. Crazy outsider.» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]
- Mission: «You're not fooling anyone, ya know.» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]
- Mission: «If you say so, Big Z.» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]
- Chuundar: «That finishes our business. Take care, Zaalbar. We'll welcome you back someday. When I decide the people are ready. Not before.» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]

**[K1/Hangar]**
- Mission: «You mean she's... she's dead?» [K1/Hangar]
- HK-47: «Observation: That does not matter, master. I am your droid, regardless of your actions or personality.» [K1/Hangar]
- Computer: «Begin socialized interface. Neural scan indicates positive identification of subject "Revan". No further testing of the subject is required. I will brief you as programmed. You are Lord Revan. I am waiting for your request to begin transfer of the Star Map.» [K1/Hangar]
- Computer: «List of attempts prior to this were removed from memory five years ago, as per your instruction, Lord Revan.» [K1/Hangar]

**[K1/Hunting_Lodge]**
- Gurke: «What you want, humee? We said no more talking, and we meant it. Go away.» [K1/Hunting_Lodge]
- Tanis Venn: «Well of course, darling, whatever Tanis Venn can do. You know, very few human females come to Tatooine...» [K1/Hunting_Lodge]
- Tanis Venn: «Ask your questions. Please.» [K1/Hunting_Lodge]

**[K1/Jolee_Bindo]**
- Davin Kotras: «Excuse me, have you seen a mechanic about? Short fellow, bald. I've been looking --» [K1/Jolee_Bindo]
- Davin Kotras: «Yeah, yeah. I'll just bet. Damn, old man, you look exactly the same as the last time I saw you! You pickle well.» [K1/Jolee_Bindo]
- Davin Kotras: «Of course, old man. Oh, wait... if you've been traveling in this sector, I suppose you heard about Sunry?» [K1/Jolee_Bindo]
- Davin Kotras: «I know. Elora is beside herself, as you can imagine. Maybe you could help them? I'm sure Sunry would appreciate a Jedi's assistance right about now.» [K1/Jolee_Bindo]
- Davin Kotras: «Same here. Well, heh, it was good to see you again. Give my regards to Elora, if you see her.» [K1/Jolee_Bindo]

**[K1/Lower_Shadowlands]**
- Computer: «In this instance, the Wookiee is unreliable. His family has been a mire of treachery. I judge the answer correct. You display the proper behavior to match the pattern in memory. Evaluation will continue.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «More of you Czerka core-rats? Is even the heart of Kashyyyk not free from your kind?» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «After years in the Shadowlands, I remember only that outsiders are not to be trusted! I'll see you dead!» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Freyyr: «For years I have hid, hoping to someday free my world! I swear you will not take me! I swear!» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Gorwooken: «Very well. I will wait here for you to return.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- HK-47: «Observation: Your ruthlessness and cruelty are quite refreshing in an organic meatbag, master. Are you sure you aren't part droid?» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]

**[K1/North_Beach]**
- HK-47: «Query: You are able to comprehend the speech patterns of these organics? You must have visited this planet and learned their language in your previous incarnation as Darth Revan.» [K1/North_Beach]
- Juhani: «You understand these aliens? Of course... you must have come here and learned their languge in your search for the secrets of the Star Forge. Back when you were still Revan.» [K1/North_Beach]

**[K1/Prison_Block]**
- Mission: «Or I could use the keycard I lifted from your pocket to slice into the security panel and get myself out of this cell. Piece of cake! I wonder when people will stop underestimating me?» [K1/Prison_Block]
- Sith Guard: «What do you want, old man? You better not be trying to cause any trouble or you'll be sorry!» [K1/Prison_Block]
- Sith Guard: «Uh... yes. It's too drafty in there. Your old bones might catch a chill. We don't want that. Get out of there.» [K1/Prison_Block]
- Sith Guard: «Huh? What... what just happened? What am I doing in here? Damn you, old man! I'll kill you if I ever get out of here!» [K1/Prison_Block]
- Rodian Prisoner: «You there. Little droid. You not Sith droid. What you doing here?» [K1/Prison_Block]

**[K1/Sith_Academy]**
- Uthar Wynn: «I see. It is good that you have come to me with this information, young one. It is a bit... ironic... that Yuthura has begun her plotting. I have been aware of her growing ambitions for some time, and had in fact already decided to remove her. Normally, the one who gains the most prestige would engage in a final trial... two of the students would fight. This time it will be Yuthura who battles, though she does not know it. Perhaps it will be you who combats her? Yes... perhaps so. This is what you can do. Give this pad to Adrenas... he will put some poison in her bath. This will weaken Yuthura for that final test, making her an easy target. Rather generous of me, don't you think?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Carth: «I'd love to know what's going on in that tricky mind of yours.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Zaalbar: «This is not wise. She'll be as willing to lie as you are.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Canderous: «Shut up, old man, and don't spoil anything or I'll twist that scrawny neck of yours.» [K1/Sith_Academy]

**[K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]**
- Mekel: «Hmm. It is a bit boring standing out here all day, however. I think I'll go for some dinner... it will be fun to think of them while I gorge myself. They'll still be here in an hour or two, surely. I suggest you run along before I decide to make you part of the fun.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- HK-47: «Observation: Marvelous elimination of the enemy, master! I applaud you!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Sith Academy Guard: «So long as the Sith do not object to them, neither will I. They will be your responsibility, of course.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Prospective Sith: «[Failure] That sounds like it would be suicide. You are obviously lying to me.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Juhani: «You should not do this. Taking advantage, even of a Sith, leads to the dark side.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Prospective Sith: «[Success] I... but... oh, you're right. It's hopeless. *sigh* I... I guess I should go home. Perhaps I could get a job at the droid shop...» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]

**[K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]**
- Dark Jedi Master: «How did you get in here? Wait - I recognize you! Lord Malak was most displeased when he learned you had escaped Taris alive! He has promised a great reward to whoever destroys you.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- HK-47: «Commentary: It is not possible to destroy the master. It is suggested you run while my blasters warm up, meatbags.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Selkath Apprentice: «Master, give us the honor of aiding you in destroying this enemy of the Sith.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Dark Jedi Master: «As you wish, my eager apprentices. We shall remove this thorn from Malak's side once and for all!» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Carth: «*whisper* How could you say something like that? What's gotten into you?» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Bastila: «*whisper* How does promoting the Sith help our cause? Think about what you are doing!» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Shasa: «[Failure] Do you think we are weak minded fools? Our Sith Masters have taught us how to resist such simple Jedi mind tricks! You have no proof of your claims - that is why you must resort to such tactics to make us believe you. The Sith would not try deceive us like this!» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Bastila: «I can understand why you're doing this, but surely it can't be that hard to *prove* the evil of the dark side, can it?» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Bastila: «You have been saved from more than you know. Given time, the Sith would have turned you fully to the dark side... and you would have betrayed your world gladly.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]

**[K1/Star_Forge_System]**
- Juhani: «During our rather rapid descent I noticed the hulls of many crashed ships scattered across the landscape. Perhaps the parts you need can be found among their wreckage.» [K1/Star_Forge_System]

**[K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]**
- Elora: «I didn't. Nobody knew where you were, not even Sunry! But I heard the Selkath mention you and your friends. The Force has brought you to help us!» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Elora: «Sunry's being held at the Selkath courts. They won't let anyone in to see him. Please, go to the courts, talk to the Judges. Maybe the Selkath will listen to you.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Shelkar: «That is acceptable, I believe. In Manaan legal tradition the defendant can have an Arbiter who tries to prove his innocence in court. This Arbiter is a neutral party that can investigate and present evidence in the trial, and argue on the defendant's behalf. As you are a Jedi, I believe you could fulfill this role adequately.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Shelkar: «So be it! You are now appointed Arbiter in the case of Sunry vs. The Sith Empire. Your name and position have been recorded in our files. I will now inform you of the pertinent facts in this case. You have a limited amount of time in which to investigate and organize a defense of your client, and I advise you to use it wisely.<br/><br/>Sunry was seen leaving a hotel, leaving behind a dead Sith woman - Elassa Huros. He has been charged with murder and is being held in the Ahto City prison facilities. Due to his crippled status he is being kept in a solitary holding cell. Witnesses claimed to have seen the killing and are being detained at the hotel in question pending the trial. The manager of the hotel has now been informed of your appointment. Elassa was found dead of a blaster wound with an incriminating Republic medal clutched in her hand.<br/><br/>This information has been uploaded to your datapad. It would be wise to review all information before the trial starts. You may question Sunry or the witnesses. The evidence in this case is heavily stacked against Mr. Sunry. I believe it will take some doing to absolve him of guilt.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Shelkar: «You may also interview the judges here, including myself, to get an idea of the pertinent factors in this case. Since this is also your first case, and you are an off-worlder, I feel compelled to add "good luck" as my final missive.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Carth: «Hey! I thought you Selkath judges were supposed to follow the law! You can't already have decided that Sunry is guilty!» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Bastila: «If you had listened, Carth, you would know that that *is* the law. Sunry is guilty until proven innocent.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Shelkar: «Sunry is known to us as a hero of the Republic in your perpetual conflicts with the Sith Empire. While he may have been severely injured in the line of duty, I personally hold him in quite high regard.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Shelkar: «Is there another matter you wish to inquire about, human?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Elora: «[Failure] What?? Don't insult us both! Sunry is... Sunry is a just and honorable man. How dare you accuse him... us, of such a thing!» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Bastila: «Madam. I would remind you that I am a Jedi. Now, then... was he having an affair?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «Who are you? What are you doing here?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «Ah, I see you're back. Not that I don't appreciate the visit, but I had hoped you were going to speak to the warden so we might begin the trial.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «I grow weary of staring at these four bare walls. But I suppose you know what you are doing. You know the role of Arbiter cannot be taken lightly.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «You think I'm some kind of monster, don't you? All I did was kill a Sith! How many Sith have you killed? Dozens? Hundreds? Thousands?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Carth: «You're talking about acts of war. We're just defending ourselves! You murdered your girlfriend in her sleep.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «If you turn me in, the Republic will likely lose its kolto export privileges and then we'll lose the war for sure. Are you going to send all those thousands, millions - like on Taris - to their deaths, just for your sense of 'justice'?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «I'm glad you decided to see reason in this. If I'm convicted it could cause trouble for the Republic embassy here on Manaan. And none of us want that, now do we?» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Carth: «I knew you wouldn't let the Republic down!» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Canderous: «I guess you got the result you wanted, but I still think this was a big waste of time. We Mandalorians prefer to settle things with a nice, quick trial by combat.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Sunry: «Thank you for saving me. I don't know how I can repay you. Elora and I are going to leave here and get as far away from all this as soon as we can. I - and the Republic - will not forget what you've done for us. Thank you.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]
- Judge Shelkar: «Your task with the courts has been completed, human. You have fulfilled your role as Arbiter to the satisfaction of this court. Should you have any other grievances you wish to present, give them to the judge on duty at that time.» [K1/Sunry_Murder_Trial]

**[K1/Swoop_Registration]**
- Mission: «That isn't fair! On Taris you had to give a cut to the swoop gang sponsoring you, but at least the rider got the higher share of the winnings.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Motta the Hutt: «You're a swoop-jock like him. You're new, but if you win enough he'll respect you. I'm counting on it. And if he doesn't, *make* him choose correctly. You look like you could scare some sense into him.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- HK-47: «Threat: Guards give up, meatbag. Assassin droids will hunt your whole family tree. Reconsider.» [K1/Swoop_Registration] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Bastila: «[Force Persuade] I have heard enough. You want to act in a fair manner. Now.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Zaalbar: «How is this different than slavery? You push the boy against his will.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Zoriis Bafka: «Now, if we are through, I must get back to my race preparations. I prefer to leave your unpleasantness behind.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]

**[K1/Temple_Exterior]**
- Juhani: «We have had a... a premonition. The Force has given us a vision. There is great danger within the Temple. We cannot let you face it alone.» [K1/Temple_Exterior]
- Guide: «No other can go with you into the Temple. You must enter alone. That is the way of the ancient ritual.» [K1/Temple_Exterior]
- Guide: «No! You must enter the Temple alone! I will not lower the shields!» [K1/Temple_Exterior]
- Guide: «No... do not hurt me! I... I will do as you command. I will begin the ritual.» [K1/Temple_Exterior]

**[K1/Temple_Summit]**
- Bastila: «Escape? You don't understand. I have sworn allegiance to Lord Malak and the Sith; I am no longer a pawn of the Jedi Council.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Juhani: «Bastila, it is not too late for you to be saved. The teachings of the Jedi can lead you from the dark side back into the light and a true understanding of the Force.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Bastila: «You are beneath my contempt, Juhani. When you felt the power of the dark side you fled to a cave like some cowering animal! You know nothing of the Force or its true potential!» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Bastila: «Shut up, old man. Your time is over! The age of the Jedi and the Republic is no more! This is the age of Darth Revan and the Sith!» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Juhani: «A true Jedi will never bow down to the Sith. If this is your decision I have no choice but to do battle against you.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Bastila: «You are a pathetic fool, Revan! Together we could have defeated Malak and ruled over an Empire, but now I will be at Lord Malak's side instead! You will be crushed with the Republic and all the fools who bow down to the Jedi Council! No one can stand against the power of the Star Forge and the Sith fleet!» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Juhani: «She is not dead, Carth - though maybe that might have been easier for you to accept. She has fallen to the dark side and fled to the Star Forge.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Carth: «So... you did it? You turned against Revan once and for all? I knew she wasn't part of you anymore. I...I knew it.» [K1/Temple_Summit]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]**
- Carth: «You wouldn't have considered, oh, I don't know... getting it yourself?» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]**
- Rogue Assassin Droid: «There is no need for things to come to that, however. If you could perform a task for me, then we could both leave here in peace.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]**
- Juhani: «You can always turn back to the light.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]

**[K1/Upper_Shadowlands]**
- Mission: «It's kind of nice to meet a Jedi who isn't all hung up on this light side, dark side stuff. It gets pretty tiring, you know?» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Bastila: «I can feel the power of the Force within you, old man, but I do not sense the taint of the dark side. I think you are a servant of the light, despite what you claim.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]

**[K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]**
- Wookiee: «Our hunters say one of you has been living in the Shadowlands, and that is crazy! I don't know how he survives. It's probably just hunter-tales, though. No one could live in the Shadowlands for the years he has. No one.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee: «Maybe insanity makes you taste bad. I don't know. You tell me.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]

**[K1/Woorwill's_Home]**
- Woorwill: «I've... I've never spoken to an outsider. I mean, Chuundar makes us learn to understand you, but I've never been this close to one of you.» [K1/Woorwill's_Home]
- HK-47: «Warning: And get no closer, hairy organic! I will protect the master from your barbaric ways, I warn you!» [K1/Woorwill's_Home]
- Mission: «Yours? Why would we find your casing beside... no, it couldn't be. Could it?» [K1/Woorwill's_Home]

**[K1/Worrroznor's_Home]**
- Zaalbar: «My life debt won't let me speak for you, {{s» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
- Zaalbar: «See how this Wookiee truly understands honor? Do you see how important we consider these things, {{s» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]

### Quote bank C: walkthrough narration (feats and scene events; narration, not dialog)
- «Once Jolee joins you and you leave Kashyyyk, if he is in your party then an elderly man named Davin Kotras may approach and speak to you in one of the following locations:» [K1/Jolee_Bindo, narration]
- «Surprisingly, you don't actually have to speak to Davin to activate the ../Sunry Murder Trial/ in Ahto West on Manaan: Jolee joining your party is sufficient. Once Davin has left, you can ask Jol» [K1/Jolee_Bindo, narration]
- «If you don't speak to Jolee when he's in your party after leaving Kashyyyk, then you may be prompted to do so:» [K1/Jolee_Bindo, narration]
- «Jolee needs to be persuaded to talk:» [K1/Jolee_Bindo, narration]
- «Jolee may speak to other party members as well when both are in your party:» [K1/Jolee_Bindo, narration]
- «Even if you don't speak to Jolee directly he may comment during dialog when in your party, although unlocking this may also be conditional on others being added or removed, among other things:» [K1/Jolee_Bindo, narration]
- «Normally, if you ask your party if they know anything about this place while still aboard the Ebon Hawk, they respond as they do upon disembarking. Anywhere other than Kashyyyk, Jolee is a pedant» [K1/Ebon_Hawk, narration]
- «If Jolee isn't in your party, Carth or Zaalbar may react:» [K1/Hunting_Lodge, narration]
- «However, even this isn't necessary if Zaalbar, Canderous, HK-47, Jolee or Bastila backs up your threat:» [K1/Swoop_Registration, narration]
- «However, even this isn't necessary if Zaalbar, Canderous, HK-47 or Jolee backs up your threat:» [K1/Swoop_Registration, narration]

## Juhani
*Corpus coverage: 415 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 34 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 4 pronoun references resolved from context · 107 lines addressed to them · 14 narration lines · 45 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Juhani is a Cathar from Taris. Revan's troops saved her during the Mandalorian Wars: «It was Revan, leading an army out to defeat the Mandalorians.» [K1/Hangar]. She believed she had killed her master Quatra and retreated to Dantooine's grove.
### Personality and motivations
Insecure and haunted by her anger: «The lure of the dark side will always be my strongest foe.» [K1/Juhani].
### Arc (scene by scene)
- **Dantooine grove:** the player either redeems her by revealing Quatra survived («Quatra's injuries were not so severe as I first believed.» [K1/Grove]) or kills her.
- **Xor quest:** the encounter with Xor, who taunts Juhani [K1/Juhani].
- **Dark ending:** turns against the dark Revan.
### Relationships
- Quatra.
- Belaya.
- Revan.
- Xor (enemy).
### Abilities
- Cathar ferocity; Force Camouflage-type stealth (game mechanics).
### Feats
- Defeated Quatra in a staged fight (it was a test).
### Power-scaling statements
- None.
### Duels and fights
- vs the player in the grove (loses).
- Xor confrontation.
### Verdict implications
- Mid-low tier.
- Confidence: medium.
### Open questions
- None.

### Abilities: game mechanics (FLAGGED: gameplay stats, not lore)
- [K1/Grove] Juhani: Model Juhani (Grove), Class Jedi Guardian, Level 9, Vitality 150, Force 77, Defense 22. Powers: Burst of Speed, Choke, DC=17, Force Aura, Force Push. Feats: DC=10, Improved Flurry, Knight Sense, Master Jedi Defense.
- [K1/Party] Juhani: Model Juhani, Class Jedi Guardian, Level 6, Alignment 70 (light). Powers: Affect Mind, Burst of Speed, DC=13, Energy Resistance, Force Aura, Force Push, Stun, Throw Lightsaber. Feats: BBD=6, Improved Force Jump, Knight Sense.

### Cut content (RCM)
- None found on the RCM-tagged lines for this character.

- «I, Juhani, shall be your death!» [K1/Grove]

- «I am pathetic. I sit here and think myself to be great by embracing the dark side, but I am nothing! There is no way I could be turned back! I always thought they held me back, were jealous of my power. But it is only because I was not good enough to meet their standards... I never have been.» [K1/Grove]
- «[Success] I thank you for your kind words, Jedi.» [K1/Grove]
- «You seem to know just what affects me...» [K1/Grove]
- «I seem to still have much to learn... Both about being a Jedi, and about myself. But I wish the cost of my ignorance had not been so high. I wish that my Master had not suffered because of me.» [K1/Grove]
- «[Success] I only wish things could have been different...» [K1/Grove]
- «If she were alive now, there would be so much I would say to her... So much I would apologize for.» [K1/Grove]

- «If I show them I am free of passion... That I am serene... That I am willing to forsake the dark side.» [K1/Grove]

- «[Success] I... I thank you, master Jedi.» [K1/Grove]
- «I... I thank you, master Jedi.» [K1/Grove]

- «If only there was some way I could make this up to you...» [K1/Grove]

- «They came to me! They sought me out! So it is their failure that I have fallen. Come back when you have something more meaningful to say.» [K1/Grove]

- «I do not know what to do. Now that I have caused such suffering to my Master and those around me...» [K1/Grove]
- «[Failure] It is! It is!» [K1/Grove]

- «Life is suffering, yes... but it was by my conscious choice!» [K1/Grove]
- «It was I who hurt her, I who killed her! Her suffering is my fault alone! Leave me to my pain!» [K1/Grove]
- «[Failure] The Council may forgive a lot... But not this.» [K1/Grove]
- «The Council may forgive a lot... But not this.» [K1/Grove]
- «You... You monster! To mock my grief that way! Leave me at once, lest I strike you down!» [K1/Grove]
- «And denied! No, I cannot! And so here I will stay. Leave me to my hopelessness!» [K1/Grove]
- «I am lost! Even though I would repent, there is nowhere left for me to go! Leave me to my hopelessness!» [K1/Grove]
- «How can I ever face the Council now with what I have done? It is hopeless! It could not be possible that they would accept me back...» [K1/Grove]
- «The Council may forgive a lot... But not this. I am lost! Even though I would repent, there is nowhere left for me to go! Leave me to my hopelessness!» [K1/Grove]
- «I must give you my thanks. Because of you, I am once again welcome within the Jedi Order.» [K1/Grove]
- «I Have spoken to the Council, and they have helped me see the truth. The truth about myself, and the truth of my actions.» [K1/Grove]

- «Quatra's methods may seem strange, but I trust her wisdom. The lesson has been difficult, but I am better because of it. Humility is never easy to teach, but now I truly understand my own limitations.» [K1/Grove]
- «After our last battle, Quatra had nothing left to teach me. I needed time alone to explore the turmoil of my own spirit. Only then was I ready to follow a guide – you – back to the light. When I left, Quatra knew her work with me was done. There are other disciples who need training throughout the galaxy, and she could not stay to see if I passed this most difficult trial.» [K1/Grove]
- «With your help, I have passed this difficult trial. The Council now feels I am ready to continue with my training, though they have asked me to wait here for the time being.» [K1/Grove]
