**[K1/Hangar]**
- «How... how can you possibly be Revan?» [K1/Hangar]
- «The one... the Jedi whose troops saved me on Taris... It was Revan, leading an army out to defeat the Mandalorians. It was you...» [K1/Hangar]
- «You are nothing like the one who saved me on Taris.» [K1/Hangar]
- «You are obviously not who I thought you were, once upon a time. I thought you were the ideal Jedi. I thought you would never turn against us. Yet here you are.» [K1/Hangar]
- «You did turn in the end. I did not want to believe it. But now I suppose you have a chance to redeem yourself. You can work with us. For the Republic. For the Order. We can set right what you started so long ago.» [K1/Hangar]
- «Yes we shall. I only hope that you will see this through until the end.» [K1/Hangar]
- «And I hope that what you see as necessary is what we originally set out to do.» [K1/Hangar]
- «Only through serving the Republic do you serve yourself as well. In defeating Malak you save all of us along with you.» [K1/Hangar]
- «Time will tell what choices you make. I shall stay by you to see this task done.» [K1/Hangar]
- «I knew when I heard that you had become the Dark Lord of the Sith that something was wrong with it.» [K1/Hangar]
- «The man who saved me could never go completely to the dark side.» [K1/Hangar]
- «[[../Juhani» [K1/Hangar]
- «The woman who saved me, who I... who I have come to admire, could never go completely to the dark side.» [K1/Hangar]
- «It is good to know that you have truly changed. It gives me hope. I do not think now that I will fall to the dark side. I only have to look to you and follow by your example and there is no way I can fail.» [K1/Hangar]
- «Yes. That is true. I suppose you would know better than I.» [K1/Hangar]
- «''Thank you for placing your confidence in me. I only wish I had your heart as well...''» [K1/Hangar]
- «''That is especially meaningful coming from you.''» [K1/Hangar]
- «I... I am sorry, I did not realize. I will endeavor to be more considerate in the future.» [K1/Hangar]
- «I will never forget what you did for me, even if you do not remember it.» [K1/Hangar]
- «What is it you would like to speak to me about?» [K1/Hangar] *(+2 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I have been thinking much about our journey, and I am grateful and honored that you have accepted me with you. It is an honor to be by your side and to see you redeemed from the dark path you had chosen before. We Cathar do not make friends easily, or for long. Even on Dantooine, I remained alone. Not ostracized exactly, but... separate.» [K1/Hangar]
- «I have been doing well, I suppose. As well as I can. But I am more interested in you right now. Do not mistake me, it is just that even though we have traveled together for a while, it seems I know next to nothing about you, personally.» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Yes. Yes, I do. I more than most perhaps. But I do not know the stories of your past. Legends rarely are laid bare like that. We have been travelling together since Dantooine, but I know nothing of you before that. Would you... would you humor me and tell me a little about yourself?» [K1/Hangar]
- «Hah. Humility. Please, if you would, indulge me.» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Your humility is... astounding as always. So what can you tell me?» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Your job? Your childhood? Your life?» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Your... family?» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I am sorry if I am making you uncomfortable. I will fully understand if you choose not to answer.» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I greatly appreciate your taking the time to talk to me. How did you come to be on Dantooine? Besides the obvious, of course.» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Heh. Yes, I kind of figured that. From where? Oh wait... I remember...» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Taris... It always seems to come back to Taris for me... I am sorry... I get distracted. I already know the tales of your exploits: how you defeated the Mandalorians in the war... and how you fell. But now you have the chance to redeem yourself. To make a new page in your history. So now, I suppose, that all does not matter. Only your life now does. But again I seem to be wasting your time with my carrying on. I apologize.» [K1/Hangar]

**[K1/Juhani]**
- «[Juhani seems tense and wary in your presence. Perhaps you could make her feel more welcome by talking with her.]» [K1/Juhani]
- «[You decide to ignore Juhani and continue on with your quest for now, though you might want to speak with her later.]» [K1/Juhani]
- «I feel I must apologize for the way I acted towards you before, in the grove. It was wrong of me.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I realize you have justification, but that anger only leads to the dark side. Please do not fall into the same trap that caught me.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I am sorry for attacking you. I am sorry for thinking you would only try to kill me. I hope that by helping you in your task I may redeem myself in your eyes... and in my own.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I thought that too. But I was wrong...» [K1/Juhani]
- «The dark side is most devious and deceptive. It can find the smallest weakness to exploit. I hope that by helping you in your task I may redeem myself in your eyes... and in my own.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Thank you... It is most reassuring to know that you can forgive me, even though I tried to take your life.» [K1/Juhani]
- «It may. But I am prepared to do what it takes.» [K1/Juhani]
- «You mock me! I will do what it takes to redeem myself in the eyes of my peers. I WILL succeed! I will prove myself to you and to everyone! I will be a faithful companion to you and Bastila as long as our journey may last.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I can only hope that, in our time journeying together, I will succeed.» [K1/Juhani]
- «How I came to be a Jedi? I am sure you would not find it very interesting... Are you sure you would like to hear?» [K1/Juhani]
- «Very well then. Perhaps some other time you might deign to be interested.» [K1/Juhani]
- «My attempt at humility seems to fail around you. So be it then, go immerse yourself in something else.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Well... It goes back a number of years... Back on my homeworld we did not see Jedi very often, especially where I lived.» [K1/Juhani]
- «It was not the homeworld of the Cathar that I lived on. My parents had long fled from that place, and perhaps that is a story for another time. Rather it was a human hive-world I was raised on.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Bad enough that Jedi rarely came to that place.» [K1/Juhani]
- «The hind end of space. A pit of a world, to be sure. Where Jedi rarely tread...» [K1/Juhani]
- «But we had heard of them. Well, everyone had, so that is not to be unexpected. Champions of truth. Defenders of justice. Heroes of the Republic. It was very easy for a child to be enthralled by their image, their mystique. Maybe I was one of those children.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Yes... Yes I did.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Allow children their innocence while they still may have it! I was not so innocent, but I too was affected...» [K1/Juhani]
- «When I saw a Jedi for the first time they lived up to everything my imagination had created them to be. I was awed... and maybe a bit enamored...» [K1/Juhani]
- «They were quite striking... especially the tales of their leader.» [K1/Juhani]
- «From that moment on I knew that I would have to try to become a Jedi. To lift myself out of the rut I had been living in for years and to make a real difference, as the Jedi were. The foolish delusions of a child. But THIS child made it happen! As soon as I was able I left my world and went in search of them. I found them and was accepted I had been living my dream on Dantooine for several years before you came. Although... perhaps I was not entirely ready for it... or not completely suited to the task. Otherwise I would not have fallen... But thanks to you I have been redeemed. Perhaps I may yet live to see that dream of mine come true. Come, there is much we should do. Let us not waste time talking. Action is what is needed.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I... I thank you for your concern, but I am still a bit shaken.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I am. I try to be strong, as one of my people should, but I know I am still weak inside.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I have been thinking about myself... about Quatra... and about my fall to the dark side. I keep thinking that it was my anger that drove me that far, that nearly damned me. I look inside myself now and I can still see it, I still feel it.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I think maybe, that I just need more time.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Would you rather I fell again and attacked? Slaughtered you in your sleep like an animal? I see in your pride you do not think that possible. Very well, then! Keep your own counsel.» [K1/Juhani]
- «More time would do me good. Time to distance myself from that anger. I think that is why the Council agreed to send me with you. They think, perhaps, that in your company I will be able to free myself from it.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I do not come here begging for scraps from you! I will earn my way with you. You will see just how valuable I am!» [K1/Juhani]
- «I thank you for your concern and your acceptance. I will strive to prove that I am worthy of your company and trust.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Well... I suppose I have not talked very much about the Jedi I met back home. They, all of them, were so very... invigorating.» [K1/Juhani]
- «If you do not wish to hear what I have to say, then find some other being to converse with!» [K1/Juhani]
- «What?! You... Bah! It is useless talking to you it seems! Come back when you are more civilized or when we have something to do.» [K1/Juhani]
- «They were so very alive. So full of hope and energy and zeal. In retrospect I can see it was a little bit tragic...» [K1/Juhani]
- «Well... yes...  these Jedi were going to fight the Mandalorians just after they had invaded.» [K1/Juhani]
- «These Jedi were going to fight the Mandalorians just after they had invaded.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Many of those Jedi perished in the fighting. But to us, they seemed invincible, especially their leader, who they talked about all the time. Paragons of light and justice, sweeping away all iniquity before them. It was like looking at gods...» [K1/Juhani]
- «I know that! I was using poetic licence! But those Jedi...» [K1/Juhani]
- «I can only assume that was some misguided attempt at humor. I was using poetic licence. But those Jedi...» [K1/Juhani]
- «I was merely using poetic licence. Those Jedi...» [K1/Juhani]
- «They were enthralling... Everyone wanted just to touch them. Some people thought it would bring them luck. Not that the peace they brought lasted very long...» [K1/Juhani]
- «Of course they left! They had a war to go fight, so that we would not all be killed by those psychotic Mandalorians!» [K1/Juhani]
- «The Jedi left. The people grew complacent. Those who had been wronged saw their chance at revenge... and so the cycle continues. The oppressed become the new generation of oppressors. The human oppressed that is... The non-humans were never treated well in any case... We felt the brunt of both administrations...» [K1/Juhani]
- «Of course it was!» [K1/Juhani]
- «What?!? Of course I can!» [K1/Juhani]
- «Yes but humans seem the most pervasive... the most consistent.» [K1/Juhani]
- «They took their frustrations and hates out on us because the people they wanted had already fled or were too well protected! But no one looks out for the injustices we suffered! Oh, no! But... I am sorry, I should not have outbursts like that.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Don't you see? The very fact I mentioned it means it has its influence. Anger can lead to the dark side and I must be ever careful that I do not fall back into those ways.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I know... And I must be ever careful that I do not fall back into those ways again.» [K1/Juhani]
- «No, it is not. Anger can lead to the dark side and I must be ever careful that I do not fall back into those ways.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I... I thank you for your support. My outburst was uncalled for, but you did not lash back at me. You are a much better Jedi than I, it would seem. But let us not speak more of this now. We should continue on our journey. Perhaps later we will talk again.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I have been thinking much about our journey, and I am grateful and honored that you have accepted me with you.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I am not used to such unbiased acceptance from one who is nearly a stranger to me. We Cathar do not make friends easily, or for long. Even on Dantooine, I remained alone. Not ostracized exactly, but... separate.» [K1/Juhani]
- «In truth, I have lived in the Republic too long, and know little. I never met any others of my race other than my parents. But we are not diplomats. We do not deal well with groups. This situation is new to me.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Yes, it was. I find it difficult to speak of those things. I do not want to live that way ever again. And here it seems different...» [K1/Juhani]
- «Well, even if we do not speak that often, or are apart, I still feel a part of a team; this group you have assembled that accompanies you and Bastila on your quest.» [K1/Juhani]
- «It is... warming. I feel almost welcome. Needed. You seem to be very decisive when it is required of you. It is reassuring to know that I can count on someone like you.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Wha...» [K1/Juhani]
- «What? But... I thought...» [K1/Juhani]
- «It seems I was mistaken... I should have known things could not change for me that easily. As it is the Council's wishes, I will accompany you. I will do my duty, and trust that you will do yours.» [K1/Juhani]
- «To be accepted so readily... I find it difficult to explain. It is so different from what I am used to.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Yes... and that is my point. I find it difficult to explain. It is so different from what I am used to.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I feel that I could count on any of you. But you... you are the one who drew me back from the dark side. It is to you that I feel a debt.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Just... Thank you for accepting me.» [K1/Juhani]
- «And I SHALL make it up to you.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Yes? What is it?» [K1/Juhani]
- «I... have been bothered by something of late...» [K1/Juhani]
- «I never told you where I came from, did I? Where I grew up as a child? I suppose I was trying to deny what I was feeling.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Fine! Then I will not! What does it matter to you if my homeworld was wiped out by your actions! Who cares if it is because of you that everyone I knew as a child is dead!» [K1/Juhani]
- «Someone else is not responsible for wiping out my homeworld by their actions. But who cares if it is because of you that everyone I have ever known off Dantooine is dead!» [K1/Juhani]
- «I have been wrestling with my feelings inside. Trying to come to terms with it, but I find I cannot. I must have someone to blame! Someone to blame for the destruction of my homeworld. Someone who is responsible for the death of everyone I have ever known except those on Dantooine!» [K1/Juhani]
- «You self centered fool! To think that I would desire to mate with one such as you, should that even be possible! You are the center of the universe, what does it matter if my homeworld was wiped out by your actions! Who cares if it is because of you that everyone I knew as a child is dead!» [K1/Juhani]
- «Taris! It was Taris that the Sith destroyed to try to kill you and your precious Bastila! Taris, my homeworld!» [K1/Juhani]
- «Oh yes, I have been told all about her vaunted powers with the Force. Her Battle Meditation that can turn the tide of any conflict. Well if it could, then why could Taris not have been saved?» [K1/Juhani]
- «The sacrifice of an entire world is too much for any cause! How would you feel if Dantooine or Alderaan or some such world were destroyed? Would you be so callous then?» [K1/Juhani]
- «Not that you care!» [K1/Juhani]
- «You... you animal! It is only because of the Council's will that I stay with you. But if you betray us, I may decide that falling to the dark side will be a small price to pay for letting my anger run loose.» [K1/Juhani]
- «It was my home!» [K1/Juhani]
- «What did you think would happen by aiding the Republic?» [K1/Juhani]
- «If it were not for you and Bastila, the Sith would have never had reason to destroy that world! It was your fault for being there, and your fault for rescuing Bastila! Without your intervention the Sith would have had no cause to lay waste to my childhood!» [K1/Juhani]
- «Just let me vent my anger! I need someone to blame... something... anything! I hated that world, yet everything I learned as a child I learned there. It is as much a part of me as the air I breathe. I have this ache inside me where all my childhood memories lay, and I find your face there with them. If it was not for you, that world would still exist!» [K1/Juhani]
- «I... I know... I suppose I realized that.» [K1/Juhani]
- «There is no emotion. There is peace. ... I suppose you did what you had to... and it could not have been avoided. The Republic needs you and Bastila. Maybe needs you more than it needed Taris.» [K1/Juhani]
- «But it is so hard to lose your entire past. You would not understand.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Yes... Yes I suppose it will. As long as we remain focused on our task it will.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I suppose that is to be expected. Everything will turn out for the best as long as we remain focused on our task.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I am sorry. I will try not to distract you in the future with my trivial experiences. We should stop for now.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Taris was my home for many years. As much as I hated it, it was still home. A rat-hole; a warren; a paradise. I suppose you could call it all these things. For me it was hell...» [K1/Juhani]
- «You have no idea what it is like to have to live there for years...» [K1/Juhani]
- «But it was precisely the people who made it so bad!» [K1/Juhani]
- «To walk down the streets. To have people look at you in disgust; like an animal. Most did not know anything about me, but it was there all the same. Because I was an 'alien'. Because I was different from them; frightening. I doubt any of them had even seen my people before. So they persecuted me... They looked down on me and my family. Charged us more for food. Would not let us walk their streets. They spat on us. You are human... you could not possibly know.» [K1/Juhani]
- «What? You.. You are just like them!» [K1/Juhani]
- «That is true I suppose. But it much more obvious for someone like me.» [K1/Juhani]
- «How can you say that? You have seen Taris, you know what kind of a scum infested hole it is. For us there was no respite. No one to turn to.» [K1/Juhani]
- «There were no other Cathar on Taris. No one to talk to. No one to confide in. Humph! But you must think me weak for saying this. But I am strong enough to admit it!» [K1/Juhani]
- «I thank you for your concern, but...» [K1/Juhani]
- «I... I do not know. I have been keeping it inside for so long. I thought you might understand. You do not seem to fit in exactly with the others. You are... different.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Bah! Maybe I am weak. To think that some stories of my stupid past could possibly interest you.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I thank you. You are kinder than I would have expected... To put up with me using you in this way. I hope that I will not use your time so badly in the future. Thank you.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Maybe there is no point. Maybe I am just lost after leaving yet another planet. I apologize for my outburst. I will try to control myself next time.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I was remembering Taris...» [K1/Juhani]
- «You mock my pain! It was a horrible place to live... we had to go through hell every day just to survive, and you think it bothering me is an irritation?! Your insensitivity speaks ill of your qualities as a Jedi. Still, as the Council ordered me to do, I shall accompany you, but expect no great sympathy for your plights.» [K1/Juhani]
- «No, it is alright. I think I am over the worst of it. I apologize again for lashing out at you. It was not your fault.» [K1/Juhani]
- «It was a horrible place to have to live. At least in the lower cities where the non-humans tended to get relegated. Living for years in a place with no sun, living off the trash dropped from the upper levels, and the meager pay doing back-breaking labor.» [K1/Juhani]
- «There were none. Bad enough to be a race that is feared and hated by humans, but to be one that is strange and different even among non-humans? But living was hard enough without the stigma of being from a lost race.» [K1/Juhani]
- «There was always the danger of rakghouls coming up from the sewers, or more mundane predators living and working in the area.» [K1/Juhani]
- «My family and I struggled each and every day to make something of our lives, but we could only go so far. Taxes from the corrupt government, more fees from the gangs controlling the streets, and whatever was left paying for what food and medical supplies we could afford.» [K1/Juhani]
- «It did not. But only because of the Jedi who came. It was very bad.» [K1/Juhani]
- «With no money to spare for any amenities, even the Enclave on Dantooine seems like a paradise in comparison.» [K1/Juhani]
- «And of course there was the constant bigotry and hate from the more affluent and human citizens. Lording their wealth over us living below. Every once in a while a rich human would come down through the lower levels with his droid entourage just to see how the ‘wildlife' lived... and laughed at the mockeries that were our successes. But I have come to meet many decent humans in my travels since those days. Indeed, some of the greatest people I have ever met are human.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I have noticed that. But the Jedi order seems to have collected a fair number.» [K1/Juhani]
- «The... the Jedi who encouraged me to join the Order, the one who was with the group going to fight the Mandalorians, she was human.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I... I suppose I see something of her in you when I look...» [K1/Juhani]
- «I am sorry... I am getting away from my point. If there even was one... Sometimes I curse the day my parents fled to Taris. But then again, if they had not, I would not be where I am today.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Another story for another time. For now we must continue our own epic: To save the galaxy, if we can.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Oh. I see. It is because you wish to keep me at arm's length. Are you worried about me turning to the dark side? Or do you merely not wish my company. Whatever the case, I am sorry for presuming too much. I will remember my station from now on.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Please, forgive me... I did not mean to insinuate that I... that we...» [K1/Juhani]
- «It did not come out the way I had intended.» [K1/Juhani]
- «We have been travelling together since Dantooine, but I know nothing of you before that. Would you... would you humor me and tell me a little about yourself?» [K1/Juhani]
- «I understand. I appreciate that you take the time to talk to me. I will be more considerate of your time in the future.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Taris... It always seems to come back to Taris for me... I am sorry... I get distracted. What did you do before Ta... before that?» [K1/Juhani]
- «A life in the army. Not my career of choice, though. We Cathar cannot seem to adjust to regimented organizations like the military.» [K1/Juhani]
- «But in a way it does seem to have its own romantic appeal. Defending the galaxy from evil. Much as we Jedi do. So in a way, I suppose, we are more alike than not.» [K1/Juhani]
- «But I have heard that it appeals to males of your species. Fighting, defending. That is what we as Jedi seem to be called upon to do. Perhaps we are not as unalike as I had previously thought...» [K1/Juhani]
- «But again I seem to be wasting your time with my carrying on. I apologize.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Well, I mentioned before that my parents had fled to Taris. Perhaps I can tell you about that.» [K1/Juhani]
- «No there were not... having fled the birthworld of my species, my parents flew as far and wide as they could. And so I was raised alone, knowing of my birthplace only the few stories my parents could tell, and seeing no other member of my species at all...» [K1/Juhani]
- «It all comes back to wars and fighting... the curse of your race, it seems...» [K1/Juhani]
- «In the early days of the Mandalorian war, there had been fighting closer to the Outer Rim worlds.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Cathar was there, yes. My people had a great reputation as warriors... and that appealed to the Mandalore version of ‘honor'. They sought to test themselves against us, I think... test themselves by bombing our world, slaughtering my people while they slept or while they ran.» [K1/Juhani]
- «They swooped down from space across the world, firing at anything that moved. They used ships in space to destroy all orbital facilities and bombard the surface. We did resist, and in spite of their violent attack we did stave them off for quite a while. But in the end we were doomed...» [K1/Juhani]
- «We were prepared! Just not for an invasion of that size... And we had counted on the aid of our allies. Of the Republic...» [K1/Juhani]
- «We were not members of the Republic. Cathar was beyond the edge of the Republic, in the Outer Rim. And besides, they could not have known. Our interstellar communications were the first things the Mandalores hit. All other short-range communicators were jammed... we were on our own. We knew what was coming. We had fought the Mandalorians in the first war against Exar Kun and the Sith. We knew there would be no mercy for us. The most we could do was pack the few of our people who survived onto what few ships remained and send them off into space as fast as they could. Most did not make it.» [K1/Juhani]
- «My parents carried me as a baby with them and were lucky enough to escape. They fled as far as they were able, and eventually settled on Taris. They could stand running no further, I think... But Taris was a horrible choice. Dominated by humans, intolerant of other species, it made everyday life unnecessarily hard.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Perhaps they were not. Perhaps they did not know better. It did not take long for things to get worse.» [K1/Juhani]
- «No, it is not. But they had little choice. Each of us dealt with it in our own way.» [K1/Juhani]
- «My father... my father turned to stimulants. He spent much of his time in local bars and dives. But we are warriors. It runs through our blood. And when he was on stims he... he... he became foolish... he let his warrior nature get the best of him. So he would get intoxicated, and he would fight, and finally one day he would die.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I don't need your sarcasm, or your pity! He died at the hands of another.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Killed by a man who provoked him into a fight and killed him like an animal. I... I am sorry, I cannot talk about this any longer right now...» [K1/Juhani]
- «I... I have been watching you for some time. I wonder if you have noticed. I have been thinking of how far I had fallen, and how you exemplify the Code of the Jedi. I find myself ashamed.» [K1/Juhani]
- «But I have so little to show when I compare myself to you... and that makes my shame even worse...» [K1/Juhani]
- «Your power in the Force is strong, and I can sense no taint of the dark side in you. That makes my shame even worse...» [K1/Juhani]
- «That I had so easily fallen to the dark side. That I could convince myself that by injuring my master I became a great force of evil in the galaxy. For my pride, my hubris. For the inner rage that all of my species feels. Sometimes I wish that I had not been born this way...» [K1/Juhani]
- «I am sorry. I start to talk thinking about how you stay strong to the path of virtue and justice, and I find myself whining again about my own inadequacies. The Council would be proud to see that you had learned your lessons so well.» [K1/Juhani]
- «... As am I.» [K1/Juhani] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I have been thinking about our journey together, and how we are more similar than I had first thought.» [K1/Juhani]
- «But similar enough. We have had many of the same sort of experiences.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Heh. I did not mean in appearances, but we have had some of the same experiences.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I know how hard it was for me to resist the call of the dark side, and I know that you have suffered those pains as well. The continual struggle between restraint and freedom... light and darkness. It is hard for me with my Cathar blood to resist sometimes.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I do resist. I look to you and see that we are in this together, and that you are resisting as well. That gives me strength. I think the Jedi Council would be proud to see that you are holding up so well in spite of the hardships you have endured.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I have been watching you... and I cannot help but feel that you are becoming more distant. When we met it was you that convinced me to turn away from the lure of the dark side. But now it seems that you are falling into the trap that had caught me.» [K1/Juhani]
- «No. Please. I must speak. When we first met I looked at you with contempt. You were some insect sent by the Jedi Council to eliminate me. But you were more powerful than I would ever have thought. It seemed so easy for you to overcome me.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I think that you flatter me again, and I do not know why. I worry for you.» [K1/Juhani]
- «That you did. You took away the dark taint that had been consuming me and restored me to the light. You took me away from there. But now... now I wonder what it is that you see in your own reflection.» [K1/Juhani]
- «And this is just the problem! You seem to revel in your power, use it far too frequently for your own gain.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I look and I see you turning away from the Code of the Jedi. I wonder if you can see it.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I... I do not know... I fear that you may be starting to fall.» [K1/Juhani]
- «If only there were some way I could turn you away from the path that you seem to be headed down...» [K1/Juhani]
- «I wonder if it started when you met me in that grove. I wonder if it could be my fault.» [K1/Juhani]
- «That is true. But still I worry about the ones you make.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I wish I could believe that as you do.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Maybe I do. I have had reason to wonder recently if I have had any effect at all...» [K1/Juhani]
- «But I have faith in you. I would follow you into battle with Malak himself. I will stand by your side.» [K1/Juhani]
- «After my father died, my mother was left to support me alone. A single non-human woman, living in one of the worst, most violent slums on Taris. It was not easy for her.» [K1/Juhani]
- «When my father was killed it seemed that something in her began to die as well. She worked. She worked as hard as she was able, but over time she began to waste away. I later learned that she was unable to get enough money to feed both of us, and had to start borrowing from the Exchange, a band of cutthroats and smugglers. But even that was not enough... She hid what she was doing from me. She gave most of her food to me, so that I would be strong. But she herself was suffering deeply from it. In the end she could go on no longer, and collapsed at the cantina where she worked one day.» [K1/Juhani]
- «She never recovered. There was nothing really that I could do for her... She left me no money, and no doctor would see her without being paid first. I sat by her bed for days as she lay there dying. I never want that to happen to someone I care about ever again... But there was still the money she had borrowed... She had never paid it back, and made no provisions for her death. You must understand: the Exchange is brutal. They care nothing for the life of a sentient being. They are the major suppliers of slaves on Taris.» [K1/Juhani]
- «They thought it was appropriate that, with my mother owing them money, the debt should be passed on to me. And with no way to pay, they took me by force. You have no idea what it is like to be bound like a beast and treated as such. It was the worst time of my life. They treated me like livestock. They were waiting for a buyer to give them some credits for me when the Jedi came to fight the Mandalorians.» [K1/Juhani]
- «The Jedi could not abide by what they saw there, and drove the Exchange from the face of the world, freeing those of us imprisoned there. But the Jedi soon left to fight their war. And I was left with a dream...» [K1/Juhani]
- «I swore that I would become a Jedi. As soon as I had enough money to do it, I bought passage on a freighter headed for Dantooine. And we both know what has happened since then.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I am grateful to you for having given me the opportunity to fulfill my dream, rather than become what I hated. Someday I may make it up to you, but for now, let us keep on the task at hand.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I have told you my life story already, what more could you possibly want to know? Well... there is one thing... I did not want to mention it before, because I was unsure how you would react.» [K1/Juhani]
- «If you do not want to listen, I understand. But hear me out first.» [K1/Juhani]
- «You... you will see. Perhaps...» [K1/Juhani]
- «Do you remember when I told you of the Jedi who had come to my world to fight the Mandalorians? They were recruiting soldiers for their crusade against the Mandalores. Many people joined them.» [K1/Juhani]
- «The main reason for that was their charismatic leader. The one whose troops freed me and let me dream of seeking out and joining the Jedi Order. I was hesitant to talk to you about this because... well...» [K1/Juhani]
- «The Jedi had come to fight the Mandalores under the leadership of a powerful young Jedi... a Jedi named Revan.» [K1/Juhani]
- «THE Revan. The Jedi who turned to the dark side, became the Dark Lord of the Sith and was defeated by Bastila and her strike team. I do not know what happened in the wars with the Mandalorians that turned Revan and Malak, or what they sought with these Star Maps, but the Jedi I knew was a paragon of the Order. I am sorry, if this seems wrong to you, but to understand you must realize that Revan was as great as a Jedi could be. Whatever turned Revan must be terrible indeed. I wonder if what we find at the end of our journey will turn us as well...» [K1/Juhani]
- «This is meaningless speculation I guess. I just wanted you to know what I held from you. But rest assured that I will not hold back if our enemy is Malak, Revan's apprentice, or indeed even if it was Revan. I will help you through all trouble. Such is the will of the Council... And my own.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I have as much right to be here as you do, SIR!» [K1/Juhani]
- «Wha... what do you know about my world?» [K1/Juhani]
- «What? You...» [K1/Juhani]
- «What are you talking about??» [K1/Juhani]
- «I am not for sale!» [K1/Juhani]
- «What?! WHAT DID YOU SAY?!» [K1/Juhani]
- «WHAT DID YOU DO ON TARIS, YOU SCUM?!» [K1/Juhani]
- «It was you!» [K1/Juhani]
- «You... My homeworld...» [K1/Juhani]
- «NO, I WILL NOT BE SOLD!» [K1/Juhani]
- «And I will see you dead for what you have done to my people!» [K1/Juhani]
- «...There is peace.» [K1/Juhani] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Yes, we... NO! I must not fall prey to the dark side again.» [K1/Juhani]
- «I... I will remain calm. I am a Jedi now.» [K1/Juhani]
- «My lust for vengeance must be curbed.» [K1/Juhani]
- «My Cathar blood seethes at the thought of that man still running free. I cannot stand still while I think about it, but... but I will not give in to the dark side either. He will pay for his crimes, though. He will most likely be following us... me. If we could find him first, then he may not be able to set in motion whatever he has in store for us.» [K1/Juhani]
- «What do you mean?» [K1/Juhani]
- «He may not, but I will make him pay with what little time he has left! I will make him tell me what he has done to my people!» [K1/Juhani]
- «You... you animal! You wretch!» [K1/Juhani]
- «I will not give him the satisfaction of seeing me give in to the dark side.» [K1/Juhani]
- «No! I cannot give in to my anger. I will not give him the satisfaction of making me that way.» [K1/Juhani]
- «No, I will not give in to him.» [K1/Juhani]
- «You... You killed my father, you killed my people, you treat us like animals... You deserve to die!» [K1/Juhani]
- «I... I...» [K1/Juhani]
- «I... Help me, please! Do not let me give in to my anger again!» [K1/Juhani]
- «No, I will not do this. He may die, but it will not be out of my vengeance.» [K1/Juhani]
- «That man was a beast! A monster! I am glad that the galaxy is now rid of him, so that he may not harm others again. I do regret, though, that he did not die by my hand...» [K1/Juhani]
- «I know. But must I always fear the lure of the dark side?» [K1/Juhani]
- «I... I do not know anymore what I am strong enough for. I only take what comes to me and hope that I can handle it. The lure of the dark side will always be my strongest foe. I should have killed him.» [K1/Juhani]
- «But... it was for the best. I am glad that you stood by my side during this. We should continue our journeys now. No reason for staying here for this.» [K1/Juhani]
- «You... You will die for what you have done!» [K1/Juhani]
- «I felt statisfaction taking revenge for my people like that... but... but I should not have let my anger consume me so.» [K1/Juhani]
- «But I could have! I should have... I must try harder in the future to resist the call of the dark side. I suppose I am fortunate that, in this case, he was deserving of such an end.» [K1/Juhani]
- «Yes... Yes I do. I must try harder in the future to resist such urges. I suppose I am fortunate that, in this case, he was deserving of such an end.» [K1/Juhani]
- «No I didn't. I let my anger and passion consume me and did something rash. Something which leads me down the path to the dark side. But... I recognize this, and I am truly regretful that I committed this act. I shall not let it happen again. I suppose I was fortunate that, in this case, he was deserving of such an end.» [K1/Juhani]
- «We should continue on our way. I do not want to linger around here anymore.» [K1/Juhani]

**[K1/Leviathan]**
- «You speak the truth. I alone of all the group might be able to remain hidden if the Sith search the ship. I could wait until the guards have completed their search, then come to rescue you.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «I think you may be underestimating my abilities, but I will bow to your decision.» [K1/Leviathan]

**[K1/Lower_Shadowlands]**
- «Wait - maybe this doesn't have to end in bloodshed. Maybe we should try talking to this Wookiee.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]

**[K1/North_Beach]**
- «You understand these aliens? Of course... you must have come here and learned their languge in your search for the secrets of the Star Forge. Back when you were still Revan.» [K1/North_Beach]

**[K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]**
- «You must not give in to your baser desires. The lure of the dark side is strong.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- «For once I will have to agree with the Mandalorian...» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- «We should inform the Jedi Council of this crime.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- «This doesn't have to end in violence! Don't give in to your bloodlust!» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud] *(+2 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «*whisper* It is obvious Master Vandar senses something is amiss in your tale. Yet he chooses to believe you, and I will not question the wisdom of a Jedi Master.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]

**[K1/Sandral_Grounds]**
- «Do not do this...» [K1/Sandral_Grounds]

**[K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]**
- «You should not toy with people's lives!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «That was the right thing to do - turn him away from the Sith.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «You should not manipulate people this way! You may fall!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «You should not do this. Taking advantage, even of a Sith, leads to the dark side.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I... yes -- we are only servants to the... master.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]

**[K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]**
- «*whisper* Why are helping to propagate the Sith lies on this world? If this is some kind of attempt at reverse psychology, I do not understand its purpose.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]

**[K1/Star_Forge_System]**
- «During our rather rapid descent I noticed the hulls of many crashed ships scattered across the landscape. Perhaps the parts you need can be found among their wreckage.» [K1/Star_Forge_System]
- «I only hope we're not too late. Bastila has been Malak's prisoner for a long time. If he can turn her to the dark side she will join him and the Sith will be invincible.» [K1/Star_Forge_System]

**[K1/Swoop_Registration]**
- «You should not force someone to do what they don't want.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- «This is hardly becoming for a Jedi.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- «A Jedi should not use the Force for personal gain. We are better than that.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]

**[K1/Temple_Exterior]**
- «We have had a... a premonition. The Force has given us a vision. There is great danger within the Temple. We cannot let you face it alone.» [K1/Temple_Exterior]
- «Jolee speaks for both of us. You must enter the Temple, but we must go with you.» [K1/Temple_Exterior]

**[K1/Temple_Summit]**
- «Quickly, Bastila - come with us! We have to escape before Malak arrives!» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «Bastila, it is not too late for you to be saved. The teachings of the Jedi can lead you from the dark side back into the light and a true understanding of the Force.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «A true Jedi will never bow down to the Sith. If this is your decision I have no choice but to do battle against you.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «No! This is the way of the dark side! Do not give in to the anger and hatred!» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «This may be the last time I will have a chance to talk to you...» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «I just want you to know that I... that I...» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «Of.. Of course. I am sorry. I should have known better. I can only say that if I am going to die, as we stand likely to, I am glad that it will be you beside whom I do it.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «But I feel I must. I have never said this before...» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «I... I... I care for you. I do not know why. I do not know if anything will be possible or if you even return what I feel, but I do know it is there. I am sorry if this upsets you. I am so sorry if I am wrong, but I cannot deny what it is that I feel.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «I... I understand. You have someone somewhere... or I just do not seem to appeal to you.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «I was afraid that I would have no more time to say it... I wonder if all of us will survive what we must do...» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «I am sorry for pushing myself on you. I apologize for my actions. I suggest we find and confront Malak as soon as possible to remove the threat to the Republic once and for all.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «Now that I have put myself in this position, I know not what to say.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «Yes. I thank you. But as much as I may care for you, and you for me, first we must deal with Malak. After there will be time to sort things out between us.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «I just want to thank you for all that you have done for me. For saving me from the dark side, for accepting me on your journey.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «It is good to know that I have been of some value...» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «I will help you in any way I can.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «Even if not as much as I would like...» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «I will prove my true worth to you.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «We WILL defeat Malak and save the Republic from the Sith threat once and for all!» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «I... defer to your wisdom in this matter. I have little knowledge of the true power of the Sith.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «I pledge my life to your cause. I will stand by you until the end.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «She is not dead, Carth - though maybe that might have been easier for you to accept. She has fallen to the dark side and fled to the Star Forge.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «I don't know what fate awaits us, but I sense Bastila still has a role to play in the events to come. I have no doubt she will be waiting for us on the Star Forge.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «Bastila tried to tempt her to the dark side, to reclaim Revan's heritage. She failed.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- «Confidence is all well and good, Carth, but if we are going to catch up to Bastila we should leave soon, don't you think?» [K1/Temple_Summit]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]**
- «Beware this... this spirit. It may not be what it appears. Great emotion lies within...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]**
- «You can always turn back to the light.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]

**[K1/Upper_Shadowlands]**
- «I do not feel the taint of the dark side within you, old man. I sense you are a servant of the light, whether you will admit it or not.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]

**[K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- «Dak? That is you, isn't it? I... hardly recognized you.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «What are you doing here? Did... did you fall to the dark side?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «I was. I'm not anymore. Dak... you could...» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Running away? Again?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «I don't think there's much to talk about.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Let him go. He... won't say anything.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Goodbye, Dak... and... thank you.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «There is little to tell. Dak and I were both Padawans... we joined the Order at the same time. He was a kind man, but... frustrated. He felt the Jedi were too controlling, too limiting... more so than I did. When he decided to leave the Order, he asked me to come with him. He... he said he loved me.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «No, I didn't. I enjoyed his company, as a friend, but nothing further. I had no idea he felt as he did.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «It was serious enough to him... and to me, for I did not wish to hurt him. I had little choice in the matter.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «I suppose the rest is obvious enough that I need not tell it.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «He was angry at my refusal. I... assumed that he would get over the hurt in time. I suppose such feelings can fester and darken, and they never go away.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «He reminds me of what I was when you found me in the grove. I... feel sorry for him. But he has chosen his path, hasn't he? If I thought he would accept an offer of help, I would give it. Let us... just move on, please.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Perhaps... you could go back to the Jedi. I did.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Leave him be! I said that he would not betray you. Just... let him go.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Just... go, Dak.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

**[K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]**
- «It is wrong to leave him imprisoned here. We should seek some way to free him when we have the chance.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- «Threatening her may not get you the results you want.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]

**[K1/Worrroznor's_Home]**
- «Greed will lead you down the dark side if you are not careful. You must not seek material gain at the expense of the ideals the Jedi hold dear.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]

### Quote bank B: what others say about Juhani (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K1/Grove]**
- Nemo: «I hear you managed to return Juhani to the Order. Congratulations. I regret not being able to tell you more, but some things you must see with your own eyes.» [K1/Grove]
- Belaya: «You have done a great thing. One of our own had strayed, but you have returned Juhani to the Order. For this you deserve the highest praise.» [K1/Grove]
- Vrook Lamar: «I must congratulate you on your actions. You have saved Juhani and brought her back into the Order, and have given us all great hope for your future success. May the Force be with you as you continue your training.» [K1/Grove]
- Vandar Tokare: «It is good to see Juhani has returned to the way of the light. You are to be commended for your role in this. Your actions give us great hope for the future. Go to Master Zhar and inform him that Juhani has returned to us. I think you may be nearing the end of your apprenticeship.» [K1/Grove]
- Bastila: «This has been a painful lesson, Juhani, but it seems you have learned it well. I am certain you will be a credit to the Order.» [K1/Grove]
- Nemo: «Juhani has been slain, I hear. You fought with her in the grove?» [K1/Grove]
- Nemo: «It should not have had to go that far... but some things cannot be avoided. You would do well to mind Juhani's tragic example and not to let yourself fall into the sway of the dark side.» [K1/Grove]
- Vrook Lamar: «I have heard what you have done, and how Juhani has been slain. I had hoped that this would not have to come to pass. Could you find nothing in Juhani that could be redeemed?» [K1/Grove]
- Vandar Tokare: «It is most regrettable that Juhani could not be saved. We in the Council had high hopes for her in the future. In every heart there is some means of redemption. Could you find none in Juhani?» [K1/Grove]
- Belaya: «Juhani was a... a dear companion to me for many years. We spent many nights together alone under the stars. That will never happen again, thanks to you. Curse the Council for sending you, of all people, to speak to her! Could you have done nothing else?!» [K1/Grove]
- Belaya: «Pardon me if I find your apology rather disingenuous. Juhani meant nothing to you, and the Council should have known better than to entrust her life to you.» [K1/Grove]
- Belaya: «The Juhani I knew deserved more than this! She deserved more from the Council, and more from you! You... you did not even try, did you? Did it even occur to you that Juhani might have been saved? That she might be worth saving? I should kill you for what you've done! And yet I can't, can I? I am a Jedi, and we are sworn to protect all... even the likes of you. Get... get out of my sight! The very thought of the Council... and of you... makes me ill.» [K1/Grove]
- Vandar Tokare: «We do not know. She was training here until your unfortunate incident with Juhani, then she simply disappeared... We would like any word of her you may bring. I do not believe she has died, or fallen prey to beasts, but I worry that she may have done something rash. She had always been close to Juhani, and her death may have hit Belaya... hard. Is there anything else you wish to ask?» [K1/Grove]
- Zhar Lestin: «You have done well, my pupil. The ancient grove has been purified, and Juhani's journey down the dark path has been halted. Because of you she walks once more in the light. But though she was saved, do not dismiss what happened to her. Juhani is both dedicated and true to the ideals of the Order, yet she was still vulnerable to the dark side. As are we all. She struck her master in anger during training, and injured her greatly. But it was Quatra's choice to test Juhani this way, and it seems to have made its point. Juhani has been redeemed, and you have passed your final test.» [K1/Grove]
- Zhar Lestin: «You have cleansed the grove of its dark taint, but at great cost. Juhani was a most promising student and could one day have been strong in the Force. Do not dismiss what happened to her, however. Juhani was both dedicated and true to the ideals of the Order, yet she was still vulnerable to the lure of the dark side. Keep this in mind always in the future, and do not forsake the path of the light.» [K1/Grove]

**[K1/Juhani]**
- Xor: «When I was fighting with the Mandalorians against the Cathar, I developed an appreciation for these creatures. They make excellent servants if properly trained. You Jedi act all prim and proper, but inside you must feel the same way I do about the lesser, non-human species. The Sith at least let their feelings show on the outside.» [K1/Juhani]

**[K1/Prison_Block]**
- Bastila: «Well done, Juhani. In freeing us from the cell you have proven yourself truly worthy of the title of Jedi.» [K1/Prison_Block]

**[K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]**
- Vandar Tokare: «And Juhani has also asked to accompany you. After long deliberation we have granted her request.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Vrook Lamar: «Juhani nearly fell to the dark side. Perhaps her presence will serve as a reminder to you of the dangers of that path.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]

**[K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]**
- Belaya: «I am Belaya, once of the Jedi but now of the Sith... it was your murder of Juhani that placed this bitter blackness in my heart!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Belaya: «You are a spy... and a murderer. It was you who killed Juhani and placed this bitter blackness in my heart that I cannot get rid of!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Belaya: «The Council is nothing but a slow and doddering farce, placing all their trust in you... and you failed them, just as you failed Juhani.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Belaya: «Do you even remember me? I am Belaya, and it is your murder of Juhani that brought me here. Fortunately, I have learned the power of revenge!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]

**[K1/Temple_Summit]**
- Bastila: «You are beneath my contempt, Juhani. When you felt the power of the dark side you fled to a cave like some cowering animal! You know nothing of the Force or its true potential!» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Carth: «Where are Juhani and Jolee Bindo? Are they okay? What happened inside that temple?» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Bastila: «Juhani and Jolee would not swear loyalty to the new ruler of the Sith. They died for their foolishness. Now swear loyalty to Darth Revan, or face your own death!» [K1/Temple_Summit]

**[K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- Dak Vesser: «Back again? Something... oh. Juhani... is... that you?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «It's been a long time, Juhani.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «I didn't 'fall', Juhani... I had my eyes opened. And don't look at me like that. Last I recall, you were having your own doubts.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «Have it your way, Juhani. You always do.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «Goodbye, Juhani.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «She and I were... students together at Dantooine, for a time. We were close, and we shared doubts about the Jedi's ways. I left for the Sith because I thought it would be better here. It looks like Juhani changed her mind and stayed with the Jedi after all. Your doing, maybe? Anyway... I'm done with the Jedi, the Sith... and with her. Just leave me alone.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «I don't know. I'll think about it. Just leave me alone, Juhani.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «Still sticking up for me, Juhani?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

### Quote bank B2: pronoun references resolved from context (context-resolved, hand-reviewed scene-wide (each line read by hand against the whole conversation; anchor = the line that names the character))
- [context-resolved] Belaya: «She had it coming, did she? She had it coming?!» [K1/Grove] (anchor: Belaya: Juhani was a... a dear companion to me for many year...)
- [context-resolved] Belaya: «Why? Because the Council believed that my closeness to her might do more harm than good... and yet they send *you*, instead? And look what has happened.» [K1/Grove] (anchor: Belaya: Pardon me if I find your apology rather disingenuous...)
- [context-resolved] Xor: «Ooooo! Big woman, the Jedi, shooting her mouth off from behind a lightsaber, or whatever you're carrying.» [K1/Juhani] (anchor: Just ignore him, Juhani....)
- [context-resolved] Dak Vesser: «Why don't you ask her?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords] (anchor: How do you know Juhani?...)

### Quote bank D: lines addressed to Juhani (Heuristic: lines in second person spoken directly before or after one of this character's lines on the same page)

**[K1/Ahto_West]**
- Jolee: «Be careful here, kid. Start poking your nose in places it doesn't belong and you might not like what you find.» [K1/Ahto_West]
- Bastila: «So you brought these youths to the Sith. And then what did you do to them?» [K1/Ahto_West]
- Zaalbar: «Your actions disturb me. Among my kind, those who take pleasure in the suffering of others are considered dangerously unstable.» [K1/Ahto_West] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- HK-47: «Statement: Oh, master. I'm so very disappointed in you.» [K1/Ahto_West]
- Canderous: «Mighty generous of you. A little too generous, if you ask me.» [K1/Ahto_West]

**[K1/Anchorhead]**
- Bastila: «This is beneath even you, stealing from a helpless woman. How could you?!» [K1/Anchorhead]
- Jolee: «My, my. You should be proud of yourself, outwitting her like that.» [K1/Anchorhead]
- Canderous: «The role of the weak is to serve the will of the strong; I'm glad you understand that.» [K1/Anchorhead]
- Bastila: «It troubles me to see you behaving this way. Such acts can lead to the dark side.» [K1/Anchorhead] *(+2 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Mission: «Wow, sometimes helping people feels pretty good, you know?» [K1/Anchorhead]
- Bastila: «Thank you for helping this woman. It may not seem like such a great thing to you, but you are making a difference.» [K1/Anchorhead]
- Jolee: «I know I'm just an old codger, but in my time I've learned what goes around comes around. These things have a way of coming back to you in the end.» [K1/Anchorhead]
- Bastila: «This nothing but wanton greed and cruelty! Beware, lest these things lead you to the dark side.» [K1/Anchorhead]

**[K1/Courtyard]**
- Gar: «Mandalorian beast! Some of us don't like fighting and killing and butchering as much as you!» [K1/Courtyard]
- Jon: «Thank you for exacting my revenge on those Mandalorian beasts. If only I could have been the one to tear out Sheruk's heart.» [K1/Courtyard]

**[K1/Czerka_Landing_Port]**
- Janos Wertka: «We will also arrange for translation services, for a nominal fee, of course. You'll be encountering a number of Wookiees while here.» [K1/Czerka_Landing_Port]
- Bastila: «Perhaps you should reconsider leaving Zaalbar behind. This is, after all, his homeworld.» [K1/Czerka_Landing_Port]
- Bastila: «Is this about power, then? You would rather make a decision for the sake of making it than make it wisely? I am only suggesting that both we and Zaalbar would benefit from his company while on Kashyyyk. You do want to find the Star Map, don't you?» [K1/Czerka_Landing_Port]
- Carth: «No business sense? You mean they don't like to be cheated out of all their belongings and freedom.» [K1/Czerka_Landing_Port]
- Bastila: «Feeding this man's hatred is an act of malice and petty evil. The Council would not approve of your actions here, and neither do I.» [K1/Czerka_Landing_Port]
- Bastila: «[Force Persuade] You wish to tell us more about this agreement with the Wookiee leadership.» [K1/Czerka_Landing_Port]
- Janos Wertka: «[Success] I can tell you about the agreement.» [K1/Czerka_Landing_Port]
- Janos Wertka: «It's not up to you. This planet is not a member of the Republic, and its backward citizens hold no rights. I'm sorry you are offended, but this operation is very humane. Even the Wookiee leadership knows it is more beneficial to work with us.» [K1/Czerka_Landing_Port]
- Carth: «Humane, you call it? Beneficial? Sounds like a bunch of excuses, if you ask me.» [K1/Czerka_Landing_Port]

**[K1/Droid_Shop]**
- Jolee: «*whisper* The Jedi Council wouldn't approve, but they always were a little stodgy about using the Force on people. Sometimes you gotta do what you gotta do.» [K1/Droid_Shop] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K1/East_Central]**
- Bastila: «I know you want to help Sunry, but we should not be manufacturing evidence like this.» [K1/East_Central] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Carth: «Are you sure this is a good idea? I mean, I want to help get Sunry acquitted, but what if the Selkath find out about this?» [K1/East_Central]

**[K1/Eastern_Dune_Sea]**
- Mission: «You're going to turn on this guy now? This is low, even for you.» [K1/Eastern_Dune_Sea] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Zaalbar: «You would betray this noble hunter? At times like this I regret ever swearing my life debt to one such as you.» [K1/Eastern_Dune_Sea] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Jolee: «Finding this Star Map here in this cave gives me a cold shiver. You don't think it's just coincidence that the krayt dragon made its home here, do you?» [K1/Eastern_Dune_Sea]
- HK-47: «Statement: I believe there can be little doubt that we have found the Star Map you were seeking. I am surprised it is still working after all this time.» [K1/Eastern_Dune_Sea]

**[K1/Ebon_Hawk]**
- Jolee: «Stop bothering me with your questions! I can barely hear myself think.» [K1/Ebon_Hawk]
- Jolee: «*Sigh* I have no idea what you're talking about. Rabble rabble... what are you saying.» [K1/Ebon_Hawk]

**[K1/Great_Walkway]**
- Bastila: «Put it down? You... you murdered him?» [K1/Great_Walkway]

**[K1/Grove]**
- Canderous: «You can't expect everything to get handed to you on a silver platter.» [K1/Grove]
- Mission: «Hey, I'm glad to see you're not getting hung up on all this. The past is in the past, you know?» [K1/Grove]
- Bolook: «Are you certain? It would be beneficial to your training if you were to at least make the attempt. By assisting me with this case you may learn much about how well you have learned the wisdom of the Jedi... or how much further you have yet to go.» [K1/Grove]
- Bastila: «Don't be absurd. Can't you be serious?» [K1/Grove]
- Canderous: «Well, if you can't keep her, it's your own fault.» [K1/Grove]
- Bolook: «I do not believe Rickard was lying about being in business with Calder. We have a record of that, fully registered and licenced. It seems you are having trouble separating the facts from wild guesses. Maybe I was mistaken in believing you were ready for this case.» [K1/Grove]
- Bolook: «Your personal feelings towards the man should not influence your decision in this matter. There is no emotion, there is peace. If you let your emotions rule your judgment you will be of little use in cases such as these.» [K1/Grove]

**[K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]**
- Jolee: «Now, you just close your hole, child. It's a shock, I'm sure, but this isn't about you.» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]
- Bastila: «This Wookiee does not like this line of questioning. You'll anger him past reasoning.» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]
- Freyyr: «What did you just say?» [K1/Hall_of_the_Chieftain]

**[K1/Juhani]**
- Xor: «*grunt* What the hell is your kind doing here? Bad enough I have to deal with all these other idiots, but now there's a stinkin' Cathar on this world too?» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «Hide behind your pet Cathar... And a woman at that! We should have exterminated all you Cathar when we had the chance.» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «I know enough that... Hey, wait a minute... you look familiar somehow...» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «This doesn't concern you, Jedi trash. Hm... now where could I have... No, he's dead and she likely is too. I...» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «Maybe I was wrong. Still, I think a specimen like you would be a nice addition to my collection. So what would it take, Jedi, for you to sell your pet here to me?» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «Put one of you down like the animals you are. So easy... then I saw one of the females on the auction block, but those darn Jedi...» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «What? Me? Oh ho! Now I recognize where I've seen that face before. You were the little Cathar I was going to purchase. But those Jedi came and stole my pet away from me!» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «Come now, will you let your pet go? I'm sure we can come up with a price we both think is fair.» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «Yes, yes... Say no to the dark side... But I will have you yet! Heh.» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «Ack! *cough* Heh, you seem to have defeated me, Cathar worm, but I'll have the last laugh in the end!» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «*cough* You may have killed me here, today, but I had the pleasure of killing off your species! Not everyone who fought alongside the Mandalorians did it for their stupid concept of 'honor'. Some of us did it just for the pleasure: the pleasure of seeing your world burn.» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «*cough* *cough* Heh, but I'm beyond your petty vengeance now, girl. You can never have your revenge!» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «Worm! I killed your people on Cathar in the sky, on the ground, in their homes. I hunted them down like the animals they were, just to see them burn. But the treacherous beasts fought back and left me with injuries that meant I could never fight again... or so they thought!» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «Hahah- *cough* *cough* But you won't! You're the cowardly type, all of you. You don't have the guts to finish me now!» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «Give in and embrace your hate! You're no better than me! You're nothing!» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «Haha! Your kind always were weak-minded!» [K1/Juhani]
- Xor: «You coward! You weak... ahhh... gurk! *death rattle*» [K1/Juhani]

**[K1/Lower_Shadowlands]**
- Freyyr: «The words of outsiders are tainted with lies. You can't convince me otherwise.» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]
- Mission: «I know you're supposed to save the galaxy and all, but could you try being less of a scummy core slime while you're doing it?» [K1/Lower_Shadowlands]

**[K1/North_Beach]**
- Jolee: «Wait a minute - do you understand these creatures? You must have learned their language when you came here in search of the secrets of the Star Forge. Back when you were still Revan.» [K1/North_Beach]
- Mission: «Whoa. Can you actually understand what these guys are saying? I guess you must have come here back when you were Revan, huh?» [K1/North_Beach]

**[K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]**
- Ahlan Matale: «Two thousand? Your greed is outstripped only by your utter lack of any sense of proportion, Jedi!» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Bastila: «Two thousand? Even if he would pay such a fee, what could you possibly need so many credits for?» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Carth: «That's quite a line. You actually believe that? Don't you think someone is allowed to value privacy?» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Bastila: «Privacy is all well and good, but justice must be valued higher. Is it not obvious to you that this man hides something?» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Carth: «What are you doing? Are you trying to get everyone here killed?» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud] *(+2 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Bastila: «*whisper* It is obvious Master Vandar senses something is not right with your story. Yet he chooses to believe you, and I will not question the wisdom of a Jedi Master.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- Carth: «*whisper* It's obvious he doesn't believe you. He must have some reason for letting you get away with this lie. I swear, I'll never understand these Jedi.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]

**[K1/Sandral_Grounds]**
- C8-42: «No! You'd make her suffer like that?! You monster!» [K1/Sandral_Grounds]

**[K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]**
- Mekel: «Oh, it is. I wonder which one will be the last standing? Should I tell him then that his hopes are in vain, do you think?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Jolee: «Hmm. Did you save the boy or just crush his dreams? I wonder...» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- HK-47: «Observation: Marvelous elimination of the enemy, master! I applaud you!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Jolee: «If a Sith told you to go jump off a cliff, would you?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Prospective Sith: «[Success] R-really? That... that sounds like it's, heh, very dangerous. You survived it, though... maybe it isn't so bad...» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]

**[K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]**
- Shasa: «Ah, I see you are not as foolish as the others I have met. It is good to see that the Jedi Order also realizes the strength of the Sith, and will do what is right when the time comes. If you encounter my father, tell him I will not return to him. I have cast my lot with the Sith and that is all.» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]
- Carth: «*whisper* How could you say something like that? What's gotten into you?» [K1/Sith_Base_(Manaan)]

**[K1/Star_Forge_System]**
- Jolee: «You know, during our rather rapid descent I noticed the hulls of many crashed ships scattered across the landscape. Maybe the parts you need can be found among their wreckage.» [K1/Star_Forge_System]

**[K1/Swoop_Registration]**
- Zaalbar: «How is this different than slavery? You push the boy against his will.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Nico Senvi: «[Success] I... I'll sign the contract. Yeah, I guess that's best. I mean, the deal can't be all bad, right? I mean, you'd know, right? And all I want is to race. Who cares about the credits... who cares. Thanks for... making things clear. If you don't mind, I think we're done. Goodbye.» [K1/Swoop_Registration]
- Carth: «I'm no Jedi, but are you really supposed to be using the Force for things like this?» [K1/Swoop_Registration]

**[K1/Temple_Exterior]**
- Jolee: «I had a... a premonition. A vision through the Force. There's great danger inside that Temple. I can't let you face it alone.» [K1/Temple_Exterior]
- Jolee: «Your destiny - maybe the fate of the entire galaxy - could be forever changed inside that Temple. I'm not about to let you face that alone. Not after my premonition. There's a reason I had that vision.» [K1/Temple_Exterior]
- Jolee: «So you just tell that guide of yours to do whatever he has to do to get us all inside the Temple.» [K1/Temple_Exterior]

**[K1/Temple_Summit]**
- Bastila: «Revan - I knew you'd come for me. Malak thought you might be afraid to enter the Temple again, but he doesn't know you like I do. Not anymore. Not since you've changed.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Jolee: «It's not to late for you, Bastila. The dark side is evil but it isn't all powerful. You can still fight against it. Don't let it consume you.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Jolee: «I'm a Jedi... I'll never bow down to the Sith. If you go through with this I have no choice but to do battle against you.» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Jolee: «No! Don't give into your anger. Remain calm, remain focused. Don't let her twist your mind like this!» [K1/Temple_Summit]
- Jolee: «She's not dead, Carth - though that might have been easier for you to accept. She's fallen to the dark side and fled to the Star Forge.» [K1/Temple_Summit]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]**
- Jolee: «Now this you don't see every day. This... this is an old spirit full of the Force. I sense great sadness and regret. Walk carefully...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- HK-47: «Analysis: This emanation barely registers on my sensors, master. Blaster fire may be ineffective... but I am prepared to test that at your word.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]**
- Mekel: «[Success] You mean... the light side? I've never thought about that. Can you... can you even go back? I've done some... I mean, I've hurt a lot of people.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Jolee: «There's always remorse. And atonement. That's the harder path, though, boy. Think you can do that?» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]

**[K1/Upper_Shadowlands]**
- Mission: «It's kind of nice to meet a Jedi who isn't all hung up on this light side, dark side stuff. It gets pretty tiring, you know?» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Bastila: «I can feel the power of the Force within you, old man, but I do not sense the taint of the dark side. I think you are a servant of the light, despite what you claim.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]
- Bastila: «That was beneath you. I hope in the future you will strive to be better than this.» [K1/Upper_Shadowlands]

**[K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- Dak Vesser: «My name's Dak Vesser. I'm assigned to the valley ruins, as you can see. We've been painstakingly uncovering these tombs for many months, now.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «Save it. The Jedi aren't for me... not anymore. Look... I'm not going to ask why a couple of Jedi are in the Valley of the Dark Lords.  I don't want to know. It's better that I don't. In fact, I'll just get out of here and leave you to whatever it is you're planning to do. Fair enough?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «Does it matter? I'm going. Isn't that enough for you?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «If you must know, I intend to take the first ship off of this rock. Why should that interest you? I trust you got whatever you wanted from the tombs?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

**[K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]**
- HK-47: «Query: Shall I begin blasting, master? We could have your hirsute companion free in no time.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]
- Wookiee: «We are threatened every day. You are just another body in the army we face from all sides, at all times.» [K1/Village_of_Rwookrrorro]

**[K1/West_Central]**
- Bastila: «*whisper* The Force is not a thing to be trifled with. Continue to use it frivolously and you may find yourself straying down the dark path.» [K1/West_Central]

**[K1/Worrroznor's_Home]**
- Bastila: «You mustn't let greed rule your decision here. Justice must be served.» [K1/Worrroznor's_Home]
