## Uthar Wynn
*Corpus coverage: 138 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 86 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 3 pronoun references resolved from context · 23 lines addressed to them · 25 narration lines · 8 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Uthar is headmaster of the Korriban Sith academy. Yuthura notes: «Uthar's former master still lives» [K1/Hangar].
### Personality and motivations
A teacher of Sith competition: «All life must compete in order to flourish.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]. Aware of Yuthura's ambitions.
### Arc (scene by scene)
- Admits recruits.
- Runs the prestige tests.
- Final test in Naga Sadow's tomb.
- Branches: killed with Yuthura's help, betrayed by her, or he kills her. The dark-path test is to kill a captive or Yuthura (see the narration bank).
### Relationships
- Yuthura (apprentice and rival).
- Revan: senses the change in him.
### Abilities
- Mechanics: Force Lightning, Choke, Death Field, Throw Lightsaber; Force 370 [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow].
### Feats
- Senses that the player is Revan [K1/Hangar].
### Power-scaling statements
- «perhaps you would care to demonstrate for us the powers of a Sith Master» [K1/Hangar]
### Duels and fights
- vs Revan: Uthar dies.
### Verdict implications
- Mid Sith Master, below Malak and Bandon by role.
- Confidence: medium.
### Open questions
- None.

### Abilities: game mechanics (FLAGGED: gameplay stats, not lore)
- [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow] Uthar Wynn: Class Jedi Guardian, Level 15, Vitality 213, Force 370, Defense 24. Powers: Burst of Speed, Choke, Death Field, FR=25, Fear, Force Lightning, Force Push, Plague, Throw Lightsaber. Feats: Improved Critical Strike, Improved Flurry, Improved Power Attack, Master Jedi Defense, Master Sense, Master Two-Weapon Fighting.

**[K1/Hangar]**

- «Indeed? Then perhaps you would care to demonstrate for us the powers of a Sith Master. ...No? Then perhaps you shall keep your foolishness to yourself and actually learn something.» [K1/Hangar]
- «Excellent work, my young Sith. Your enemy lies dead at your feet and you are victorious... the final test is complete.» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «You are truly a Sith in spirit, and one gifted with great power. Of this I no longer have any doubt. There is much more for you to learn, perhaps... but not here. There is... something very strange about you, young one. Something I cannot place. You are like no other student I have ever met.» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Is it... is it possible that you are not what you seem to be? Tell me, young one... tell me what you really are.» [K1/Hangar]
- «Ah, I see. So it is... Darth Revan, not dead as we were led to believe. And yet... and yet you are *not* Revan, are you? What has occurred between the time of your reported death and now I can only guess at, but you have changed much. Regardless, your destiny does take you away from here... to confront your old pupil, without a doubt. Yes... that is what lies before you. Go to your destiny, then, my Dark Lord. I shall say nothing of what you have done here. Should you prove victorious and wish to return, then I and all Sith shall be your servants.» [K1/Hangar]
- «I see. No matter what you might be, then, I am certain that your path takes you elsewhere... and soon.» [K1/Hangar]
- «Go on to your destiny, then, and return here afterwards... if that is what you still desire.» [K1/Hangar]

**[K1/Shyrack_Caves]**
- «Ahhh. The ones I ordered executed for their mutiny? It's done, then?» [K1/Shyrack_Caves]
- «Indeed. I had the caverns checked after you left. Well done.» [K1/Shyrack_Caves]
- «[Success] Indeed? We checked the caves and found only a couple of bodies. Perhaps the shyrack ate them? Ah, it is just as good, I suppose.» [K1/Shyrack_Caves]
- «The lesson is learned, I believe.» [K1/Shyrack_Caves]

**[K1/Sith_Academy]**
- «Greetings, prospective students. It appears we have a late entry. Who do you bring before me, Yuthura? A young human, bristling with the Force?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Most impressive, if it is true. Those who were too weak to stand against you deserved their fate, so expect no retribution from us. There is much you can learn from the Sith, and we from you.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «An honest if evasive answer. Likely it is more true than you realize. Allow me to speak of the deeper matters at hand.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «A diplomatic answer, if not the core of the matter. Our power is obvious. The question in truth goes much deeper than the surface.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «An honest answer, and one I would expect from anyone who has been exposed to those sad relics amongst the Jedi. As always, young human, the truth is not quite so simple.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Untrue. The Force demands discipline from all who use it, and the Sith demand duty. The truth goes far beyond such a superficial evaluation.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «The Jedi equate the light with goodness and strength and the dark with weakness and evil. That is their tradition and it is truly no surprise that they cling to it for comfort. We, however, do not treat the Force as a burden. We treat it as a gift, a thing to be celebrated. We use it to acquire power over others... and why should we not? Because the Jedi say we should not? We are as the Force is meant to be. The Jedi would hide that from you... they would tell you the dark side is too quick, too easy, all so that they need never challenge the passions that lie within them. Joining with us means realizing your true potential. It means not stifling yourself solely for the sake of hide-bound shamans and their antiquated notion of order. Be what you were meant to be.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «What say you, Lashowe? Are you ready to learn the secrets of the dark side? Dare you?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Brash and fiery, as expected. Turn that passion to your advantage, child. What of you, Mekel? Are you ready?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I sense much anger within you, young one. That is good. That will provide you power. And Shaardan... what of you?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I see. You had best gather your wits for the trial ahead, boy, or you will not last. And you, young human? Does this interest you? Are you ready to learn more of what I speak?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Your slaves? They are irrelevant. They may accompany you, so long as they do not interfere with us or with your training. Are you ready or not, young one?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Are you? I can see into your heart, young human, and I see the dark kernel that is there. If it is ready to sprout remains to be seen.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «And so shall it be.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «An honest answer. You will make little progress, however, if you do not come to learn that any opportunity, even an unsure one, must be snatched.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Ha ha ha! You are brave to argue, young one. I have met few students who would speak so openly to me. Ask yourself first what this 'morality' is? How is it created? Who does it benefit? Where do your notions of 'good' and 'evil' come from? You may come to the realization that morality is but an obstacle to overcome... or not. The discovery is yours to make, should you be ready.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Now, then. All of you five recruits have shown a degree of facility with the Force... you all have the potential to become true Sith. Only one of you, however, will succeed. The one who succeeds will be admitted to the academy as a full Sith. All others must wait until next year and try again... if you survive. My pupil, Yuthura, shall be your teacher and master while you attempt to prove yourselves. Heed her words.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «If you wish to gain a lead over your competitors, the first of you to learn the Code of the Sith and tell me of it will be rewarded. The rest is for you to discover. Welcome to the dark side, my children... your one chance at true greatness lies here.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Greetings, young one. You have much to do, yet... you have gained little prestige. You will have to work quickly if you hope to best the others.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Greetings, young one. I have found you to be most impressive... you are off to an excellent beginning.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Greetings, young one. You have done very well for yourself so far. Continue and your promise shall be fulfilled.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Oh? It is no more complicated than I said. Perform impressive deeds or bring me a suitably valuable artifact from the Valley of the Dark Lords.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Really, however, you should seek out Yuthura and get her advice. She is your trainer, after all, yes?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «There is an exit that leads to the surface of the planet... the valley is only a short trip from there. It is not difficult to find.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «You wish to know more about me, do you? And why would I indulge your idle curiosity? Will we be good friends, you think? I strived many years to hone my power until I was able to drive out my predecessor and rule the academy. If you are smart, you will aim to do the same, or better. There is nothing else that you need to know. Do not ask again, young one.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Indeed. You have much to do, yet.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Very well. You have impressed me... definitely a good start.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «You have gained considerable prestige... you are very close to the end. Do not falter now.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Go, now... you have done well, but you have not impressed me enough to declare you the victor. I have matters to attend to.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «You have impressed me enough, by my estimation, to become a Sith in full. Congratulations, young one... you have bested the others quite completely. You have but one final test which you must take, and this requires us to travel to the tomb of Naga Sadow in the Valley of the Dark Lords. I would advise you to be rested and equipped before we leave. Return to your quarters now and seek me out in the morning. When you return, make sure that you have all that you will need... for you will face your test alone. Go, and may the Force serve you well.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «So, then. I take it you are ready to travel to the tomb of Naga Sadow? Your final test should not be delayed.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «It is a tomb that belonged to an ancient Sith Lord... the greatest of Korriban's Sith Lords, in fact. It is there that your test is performed.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I will tell you the details when we are there, young one. You will, however, fight for your life... that is why I suggest you be in sufficient health.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Then return when you are. Do not delay overly long, young one.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «[[../Tomb of Naga Sadow» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Oh? What is my apprentice up to now?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I see. Well, then... should you change your mind... you know where to come, yes?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I see. It is good that you have come to me with this information, young one. It is a bit... ironic... that Yuthura has begun her plotting. I have been aware of her growing ambitions for some time, and had in fact already decided to remove her. Normally, the one who gains the most prestige would engage in a final trial... two of the students would fight. This time it will be Yuthura who battles, though she does not know it. Perhaps it will be you who combats her? Yes... perhaps so. This is what you can do. Give this pad to Adrenas... he will put some poison in her bath. This will weaken Yuthura for that final test, making her an easy target. Rather generous of me, don't you think?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «For coming to me with this information and betraying your foolish trainer, I feel you are worthy of prestige, young one.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Do not forget our agreement regarding Yuthura's treachery. We shall put her in her place... should you reach the final test, that is.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «A student, is it? What might you have to say?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I see. Then leave me be if you have nothing to offer. I am sure you have much to do.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Ahhhh. I think I know this one you speak of. You gained his confidence to admit such, did you? I commend your competitive spirit... you have certainly impressed me. I shall speak to the boy straight away.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Then finish the Code as I speak it. "Peace is a lie, there is only..."» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «No. No, that is incorrect. I would have thought merely reciting the Code to be a simple matter. Ah, well.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «"Through passion I gain..."» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «"Through strength I gain..."» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «"Through power I gain..."» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «"Through victory...."» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «"The Force shall free me." Yes, good. You have, indeed, learned the Code of the Sith. Speaking the words and knowing the words are, however, two different things.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Tell me, then, true or false: it is our passion that fuels the Force.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Tell me then, true or false: there is nothing worse than love.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Tell me then, true or false: victory by any means is desirable.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «No, it is true. Our passion, our hate and desire... that is what brings conflict, which gives us power.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «False. Mercy is worse than love.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «False. Unless victory proves your superiority, it is an illusion, it is temporary.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Sadly, it seems you did not understand the Code as well as I thought.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Well done. I will test you no more... you know the Code. Very impressive.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Oh? And why would that be of use to me? His interrogator already brought me that information moments ago. A valiant attempt, young one, but you must be quicker than that if you wish to impress me.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «You do? That is most interesting. Where might that be?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Oh? How very sad, young one. You should learn to grab your advantages whenever you can.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Hmm. How ironic that I recently had his vessel scrapped for parts, then. I had assumed it had been thoroughly scanned... apparently not. Still... you have done well in bringing me information that others could not unearth on their own. I am impressed.» [K1/Sith_Academy]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]**

**[K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]**

- «Do you understand what I have told you? Are you ready to begin?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]

- «No. That is part of the test. Overcome the obstacles that lie ahead on your own.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]

- «You must locate the ceremonial lightsaber and return here to us. Yuthura and I cannot help you in this, young one.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «So you return to us with the lightsaber in hand, as I knew you would.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You took a great risk in acquiring the artifact. You used your mind and your power... no peaceful meditation, no pacifism. Sometimes you must fight in order to achieve. This gives you your passion, this makes you stronger. This is what makes you superior. That is the lesson we teach with that part of the final test. Do you understand?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «True, but most would say they fight only when they must... or not at all. We are the ones who know that struggle is what makes us superior.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «When a Jedi acts, it is with skill, true. But the Jedi teach you that your passions are counter-productive. That strength is only found in peace. Did you not feel a moment of excitement in the tomb? Did your passions not flow and make you feel stronger and more alive? What purpose would be served by restricting yourself from such? I would say only the purpose of the Jedi. Through this they would control you and restrict your potential.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Perhaps not, to someone of your natural strength. The point is in the recognition of the nature of the act rather than its sheer difficulty.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «If you came to the academy, you must have felt the stirrings within you that cry out for the freedom of the dark side, that cry out for release. I can only show you the path that will lead to power and superiority. You have followed it this far... you must decide if you are capable of continuing.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «This is where your path ends, young one. There is no turning back once you have embarked on it.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Good. The last part of your test will now commence. Here you will learn the lesson of competition. All life must compete in order to flourish. Such is the natural way of the universe. To stand still is to know death... one must always be moving forward. So is it the same amongst the Sith. Compete or die. Mercy is irrelevant. So it behooves you in this final test to strike down one you are familiar with, for no other reason than to prove you are superior and without mercy. Normally this would be against another student. You, however, get a special treat. You will fight Yuthura, here... my own apprentice.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Indeed. You have become too ambitious, Yuthura. It is time for you to die and someone more... talented... to take your place.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «So... you know, do you? Well, it makes no difference. You have become too ambitious, Yuthura... it is time for you to die.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Oh? Is this true, young one? You wish to join Yuthura in her foolish scheme?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Sadly, Yuthura, it is you who are mistaken. Your pupil is more ambitious than you realize... isn't that so, young one?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You are indeed a fool if you think to oppose us both, young one.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «This makes no sense. You will never become a Sith if you turn on us both! What could you hope to accomplish?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Do not fret, my dear. All will be as it was quickly enough.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You will never leave here!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «So the time has come, has it? You both wish to stand against a Sith Master and perish, do you? THEN SO BE IT!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «The final test continues as it should. Proceed and be rewarded, young one.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «It appears, my dear, that some tests are fairer than others. I wish you luck.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Do not let your attention waver! Strike her down!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «I must say that it is quite disappointing that you must die now, as well, seeing as you didn't inform me earlier of Yuthura's plans.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «I must say that it is quite disappointing that you must die now, as well, since there's no telling how far your scheming with Yuthura went.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «So you did. And now I shall destroy you as I would any other Sith who betrayed me.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Destroy me? I am not Yuthura, young one. I am a Sith Master... and I tire of foolish upstarts.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Is that so? So you were a spy all along? How interesting.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «So let's get this over with, shall we? I have a new batch of candidates arriving tomorrow.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «I must say that... unnhh! What... what is happening?! My strength is... fading! What have you done?!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You... you fool! You will never... never be a Sith, now!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «What?! But, but... why did... you come?! Why... are you here, if not to become Sith?!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Why that is, I cannot say. Your path takes you elsewhere, however, and soon. Go on to your destiny, then, and return here afterwards... if that is what you still desire.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You have passed the final test, but I see it is not yet your destiny to join the order. Perhaps that will come in time, young one. There is much you must accomplish, first. The dark side has plans for you. As I said... return once that is done, if you desire.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You... I will destroy you, mongrel! I will rip the flesh from your bones!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «May the Force serve you well, young one.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Your destiny lies elsewhere, young one, not with the Sith... at least, not yet. Go... and return another time, if you still wish to.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]**
- «Ahh, my old master. Still... unless you bring back something of his, some writings or similar, I am unimpressed. Jorak's fate means nothing to me.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- «Maybe it's better to say that Mekel is dead, yes? Considering the circumstances, I am unimpressed... though you do now have one less opponent.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- «Oh? Let me see... it appears my old master was busy studying the writings in one of the tombs. How interesting. I do hope you had to pry this from his dead fingers. Regardless, you have impressed me with your worthy act.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]

**[K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- «*chuckle* I had heard that such an artifact existed. Tell me, young one... did you acquire this device by yourself?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «You lie. Still, what matters is the fact that you succeeded. And Lashowe... does she yet live?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «So I see. And Lashowe... does she yet live?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «You didn't kill her? How very foolish. Now you have an enemy to contend with. The holocron, itself, is an impressive item, however.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Ha ha! Well done! You gained an artifact through trickery and eliminated an opponent at the same time. That is deserving of great prestige!» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «She did. Prestige is not shared, however, if that is what you are wondering. If you aided her, it was to her benefit and not yours.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- «Killed her out of revenge, did you? Well done. I can appreciate such drama, believe me. I am most impressed that you have eliminated an opponent so handily.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

### Quote bank B: what others say about Uthar Wynn (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K1/Hangar]**
- Yuthura Ban: «Is that such a daunting prospect? It is not as if I am asking you to perform the task alone, or as if you will get nothing out of it. Master Uthar cannot prevail against us both. That's all there is to it. It is a very simple matter.» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Yuthura Ban: «Anything is possible. Uthar's former master still lives... though he is in no condition to challenge his former pupil for leadership. Perhaps the same is true for Revan. Perhaps Revan is out there, somewhere, waiting to take revenge upon Malak. More likely what we were told is the truth: Revan is dead. Malak would be foolish to leave his old master alive, considering all the knowledge Revan held.» [K1/Hangar]
- Sith Apprentice: «You there! How is it that you are leaving Naga Sadow's tomb by yourself? You went for your final test, didn't you? Where is Master Uthar?» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Sith Apprentice: «But... even if it's not Revan... what if Master Uthar is actually dead? Someone who could kill the master...» [K1/Hangar]
- Sith Apprentice: «If Master Uthar is dead, then he was a weak fool! Now attack! *I* shall be the new Dark Lord of Korriban!!» [K1/Hangar]

**[K1/Shyrack_Caves]**
- Thalia May: «So you have heard about us. Yes, we ran away before Master Uthar could execute us. Is that so hard to believe?» [K1/Shyrack_Caves]
- Thalia May: «My name is Thalia May. The others here and myself refused to murder a bunch of people on a whim from Master Uthar... so here we are. We thought that becoming a Sith was what we always wanted, but that wasn't true. Now all we want is to get out of here. Fair enough?» [K1/Shyrack_Caves]
- Thalia May: «[Failure] No! I know what Sith like you want from us! You're not bringing our heads to Master Uthar so he can pat you on the back! They're here to kill us! Get them!! Get them!!» [K1/Shyrack_Caves]

**[K1/Sith_Academy]**
- Yuthura Ban: «A human that has had some training, it seems, Master Uthar. Very promising, I think.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «I dare, Master Uthar! I am ready!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «As Master Uthar said, none of you are true Sith *yet*. For that to occur, one of you must do enough of worth... gain enough prestige... to be selected. What is an act of worth? You must learn that for yourselves. Remember that you are competitors, here... fight for your destiny, or go home.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «What are you doing here? Go back to Uthar and tell him you're ready to take your final test! Our moment is at hand, go!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «As I said, you're no doubt going to be the one whom Uthar chooses to become a Sith. With my help, of course. Once that occurs, he will take you into the Valley of the Dark Lords to the tomb of Naga Sadow to administer the final test. There you and I will be alone with him. The perfect time to, shall we say, arrange for a change in the academy's leadership?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «Reaching that map, as they did, is part of the final test. At any rate, it is not the tomb that is important. That Uthar will be alone is what is important.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «I suppose you could do that. Even the hint of betrayal from his pupil and Uthar would move to eliminate me. But this is a very good opportunity for you... you can start off your Sith career as the right hand of the head of the academy: me. Uthar will not offer you that. Do you believe Uthar would not be aware of this? He would quickly figure out what you have to gain by betraying him... and would never believe someone would pass that up. Tell Master Uthar what you wish. Were I you, I would think first of what the consequences of such an action might be.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «My 'part' in this is to help you gain enough prestige... and slow down the progress of the other hopefuls. I want you in that tomb when the time is right. We destroy Master Uthar together and I take his place... and you take your place at my side. A beautiful plan in its sheer simplicity.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «*chuckle* You must not know much about the Sith, yet. If Uthar were to even suspect that I might be trying to supplant him, true or not... Well, let's just say that he wouldn't likely stop to ask too many questions before ensuring I was dealt with.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «I understand you have impressed Master Uthar at least a little. That is good... we are one step closer towards completing my plan.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «You must impress Master Uthar... go to him with news of your deeds or artifacts you have discovered. The first and easiest way would be for you to learn the Code of the Sith and speak it to him. I can teach you that, if you like. Shall I go on?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «Not unless you are accompanied by Master Uthar, no. It is strictly forbidden... and I truly doubt you could bypass the doors to the tomb with anything less than ship blasters. Even then, perhaps.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «None. I do not even know how Master Uthar gains entrance, to tell the truth, though I look forward to discovering that fact. I have searched many times, so do not bother... there is no other way. Do not fret, however. Simply get the prestige you need and you will see the tomb soon enough.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «[Failure] You know all that you need to. We can exchange niceties once Uthar is gone, if you desire.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «[Failure] Friends? We can be friends once Uthar is dead. Until then, a 'friend' is a weakness.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Adrenas: «He did? Allow me to see that for a moment... Ah, yes, I see. I will do as Master Uthar requests, of course. Thank you for bringing this to me.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «Ahhh. How very clever of him. Normally you would face some other student in the tomb, preferably one of the failed ones. Obviously Uthar has decided to get rid of me. But why...? Did you say something to him?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «If you think Uthar will truly be so grateful to you for double-crossing me, think again. Your only chance lies with me.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «Let me see that. Hmm... so it's true. Thank you for bringing this to me... now I will be far stronger than Uthar expects.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «Hah! He's a fool. I can make preparations to resist any poisons. Thank you for telling me... now I will be far stronger than Uthar expects.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «Here... this passcard will allow you access to Uthar's quarters. It's at the far end of the passages with the other quarters. Take this device as well and put it inside his cot. No-one should see you do it. Then Uthar will be too weak to stand up against us. Don't even think of failing me. You and I are too far into this now to back out.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Kel Algwinn: «Yes, of course. I must admit I've never really understood it very well. Enough to please Master Uthar, I guess... but I still have trouble with it.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Kel Algwinn: «Uh, eh, maybe you're right. *sigh* I'll go and... tell Master Uthar that I'm not ready for this. Maybe it won't be so bad. Th-thank you.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «There are a group of students who refused an order by Master Uthar to execute a group of civilians in the colony. Uthar declared that they were to be executed, themselves, but the students escaped and fled before the order could be carried out. We are certain that they have not left Korriban. No doubt they have not gotten far and are simply in hiding... we just do not know where, exactly. If you were to locate them and carry out Uthar's order, I am sure he would be most appreciative. Shall I go on?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «The rebel students I told you about must still be found and dealt with... Master Uthar would be greatly impressed by their deaths, I am sure.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Teacher: «Ah. Let me tell you what I can, then. A group of students were ordered to perform executions on civilians within the colony. They refused to carry out the orders. Master Uthar naturally ordered them killed... but they fled before this could take place. If one could carry out Uthar's order, it would be worth considerable prestige. There are five of them, if I remember... and they cannot be far, as they have no method to leave the planet. Likely hiding in the hills on the surface.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Student: «Greetings. You're one of the new hopefuls, aren't you? I imagine you're eager to garner some prestige for Master Uthar.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Student: «Hmm. Is that the sword of Ajunta Pall you have there? Fascinating. If it truly is, you might want to hand that over to Master Uthar, no?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Tariga: «There are four tombs that have been uncovered so far, each holding the remains of a Sith Lord from a time long ago when the Sith were first on Korriban. We have found evidence that there were larger structures underground, a veritable ancient fortress... but it appears to have been destroyed. The tombs are all that remain intact. Currently we are allowed access only to three tombs... the one furthest to the left may only be entered by Master Uthar and those he brings with him. Fortunately there is still much else for us to find. I am pleased that you have joined us.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Teacher: «Heh. Well... the first thing you should know is that that is not a name you should mention around Master Uthar if you can avoid it. Jorak Uln was once Uthar's master, you see, and the head of this academy. He went a little insane, however, and Uthar forced him to flee to the surface and hide. It seems that Jorak has been spending his time kidnapping students who wander into the Valley of the Dark Lords. No idea why. I've also no idea why Uthar doesn't do something about it. If you want prestige, however, you'll find that hermit, as everyone calls him... you'll find Jorak and either kill him or bring back anything he's been writing. Uthar would like that. Now begone. I have better things to do than chat with lost hopefuls.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Mekel: «[Success] Hmph. I guess there's no harm in it. Word has it that Master Uthar gives big points for Sith artifacts that are brought back from the valley.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Interrogator: «If you manage to get the location of his cache out of him, I'll be sure to pass on the good word to Master Uthar.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Interrogator: «What?! What have you done?! I told you not to put the prisoner into shock, didn't I?! Now how are we supposed to find out where the weapon cache is?! Uthar will be furious!! Bah! Now I may as well take this fool and dump him somewhere. He's useless to us! I cannot believe you are so incompetent!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Interrogator: «Oh? I suppose one would need to be alive to go running to Master Uthar to report the location of the cache, wouldn't they? Does that mean that you are ready to battle a true Sith, then? Shall we make an issue of it?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «I would suggest you talk to the other prospective Sith and find one that you can gain an advantage with. I believe Lashowe would be an excellent choice. She is too trusting. I have little doubt you could talk her into letting you 'help' her somehow. Just make sure that it is you who gains the advantage, not her. Be ruthless, if you must. That is the only thing that will impress Uthar.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Yuthura Ban: «The only other idea I can suggest to you is that you head into the Valley of the Dark Lords on the surface. There are ancient ruins there which can be explored. It's possible you might find some artifacts or information in them which would please Uthar... it's difficult to say for certain.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «I've found an artifact that will impress Uthar far more than anything you could ever scrounge up. I'll win for sure.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «[Success] Yes, I do... want to work with you. I suppose it's possible that Master Uthar could award both of us prestige. We'd be ahead of the others, for sure...» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «[Success] Flattery, is it? I... suppose it's possible that Master Uthar could award both of us prestige. We'd be ahead of the others, for sure...» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «[Success] Hmmm. You make a decent case. I suppose it's possible that Master Uthar could award both of us prestige. We'd be ahead of the others, for sure... I want something out of this, first, however. I want you to tell me about some lead you have for where I could find more prestige.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «Because it probably contains secrets from the time when the Sith ruins on this planet first existed. You don't think that Master Uthar would find that impressive?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Sith Duelist: «[Failure] Don't be foolish. If Uthar wants to send an order like that, he can bloody well tell me himself.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Dustil Onasi: «Oh, I'm sorry, did I hurt your feelings? Why don't you run to Master Uthar and tell him all about it. I'm sure he'll be very sympathetic.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Dustil Onasi: «Cute. I wonder how interested Master Uthar would be to know just who he has in his web. Unless you've switched sides, Father? But I doubt that.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Dustil Onasi: «Maybe it doesn't matter. Or maybe Master Uthar would be interested in knowing just what kind of company he has present here?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Dustil Onasi: «Yeah, I can just imagine what you two are up to, here. Master Uthar will be more than happy to hear about it, I'm sure.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Carth: «Look at it. It belongs to Master Uthar, doesn't it?» [K1/Sith_Academy]

**[K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]**
- Yuthura Ban: «Who am I? You must not have been on Korriban for very long. Either that or you have been feeling your way about blindly. Luckily for you, I am in a charitable mood. I am Yuthura Ban, second only to Master Uthar of the Sith academy here in Dreshdae. I am the one who decides which few of the many hopefuls who travel here to train actually become a Sith. Why? Is it your desire to enter the academy? Is that why you are here?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]**
- Shaardan: «Impressive bravado, I'm sure. Do you honestly think I would let an opportunity to impress Master Uthar pass me by so easily?» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Shaardan: «Oh, but I insist. I'm not about to let a chance to impress Master Uthar pass me by.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- Shaardan: «[Failure] Nice try. I may not know what the sword looks like, but I'm no gullible fool. I'm not about to pass up a chance to impress Master Uthar.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]**
- Sith Student: «No, I wouldn't do that. Well... I would if I could get away with it, but I bet Master Uthar could tell if I was lying.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Sith Student: «At any rate, if you haven't already, you might want to go and see Master Uthar about that reward. Prestige is gold, you know.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]
- Sith Student: «Wow... you should have seen it. That droid came running out of the tomb like a bantha on a rampage! It fired some kind of jet pack and off it went. Whatever you did, it sure was effective... I don't think it's coming back anytime soon. You'd better go and tell Master Uthar about this, if you haven't already. You may not have destroyed the droid, but you might still get the reward.» [K1/Tomb_of_Marka_Ragnos]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]**
- Message: «[A day passes in preparation. Uthar then takes you and Yuthura to the Sith tomb. Your companions are not permitted to join you.]» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «You are weak, Uthar, and the Force has abandoned you. We have made sure of it!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Yuthura Ban: «Uthar is finished, and a new order is brought to the academy. Excellent.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Sith Apprentice: «Well, you've found it! If you've done anything to Master Uthar, you're going to pay for it!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Sith Apprentice: «But... if the spy was able to kill Master Uthar...» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Sith Apprentice: «But... what if Master Uthar is really dead? How can we hope to stand up against someone who could kill him?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Sith Apprentice: «Don't be a sniveling coward! I will prove this one a spy... and if Uthar is dead, *I* shall become the new master!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Sith Duelist: «[Success] Hmmm... I knew something was up when Yuthura showed up and claimed control of the academy. Uthar must be planning a fight.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Sith Duelist: «Well, you go back to wherever Master Uthar is and tell him I'm on it. I'll get these prisoners out of the academy pronto!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Kel Algwinn: «Have you heard? Master Uthar is dead and Yuthura Ban has taken over the academy. How brutal...» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]**
- Jorak Uln: «What?! Old hermit?! Can’t that blasted Uthar teach a single thing right these days?! Slander! That’s what it is!» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Jorak Uln: «Liar. You think I can't tell? Obviously the academy is lax in its training, as usual. Stupid Uthar.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Jorak Uln: «Anyhow, I'd like to propose that we move onto the main event. You see, I'd like to discover if you've got the pluck of an old-fashioned Sith. Most of the drek Uthar has been passing through these days is so pathetic. Take young Mekel here... I already tested him. Didn't I, Mekel?» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]
- Mekel: «Well... you can have those writings he mentioned. I won't fight you for them. Master Uthar will be very pleased with you if you give them to him, you know.» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]

**[K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- Dak Vesser: «Master Uthar won't allow us to dig with lasers or sonics... we have to use hand tools only so we don't accidentally destroy anything we run across. That makes progress very slow, however.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]
- Dak Vesser: «Some. Most of the interesting stuff is still in the tombs, but Master Uthar prefers to leave them to be explored by the students, as a challenge. In time, they will probably become safe enough for us to enter. Until that time, however, we're stuck to digging up the valley piece by piece.» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

## Yuthura Ban
*Corpus coverage: 248 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 31 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 2 pronoun references resolved from context · 23 lines addressed to them · 24 narration lines · 23 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Yuthura is a Twi'lek former slave and ex-Jedi apprentice who became a Sith master at the academy [K1/Sith_Academy].
### Personality and motivations
Ambitious; plots against Uthar. Recites the Sith Code [K1/Sith_Academy].
### Arc (scene by scene)
- Recruiter.
- Plot against Uthar.
- **Light path:** can be redeemed: «My old master said he would start exercises with me again» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow].
- **Dark path:** fights Revan or betrays.
### Relationships
- Uthar.
- Revan.

- Sith master; mechanics in the narration bank.

- None major.
