### Quote bank A: Yuthura Ban's own lines, by scene (page)

**[K1/Hangar]**
- «I would consider that an amusing ploy to try and enter the Academy. Why? Who will you claim to be? Darth Malak, himself?» [K1/Hangar]
- «What about Revan? Revan is dead. You may have a natural gift with the Force, human, but you've no gift for lies.» [K1/Hangar]
- «Obviously you are a Jedi. One who is very strong in the Force, it seems. So were you part of the order for very long? Did they train you?» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I sense... that you are very strong in the Force. That you have already honed some of your abilities. Jedi training, is it?» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «What an odd thing to say... and, even stranger, I do not sense any deception within you. You are a curious puzzle, human, a puzzle with great power.» [K1/Hangar]
- «With that kind of power, you could become a great Sith. Perhaps... if I let you. Does that interest you?» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Hmm. A strange response, indeed. Is your mind maladjusted in some way? You should make for an interesting pupil, indeed.» [K1/Hangar]
- «I will take you to the academy and we shall see if you are ready to join the ranks of the Sith.» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Is that such a daunting prospect? It is not as if I am asking you to perform the task alone, or as if you will get nothing out of it. Master Uthar cannot prevail against us both. That's all there is to it. It is a very simple matter.» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «And so it should. It is central to our beliefs. It is my responsibility to replace my master when I am strong enough.» [K1/Hangar]
- «Revan? You mean Darth Malak's former master?» [K1/Hangar]
- «Very little. I never met him personally, though I hear he was a powerful man. Very charismatic. In the end, Revan was outshone by his pupil. Such is the way of the Sith. Why? Is this dead man of importance to you?» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Anything is possible. Uthar's former master still lives... though he is in no condition to challenge his former pupil for leadership. Perhaps the same is true for Revan. Perhaps Revan is out there, somewhere, waiting to take revenge upon Malak. More likely what we were told is the truth: Revan is dead. Malak would be foolish to leave his old master alive, considering all the knowledge Revan held.» [K1/Hangar]
- «Is there any reason I should? Beside the fact that I did not know Revan in life, Revan was known for wearing a full helmet and cape. Perhaps Revan's body had been disfigured by the power of the Force... it is not unheard of. Regardless, I doubt I shall be meeting Revan anytime soon.» [K1/Hangar]
- «Both Revan and Malak came to Korriban when they discovered the tomb of Naga Sadow. That was before my time.» [K1/Hangar]
- «Fair enough.» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «You are... too strong for me. I was a fool to think otherwise. I am... at your mercy.» [K1/Hangar]
- «I... suppose I am. Something tells me that you are... not like the other students. I don't know why that is. I... was right the first time, back at the cantina, wasn't I? There is something different about you... more so than I even suspected.» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I see. I should have known... right from the beginning. But you are no longer the Dark Lord you once were, are you? You are Revan... but yet you are not. What I sense of your destiny is... is still unclear.» [K1/Hangar]
- «So what happens now? Will you show me mercy? Will you... just let me leave?» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K1/Sith_Academy]**
- «A human that has had some training, it seems, Master Uthar. Very promising, I think.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «As Master Uthar said, none of you are true Sith *yet*. For that to occur, one of you must do enough of worth... gain enough prestige... to be selected. What is an act of worth? You must learn that for yourselves. Remember that you are competitors, here... fight for your destiny, or go home.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «What are you doing here? Go back to Uthar and tell him you're ready to take your final test! Our moment is at hand, go!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Ah, there you are... my favorite prospect for the year.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Absolutely. By my estimation, you are far more likely to achieve the prestige necessary to join the Sith than any of the others. As a matter of fact, I am so certain of that that I'm willing to offer you an opportunity of the once-in-a-lifetime variety. Would you like to hear it?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Good. I do so adore someone who's willing to take a chance.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Not really. Oh, don't worry... what I have in mind will benefit us both, you can be sure of that.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «'Yet' being the operative term. As I said, you have my utmost confidence.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «As I said, you're no doubt going to be the one whom Uthar chooses to become a Sith. With my help, of course. Once that occurs, he will take you into the Valley of the Dark Lords to the tomb of Naga Sadow to administer the final test. There you and I will be alone with him. The perfect time to, shall we say, arrange for a change in the academy's leadership?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «The tomb is an ancient ruin on the surface that was visited years ago by Darth Revan and Darth Malak. They discovered a Star Map there of great importance.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Reaching that map, as they did, is part of the final test. At any rate, it is not the tomb that is important. That Uthar will be alone is what is important.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I suppose you could do that. Even the hint of betrayal from his pupil and Uthar would move to eliminate me. But this is a very good opportunity for you... you can start off your Sith career as the right hand of the head of the academy: me. Uthar will not offer you that. Do you believe Uthar would not be aware of this? He would quickly figure out what you have to gain by betraying him... and would never believe someone would pass that up. Tell Master Uthar what you wish. Were I you, I would think first of what the consequences of such an action might be.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «My 'part' in this is to help you gain enough prestige... and slow down the progress of the other hopefuls. I want you in that tomb when the time is right. We destroy Master Uthar together and I take his place... and you take your place at my side. A beautiful plan in its sheer simplicity.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «*chuckle* You must not know much about the Sith, yet. If Uthar were to even suspect that I might be trying to supplant him, true or not... Well, let's just say that he wouldn't likely stop to ask too many questions before ensuring I was dealt with.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I'm so glad you see it my way.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «You are already a part of this. Don't be a fool.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I will begin to make preparations for your final test. Your only worry now is to get there... don't disappoint me.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «You have had an excellent start... go, gain more prestige!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «You are very close to succeeding... work quickly!» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «What do you wish? I am eager to get this over with.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I understand you have impressed Master Uthar at least a little. That is good... we are one step closer towards completing my plan.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I hear that you have gained even more prestige. You are closing in on victory... this is good.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «You've been doing well... I hear you've almost enough prestige to be declared the victor. Somehow I just knew you were the one who would succeed.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Were I you, I would not get too excited just yet. You still have plenty left to do.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «You must impress Master Uthar... go to him with news of your deeds or artifacts you have discovered. The first and easiest way would be for you to learn the Code of the Sith and speak it to him. I can teach you that, if you like. Shall I go on?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «[[../Sith Academy#Code of the Sith» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I have been involved in the work there over the last couple of years, but not often. Most of the excavating is left to the students. What did you wish to know?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Not unless you are accompanied by Master Uthar, no. It is strictly forbidden... and I truly doubt you could bypass the doors to the tomb with anything less than ship blasters. Even then, perhaps.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «None. I do not even know how Master Uthar gains entrance, to tell the truth, though I look forward to discovering that fact. I have searched many times, so do not bother... there is no other way. Do not fret, however. Simply get the prestige you need and you will see the tomb soon enough.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «We could learn much about the history of the ancient Sith... why they were on Korriban and why they left. Perhaps we could learn more about the origin of the Force, itself. With that knowledge could come great power.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «As you wish.» [K1/Sith_Academy] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «About myself? I am originally from Sleheyron, if you must know. I was a slave to a cruel master, Omeesh the Hutt. I am sure you don't need to know more.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I did. I also said that you didn't need to know more. Why are you asking?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «[Success] I... see. Very well. I suppose there's no harm in the tale. As I said, I was originally a slave to one of the Hutts. The Hutts control everything on Sleheyron, and a slave is nothing to them. I was determined not to be 'nothing'. One night when the drunken worm had me alone in his chambers I stabbed him and escaped the compound. I stole onto a cargo ship and was not discovered by the crew until they reached the next system. They left me for dead on a desolate planetoid, alone... but that was fine by me. I was glad to be anywhere other than Sleheyron. It was not luck that I was eventually rescued, of course. The Force was strong with me, though I didn't know that at the time. Not until the Jedi told me, that is.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «[Failure] You know all that you need to. We can exchange niceties once Uthar is gone, if you desire.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I'd... rather not discuss it. Perhaps another time.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Is there something else you needed?» [K1/Sith_Academy] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I hope to see you again.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Hello, again. I trust all is going smoothly?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I... would prefer not to discuss that.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Why? There is no point. Have I asked you about your past?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «[Failure] Friends? We can be friends once Uthar is dead. Until then, a 'friend' is a weakness.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «[Failure] There is always harm in becoming too vulnerable. I will not be as weak as you desire.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «[Success] I see. You... certainly have odd notions for one hoping to become a Sith. I'll play along... for now.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «[Success] You... have odd notions for someone hoping to become a Sith.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Let's see... after escaping from Sleheyron I was found by the Jedi. They took me in and trained me even though I was a bit older than most Padawans.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Not really, no. I never progressed beyond Padawan. I had discipline, but no peace... and after my treatment at the hands of the Hutts there was little room in me for the ways of the Jedi.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Yes, well... I imagine I am not unique. Things very easily could have been very different for me.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I wanted to use the Force to free the other slaves I knew, to fight for what I knew was right. The Jedi restrained me until I couldn't stand it any more. They claim the dark side is evil, but that isn't so. Sometimes anger and hatred are deserved and right. Sometimes things change because of it.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Any failure to get the results I want is due to a lack of power on my part. That can change, in time. As a Sith, my mettle is tested far more than when I was a Padawan.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «No... not yet. But my anger has not diminished, nor my desire to see change. The more time I spend with the Sith the more I am certain that one day I will be able to fight as I must.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «There is suffering and injustice in the universe. I am surprised the Jedi can even stand the stench of it, much less stand by and do nothing.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I know this may sound strange, but only my compassion stands in my way, now. Once that is gone let the slavers beware.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I... yes, of course. I mean losing my compassion as in... holding back...» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I... well, there is much training to do. I... may be a long time...» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Thank you. I only wish I was as strong with the Force as you are. You could change much, yourself, if you wanted to.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I suppose I shouldn't be telling you these things... sharing my weaknesses with you. Being a Sith doesn't make me a monster, however.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «But enough of that. I've talked about myself too much, surely you are tired of it.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Come back soon... my friend.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Greetings, my friend. *chuckle* I do rather enjoy saying that.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «What? What's happened? Our plan is still intact, I hope.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Good. Let's keep it that way.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Ahhh. How very clever of him. Normally you would face some other student in the tomb, preferably one of the failed ones. Obviously Uthar has decided to get rid of me. But why...? Did you say something to him?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «What?! You fool! While I respect your attempt to play both sides of the fence, you are playing a dangerous game.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «[Failure] Hmph. I truly doubt that. You are attempting to play both sides of the fence, I think. I respect that. You are playing a dangerous game, however.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «[Success] Then how could he suspect? Unless one of the others... never mind. It is not important, now.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «If you think Uthar will truly be so grateful to you for double-crossing me, think again. Your only chance lies with me.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «In the end, it works out better that it is only the two of us in the tomb with him. So long as we refuse to fight. Is there anything else I should know?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Let me see that. Hmm... so it's true. Thank you for bringing this to me... now I will be far stronger than Uthar expects.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Hah! He's a fool. I can make preparations to resist any poisons. Thank you for telling me... now I will be far stronger than Uthar expects.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Good. I'm not about to rest on my laurels, however.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Here... this passcard will allow you access to Uthar's quarters. It's at the far end of the passages with the other quarters. Take this device as well and put it inside his cot. No-one should see you do it. Then Uthar will be too weak to stand up against us. Don't even think of failing me. You and I are too far into this now to back out.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Yes... vaguely. The boy burdens himself with doubts and anxieties. It is sad to watch, really, for he does possess a certain flair with the Force. Kel will not survive long. Someone will betray him soon enough, and he will be dead. If you are smart and you know something, that person should be you.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Hmmm. You're a rather tricky human, aren't you? However did you manage to convince Kel Algwinn to reveal himself to you? No matter. I applaud you for the maneuver, at any rate. An act worthy of a Sith, surely.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Now that's what I like to see... the flash of ambition in a young human's eyes. Your species has such remarkable retinal coloring, did you know that?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Yes, I... suppose so. The weak can surprise you sometimes with their strength, however. He could have surprised us.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «What's this? Regrets? Not to worry, dear pupil, it... gets easier with time.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «You have other things to turn your attention to, however, I'm sure. What do you need?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I can help you to understand it. Would you like to hear it?<br/>"Peace is a lie, there is only passion.<br/>Through passion, I gain strength.<br/>Through strength, I gain power.<br/>Through power, I gain victory.<br/>Through victory, my chains are broken.<br/>The Force shall free me."<br/>Simple words, yet easily misunderstood. Would you care for me to explain anything?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «The Jedi would have you believe that peace is a desirable goal. That peace of the spirit is the way the Force is mastered, that a lack of conflict betters man. We know different. It is our passion... our hate and our desire... that fuels the Force. It is conflict that improves the lot of civilization and single being both. Conflict forces one to better oneself. It forces change, growth, adaption... evolution... or death. These are not our laws, but the universe's. Without conflict you have only stagnation.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «What fuels your power with the Force but your passion? The stronger, darker emotions. Anger, hatred, fear... these passions empower us.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Love is more dangerous than all those things. Love leads to anger and hatred more often that not... but it also leads to mercy, which is far worse. That, however, is a lesson for another time.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «Negative? Compared to what? What keeps even the most rudimentary creature alive? Fear to run, anger to fight... without it a creature would most surely die.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «We are far more than them, perhaps, but in some ways we are little different. To think us creatures beyond the need of simple passions is a delusion.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «The Force gives us all power, even the Jedi. It is our mastery of our passion that gives us the strength they lack.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «It is our goal to be stronger, to achieve our potential and not rest upon our laurels. We are the seekers, not the shepherds.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «The stronger you become in the Force, the more power you will achieve. But always must you fight for your power. Without strife, your victory has no meaning. Without strife, you do not advance. Without strife, there is only stagnation.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «How many sorts of victories can you imagine? Peaceful victory? Victory by sacrifice? A truce, an achievement? Unless the victory is achieved by demonstrating that your power is superior, it is only an illusion. Temporary at best. We seek more.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «This... has been argued over, and often. The chains represent our restrictions, both those placed upon us and those we place upon ourselves. Ultimately the goal of any Sith is to free herself from such restrictions. In a way, it is so we may do whatever we wish... but it is much more than that. One who has freed themselves from all restrictions has reached perfection. Their potential fulfilled. Perfect strength, perfect power, perfect destiny. Imagine it. That is our ideal, at any rate. It is said in Sith legend that the 'Sith'ari'... the perfect being... will one day lead us. But perhaps that is just a legend.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I... wonder what a being would be like. The legends say the Sith'ari will destroy us... and make us stronger than ever. But perhaps that is just a legend. Perfection is... a goal, I think, rather than a state of being. The Jedi would argue that, no doubt.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «The Force is our servant and our master. Our teacher and our companion. A weapon and a tool. Know it and you know the universe. Master it and you master the universe. Strive for perfection and the Force shall reward you.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I am not surprised. The Force is strong with you... master it and it shall serve you well.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «There are a group of students who refused an order by Master Uthar to execute a group of civilians in the colony. Uthar declared that they were to be executed, themselves, but the students escaped and fled before the order could be carried out. We are certain that they have not left Korriban. No doubt they have not gotten far and are simply in hiding... we just do not know where, exactly. If you were to locate them and carry out Uthar's order, I am sure he would be most appreciative. Shall I go on?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «The rebel students I told you about must still be found and dealt with... Master Uthar would be greatly impressed by their deaths, I am sure.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I would suggest you talk to the other prospective Sith and find one that you can gain an advantage with. I believe Lashowe would be an excellent choice. She is too trusting. I have little doubt you could talk her into letting you 'help' her somehow. Just make sure that it is you who gains the advantage, not her. Be ruthless, if you must. That is the only thing that will impress Uthar.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «I still think that approaching Lashowe would be your best move. She is too trusting and can no doubt be talked into aiding your cause.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- «The only other idea I can suggest to you is that you head into the Valley of the Dark Lords on the surface. There are ancient ruins there which can be explored. It's possible you might find some artifacts or information in them which would please Uthar... it's difficult to say for certain.» [K1/Sith_Academy]

**[K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]**
- «Is there something you need, Jedi? Make it good, for I have little patience.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «You think me a fool? You think I do not recognize a lightsaber when I see it?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «You think me a fool? You think I do not recognize robes of the order when I see them?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «You are far from the first Jedi who has come here, after all, willing to abandon that decrepit order of yours.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «Is there something you need, human? Make it good, for I have little patience.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «I am sure there are plenty of others here who would answer your questions. I am quite busy. So if there is nothing else...?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «Who am I? You must not have been on Korriban for very long. Either that or you have been feeling your way about blindly. Luckily for you, I am in a charitable mood. I am Yuthura Ban, second only to Master Uthar of the Sith academy here in Dreshdae. I am the one who decides which few of the many hopefuls who travel here to train actually become a Sith. Why? Is it your desire to enter the academy? Is that why you are here?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «I am. Obviously you have been told of me. Is it your desire, then, to train at the academy? Do you wish to become a Sith, human?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «You again? What do you wish, now?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «[[../Sith Academy Entrance#Sith Medallion» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance] *(+2 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «So I see. How curious that you should come to possess such a thing. Where did you get it?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «Do not be insolent, human. I asked you a direct question, and I will not be trifled with. Now... where did you get this medallion?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «I see. How very enterprising of you, though that is not the normal way that we select our students for the academy.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «[Failure] You think to lie to me, do you? I would suspect you stole this and killed the owner. Very enterprising of you, I must admit.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «We Sith do encourage such independent thinking. You may even be a suitable candidate to join us. That is what you wish, I take it? To become a Sith?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «*chuckle* An interesting question, and not one I hear often. For most the benefits of becoming a Sith are quite obvious.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «And yet here you are on little Korriban, just by accident? I find it hard to believe. If it amuses you, however, I'll play along.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «We wield ultimate power, my friend. To be a Sith is to taste freedom and to know victory. Nothing is as glorious as bending the Force to your will.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «We make no apologies for the weak. If you cannot clench your fist and know when the moment comes to strike, there is no place for you amongst us. Of those who come to train, those who are weak return home. If they are both weak and foolish, they die... but it was their choice to come.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «Hide-bound relics who burden themselves with tradition and with the protection of the weak and ungrateful. They are pitiful and misguided. Why would you take a gift as glorious as the Force and squander it? Weaken yourself deliberately and shackle yourself to outdated mores? Our gift has made us superior. It is our rightful place to rule. How can any deny that? Yet the Jedi do so... and call us 'evil' because we do not.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «Is the sarkath beast who dominates his jungle evil? The tuk'ata who leaps on the squellbug for the kill? These are things of nature, of the universe. We are no different from this. The Force is part of the universe, part of the same laws. We were gifted to set us above the rest... to deny nature is foolish.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «It is the Jedi who serve the Force... trying to discern its wishes like frightened old men staring at the stars. The Force serves *us*. We shape it to our will. We are its master. The Jedi deny their darkness, but we revel in it, we allow it to fuel our strength.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «'Corruption' is a word the pitiful use to describe the natural longing for power that they deny. 'Evil' is a word trumpeted by the weak to ease their heart. It is true that the Force exacts a physical toll on some... but not a single one of them has ever regretted it. Anything else you hear is propaganda.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «You know the name? Malak is the strongest of us, and the strongest always rules... at least until one who is stronger can take it from him. That is our way: survival of the fittest. You are always on guard, always lean for the kill. We promote it, for through this the Sith are stronger.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «If a Sith has proven to be weak... and if the time is right. The Force rewards the cunning and the mighty. Step up to the challenge if you dare... or turn tail and run.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «You've heard enough, have you? *chuckle* So what is it to be? Are you here to be a Sith or not?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «Oh? That is truly a first. Do as you wish, then, human. I'll not stop you.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «No? Why might that be? There is something... odd about you, human, that I cannot place...» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «Ahhh, so you are just another hopeful after all. Or are you? There is something odd about you that I cannot place...» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «[Success] Oh? Very well. You do seem a fit candidate, though there is... something odd about you I cannot place...» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «*chuckle* As you wish. Play your little game, Jedi, if you must. Either way, I sense an intriguing amount of power within you.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «*chuckle* As you wish. Keep your little secrets, human, if you must. Either way, I sense an intriguing amount of power within you.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «As I thought. How interesting that they would let one with such power out of their grasp. Or are you here to spy on us? For all the good it would do.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «A Padawan, perhaps, though it makes little difference. I sense enough raw power within you to... well, we shall see.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «Perhaps none. Perhaps all the difference in the world. Either way, you possess enough raw power to intrigue me greatly.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «A pity. Here I hadn't even decided, yet.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «Well, human... should you change your mind, you know where to come.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «[Failure] *chuckle* You must not think much of Sith masters to try that little trick. I won't hold it against you, however... it shows initiative. But I do desire a real answer to my question... does it interest you?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «[Failure] I see. Somehow, however, I am unconvinced. No, my friend... I'll not be the one to bring you into our academy. If one of the other Sith accept you and give you a medallion, then perhaps. But otherwise... not today. Run along now.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «But I have already told you your answer, human. Do not bother me further, or I shall become irritated.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «[Success] Ah, good. Exactly the sort of answer I was hoping for.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Ah, good. Exactly the sort of answer I was hoping for.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «So one of the others accepted you, even after I did not? You are lucky, human, for I am in a forgiving mood.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «I have only one other question. These... companions of yours? They will not be coming with you, I presume?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «There is... something odd about this servant of yours... No matter. Make sure they don't disturb your training or cause trouble. You are responsible for them. Now... are you ready to go to the academy?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «I see. Fair enough, so long as they do not disturb your training or cause any trouble. Are you ready to go to the academy, then?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «Are you ready to go there now?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «You have come back. Good. Are you prepared to go to the academy?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «[[../Sith Academy» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «Then come back when you are ready. But do not take long... I *will* leave without you.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]**
- «So... you managed to kill Shaardan, did you? Good news travels quickly. Well done... he wouldn't have made a good Sith, anyway. One less opponent is an excellent result regardless of how it occurs, correct? Now, then... something you needed?» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]**
- «Indeed you have.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Nothing of course, my master. I was merely agreeing with you. Should we not get on with the test?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «I... I don't know what you're talking about, master. I am not... scheming. Should we not get on with the test?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «As should you, my master. But I was only agreeing with you. Should we not get on with the test?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Be very cautious, here. This tomb is like the others in this valley, and many of its old defenses remain active.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Go and retrieve the lightsaber. There is nothing we can do for you until then.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «The Force has served you well.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «What?! So this is what you had planned all along. You wish me killed.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «[[../Sith Academy#Double-Double-Cross» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow] *(+2 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «No, it is time for *you* to die, master. My pupil stands with me.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «[[../Sith Academy#Double-Cross» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow] *(+6 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Hmph. To think that I actually hoped you could be of use to me.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Betrayed! He/She *is* a spy!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You will pay for this, fool! We will destroy you!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «The poison! You will pay for this! We will destroy you!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «The poison! You will pay for this, you... you... damn you! My blood burns!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Do you hear that, my master? That is the sound of a new leader rising to replace you.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You are weak, Uthar, and the Force has abandoned you. We have made sure of it!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Uthar is finished, and a new order is brought to the academy. Excellent.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «...unghh! The pain! My blood is burning!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «D-damn you! You will never be a Sith, now! I... I will destroy you for this! You... you will not leave here alive!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Unfortunately, I don't need... I don't... ...unghh! The pain! I... why did you do this to me? I thought...» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «D-damn you! I... I will destroy you for this! You... you will never leave here alive!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Unfortunately for you, I don't intend to share power. It's time for you to die.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «As if I would ever believe that! Defend yourself, if you dare!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Then you should have no trouble defending yourself. For what it's worth.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Unghhh! Stop! I... I yield!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You are... too strong for me. Gather your hatred and strike me down... if that is your will.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «No, I don't suppose you can.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «I didn't think you were here to train. Not really. I should have realized that sooner.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «That alone makes you different. Most of the Sith here... would not think twice about it.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «That's... tempting, but I don't think I could. Your path... it goes somewhere I can't follow.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You are too powerful. You would never be my second for long... if the academy even truly interested you.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «I had no choice. You... reminded me too much of a time before I became a Sith. I... didn't want to think about that.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «[Success] I... suppose you could be right. All the things I wanted to do, all the wrongs I wanted to right... I haven't done any of it. They just get farther and farther from my mind. All I've cared about is power and... and myself. This isn't the person I was.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You're right. Maybe... maybe what I needed most was peace, after all. The Jedi tried to show me that. I don't know if I can ever go back to them... but my place isn't here. Thank you... thank you for showing me that.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You have a destiny, my friend, and I wish you well on your way to it. May the Force... be with you.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Hello. It is... good to see you again.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «I did. You told me I should rethink my path, and I did. You were right.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You are the one who suggested I rethink my path. And I have... and you were right.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «It's been very hard to let go of all those thoughts... about power and strength and victory. I don't know if I'll ever be completely free of them. The real irony is that, despite what the Sith Code says, I've never felt more free. I think I might... be able to find a little peace here. Finally.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «For now. They've been very welcoming... more than I expected. I met my old master. He... he cried when he saw me. I was embarrassed to see his weakness. Old habits die hard. But... it felt good, too. It felt good to know he missed me, that he worried about me. The Council said they would give me time to decide if I wanted to try again, if I was ready. I don't know. There's... there's so much that I've done.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Yes. Part of me just wants to forget it, to put it behind me... but that isn't right, either. I have to accept it and move on.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «I suppose I have. It's still tempting, all that power... but I've had my fill of it and it wasn't enough.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «I suppose I owe it to you, and to myself, to give the Jedi another chance. I'm not the foolish young girl I once was.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «It's not that easy for me. There's an... an ache inside me I just can't seem to quench.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «My old master said he would start exercises with me again... slowly, when I was ready. He said my experience with the dark side could make me a very wise Jedi. There is no emotion; there is peace. There is no ignorance; there is knowledge. There is no passion; there is serenity. There is no chaos; there is harmony. There is no death; there is the Force. Those words never held such resonance for me until I returned. I'd... like some serenity, some harmony. Wouldn't you?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Yes, I suppose your fate still has much in store for you, just as I saw back on Korriban.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «That's too bad. A little serenity might be useful considering the fate you have in store for you.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «I don't know. I doubt it. Even if I wanted to, the Sith would never take me back. To them, I am a traitor. But the dark side... that's a different matter. The dark side is not the Sith. I still feel its temptation strongly. My emotions are so strong. Just the other day I nearly attacked a poor Padawan for tripping into me, nearly berated his clumsiness and weakness. I didn't, thankfully, but it's difficult to fight off those feelings. How do you do it?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «And to think I took my advice to come here from you. Ha ha! Perhaps you should follow your own counsel sometime.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «That's the worst thing to hear... how hard it is. I suppose that's the honest answer. The dark side is the easier path for a reason.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You jest, but I think I know how you really feel.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «The one thing that made me come here was how hard I had to think to remember where I began and where Yuthura the Sith Master ended. That wasn't the person I wanted to be, so how did I end up there? It... it will take some time for me to sort through that, I think.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You know, I'd forgotten all about that until I spoke to you about it on Korriban. Strange, considering my hate for them is what made me leave the Jedi. I think... I think I've got to help myself, first, before I can help anyone else. Maybe once that happens, I can work with the Jedi to make a difference. They may work slowly, but they do make a difference, don't they?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «More than the Sith did. The Sith bring more misery than the slavers ever did... I don't know why I didn't see that before.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You believe that? I believe that they're better than the Sith. The Sith bring more misery than the slavers ever did. I don't know why I didn't see that before.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «I may not be able to change everything at once like I wanted to... but it would feel good to really help someone, for a change. Maybe that's a reason to change. Maybe that's a reason to stay. I... I guess I'll see.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «That's... very tempting. But I'm not ready to leave here, yet. And I don't belong on the path you have to take... I told you that on Korriban. It's still true. But... perhaps you could come back, afterwards. I could certainly use a friend, then.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «That's good. I look forward to that time. Perhaps we can travel together, then.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «I know. I can... see that about you. I'm not sure that we will meet again. Still... I can hope that is the case.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Oh. Well, that's too bad. I'm still grateful for what you've done for me, regardless.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Then may the Force be with you, my friend. I hope we will see each other again.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Good luck, my friend. May the Force be with you.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «[Failure] N-no... no, it's too late for me. Please... either let me go, or end it now.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Very well. I... won't stop you, or mention what you've done here.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You have a destiny to fulfill, and that does not involve you joining the Sith... at least not yet. I won't keep you from it. May the Force serve you well.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «As I thought. You are no Sith. A real Sith would have no hesitation in striking me down.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Then I hope you got what you came to the academy for, whatever that was.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Mercy? You truly don't belong here, then. I hope you got whatever it was you came to the academy for.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «I... was right the first time, back at the cantina, wasn't I? There is something strange about you... more so than I even suspected. Your path leads elsewhere, to a destiny I can only guess at. Whatever it is does not involve joining the Sith... not yet, at least.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Go, then. Go and leave here... I'll not stop you.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Then it ends here.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «I... I see. Then let us end this here.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «I... understand. May the Force serve you well.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «You fool! You have no idea what you've done!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «The only 'reward' you will get is death!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- «Your destiny does not lie with the Sith... at least, not yet. The academy is mine, now... I trust you won't be remaining long, correct?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]**
- «You killed Mekel, I hear? Well... I suppose it is just as well. He was far too devious to be allowed to live. One less opponent is an excellent result regardless of how it occurs, correct? Now, then... something you needed?» [K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]

**[K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**
- «I understand you managed to kill Lashowe. Well done! She was devious, but I knew her trust could be turned against her! One less opponent is an excellent result regardless of how it occurs, correct? Now, then... something you needed?» [K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]

### Quote bank B: what others say about Yuthura Ban (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K1/Dreshdae]**
- Czerka Representative: «Only prospective students. We get many who come here each year, hoping to get picked. Many are simply considered too weak, I'm afraid. I understand there is one main person amongst the Sith who decides which students are worthy... Yuthura Ban, I believe her name is.» [K1/Dreshdae]
- Prospective Sith: «Well... you have to impress one of the Sith. The final decision rests with Yuthura Ban, however. She's the one who decides exactly which of us get into the academy or not. I've been hanging out hoping she'll come by and notice me, to be honest.» [K1/Dreshdae]
- Prospective Sith: «*sigh* I wish I knew, to tell you the truth. You have to impress one of the Sith, I guess. Either way, it's Yuthura Ban who makes the final decision. She's a Twi'lek Sith master, I hear. Very high rank. She's the one who decides which hopefuls make it and which ones don't.» [K1/Dreshdae]
- Mika Dorin: «[[../Tomb of Naga Sadow#Side with Yuthura» [K1/Dreshdae]

**[K1/Sith_Academy]**
- Uthar Wynn: «Greetings, prospective students. It appears we have a late entry. Who do you bring before me, Yuthura? A young human, bristling with the Force?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «Now, then. All of you five recruits have shown a degree of facility with the Force... you all have the potential to become true Sith. Only one of you, however, will succeed. The one who succeeds will be admitted to the academy as a full Sith. All others must wait until next year and try again... if you survive. My pupil, Yuthura, shall be your teacher and master while you attempt to prove yourselves. Heed her words.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «Really, however, you should seek out Yuthura and get her advice. She is your trainer, after all, yes?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «I see. It is good that you have come to me with this information, young one. It is a bit... ironic... that Yuthura has begun her plotting. I have been aware of her growing ambitions for some time, and had in fact already decided to remove her. Normally, the one who gains the most prestige would engage in a final trial... two of the students would fight. This time it will be Yuthura who battles, though she does not know it. Perhaps it will be you who combats her? Yes... perhaps so. This is what you can do. Give this pad to Adrenas... he will put some poison in her bath. This will weaken Yuthura for that final test, making her an easy target. Rather generous of me, don't you think?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «Do not forget our agreement regarding Yuthura's treachery. We shall put her in her place... should you reach the final test, that is.» [K1/Sith_Academy]

**[K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]**
- Sith Academy Guard: «You must be admitted to the academy. That decision must be made by a Sith who has already been accepted, here. The final decision, however, remains with Master Yuthura. I believe she is currently at the cantina if you wish to seek her out. Now go.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Sith Academy Guard: «It is the device given to one who has been accepted into the academy, but has not yet proved their worth as a student. If one of the Sith decides you are worthy, you will be given one... which you must then take to Yuthura Ban, who I believe is in the cantina. It is she who decides which hopefuls enter the academy.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Sith Academy Guard: «You will want to bring your medallion to Yuthura Ban. She is the one who brings all selected students into the academy for their training. I believe she can be located within the cantina here in the colony. I suggest you go to her while she is still there, student.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Sith Academy Guard: «You again? I told you to locate Yuthura Ban in the cantina. She is the only one who can bring new students into the academy.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Prospective Sith: «What does that mean? It means that you've been accepted into the academy. All you have to do is bring that to Yuthura Ban in the cantina. Didn't the Sith who gave you that tell you?» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- Prospective Sith: «Why would you ask that? The Sith who gave you that must have mentioned that it represented your acceptance. All you have to do is bring it to Yuthura Ban at the cantina and you're in!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]**
- Message: «[A day passes in preparation. Uthar then takes you and Yuthura to the Sith tomb. Your companions are not permitted to join you.]» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «I dislike your tone, Yuthura. What are you up to, now?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «Good. Yuthura and I will await your return.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «You must locate the ceremonial lightsaber and return here to us. Yuthura and I cannot help you in this, young one.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «Good. The last part of your test will now commence. Here you will learn the lesson of competition. All life must compete in order to flourish. Such is the natural way of the universe. To stand still is to know death... one must always be moving forward. So is it the same amongst the Sith. Compete or die. Mercy is irrelevant. So it behooves you in this final test to strike down one you are familiar with, for no other reason than to prove you are superior and without mercy. Normally this would be against another student. You, however, get a special treat. You will fight Yuthura, here... my own apprentice.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «Indeed. You have become too ambitious, Yuthura. It is time for you to die and someone more... talented... to take your place.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «So... you know, do you? Well, it makes no difference. You have become too ambitious, Yuthura... it is time for you to die.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «Oh? Is this true, young one? You wish to join Yuthura in her foolish scheme?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «Sadly, Yuthura, it is you who are mistaken. Your pupil is more ambitious than you realize... isn't that so, young one?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «I must say that it is quite disappointing that you must die now, as well, seeing as you didn't inform me earlier of Yuthura's plans.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «I must say that it is quite disappointing that you must die now, as well, since there's no telling how far your scheming with Yuthura went.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «Destroy me? I am not Yuthura, young one. I am a Sith Master... and I tire of foolish upstarts.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Sith Duelist: «[Success] Hmmm... I knew something was up when Yuthura showed up and claimed control of the academy. Uthar must be planning a fight.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Sith Duelist: «[Failure] Don't be foolish. If Master Yuthura wants to send an order like that, she can bloody well tell me herself.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]

### Quote bank D: lines addressed to Yuthura Ban (Heuristic: lines in second person spoken directly before or after one of this character's lines on the same page)

**[K1/Hangar]**
- Uthar Wynn: «That I'll judge for myself, thank you. Tell me, human... what do you know of the ways of the Sith? What preconceptions has your mind been polluted with?» [K1/Hangar]
- Spirit of Ajunta Pall: «[Success] I do... and yet I do not. The Force is so strong with you, human... yet the face, the soul... it has been so very long...» [K1/Hangar]
- Uthar Wynn: «Excellent work, my young Sith. Your enemy lies dead at your feet and you are victorious... the final test is complete.» [K1/Hangar]

**[K1/Sith_Academy]**
- Uthar Wynn: «If you wish to gain a lead over your competitors, the first of you to learn the Code of the Sith and tell me of it will be rewarded. The rest is for you to discover. Welcome to the dark side, my children... your one chance at true greatness lies here.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «You have impressed me enough, by my estimation, to become a Sith in full. Congratulations, young one... you have bested the others quite completely. You have but one final test which you must take, and this requires us to travel to the tomb of Naga Sadow in the Valley of the Dark Lords. I would advise you to be rested and equipped before we leave. Return to your quarters now and seek me out in the morning. When you return, make sure that you have all that you will need... for you will face your test alone. Go, and may the Force serve you well.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Jolee: «You don't say? Well, the master loves tombs, don't you master?» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Jolee: «You sound very much like... someone I used to know.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «He's... one of the students? Yes, I've met the sniveler. I think I know where you're going with this... good, yes, I accept.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Uthar Wynn: «Ahhhh. I think I know this one you speak of. You gained his confidence to admit such, did you? I commend your competitive spirit... you have certainly impressed me. I shall speak to the boy straight away.» [K1/Sith_Academy]
- Lashowe: «You? I remember you from the colony. I can't believe you actually made it into the academy.» [K1/Sith_Academy]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]**
- Uthar Wynn: «Well done! You have impressed me greatly!» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]**
- Uthar Wynn: «Very well. We are now ready for your final test, young Sith. You have earned the right to see if you shall become one of us.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «Yes. Yes, of course. We are in the sacred tomb of Naga Sadow, young one, the one discovered by Darth Malak and Darth Revan years ago. You are to follow in their footsteps and reach the ancient Star Map that lies deep within. There you will find a lightsaber, amongst other things. The lightsaber is for you: your initiation present. Return to us once you have it, for you the test does not end there.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «Do you understand what I have told you? Are you ready to begin?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «So you return to us with the lightsaber in hand, as I knew you would.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «You took a great risk in acquiring the artifact. You used your mind and your power... no peaceful meditation, no pacifism. Sometimes you must fight in order to achieve. This gives you your passion, this makes you stronger. This is what makes you superior. That is the lesson we teach with that part of the final test. Do you understand?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «You are indeed a fool if you think to oppose us both, young one.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «This makes no sense. You will never become a Sith if you turn on us both! What could you hope to accomplish?» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «So the time has come, has it? You both wish to stand against a Sith Master and perish, do you? THEN SO BE IT!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «It appears, my dear, that some tests are fairer than others. I wish you luck.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]
- Uthar Wynn: «Your destiny lies elsewhere, young one, not with the Sith... at least, not yet. Go... and return another time, if you still wish to.» [K1/Tomb_of_Naga_Sadow]

**[K1/Tomb_of_Tulak_Hord]**

**[K1/Valley_of_Dark_Lords]**

## Calo Nord
*Corpus coverage: 18 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 22 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 2 pronoun references resolved from context · 6 lines addressed to them · 10 narration lines · 4 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Calo is a top bounty hunter: «nobody gets away from Calo Nord in the end!» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge].
### Personality and motivations
Pride: «I've got a rep to protect!» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge].
### Arc (scene by scene)

## Admiral Saul Karath
*Corpus coverage: 66 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 86 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 6 pronoun references resolved from context · 12 lines addressed to them · 6 narration lines · 4 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Karath is the Sith fleet admiral and Carth's mentor: «Admiral Saul Karath is the commander of the entire Sith fleet.» [K1/Carth].
### Personality and motivations
Opportunist.
### Arc (scene by scene)
- Taris bombardment.
- Interrogates the party on the Leviathan.
- Dies on the bridge to Carth and the party, revealing Revan's identity to Carth [K1/Bridge].

- Malak.
- Carth.
### Abilities
- Non-Force; a strategist.
### Feats
- Destroyed Telos and Taris.

- None.

### Verdict implications
- None.

- None.
