### Quote bank A: Admiral Saul Karath's own lines, by scene (page)

**[K1/Bridge]**
- (Admiral Karath) «Very resourceful. I assume you had some part in this; you learned your lessons well from me.» [K1/Bridge]
- (Admiral Karath) «Don't be a fool. I am giving you and your companions a chance to surrender. A chance to live. Darth Malak himself is on his way, he will be arriving any moment.» [K1/Bridge]
- (Admiral Karath) «Malak will destroy you, but if you throw down your weapons now I will ask my Master to be merciful.» [K1/Bridge]
- (Admiral Karath) «You always did like to do things the hard way. Lord Malak would have preferred live prisoners, but corpses will have to do.» [K1/Bridge]
- (Admiral Karath) «Carth... Carth....» [K1/Bridge]
- (Admiral Karath) «Carth... *cough*... must tell you... *cough* must tell you something... *cough, cough*... come closer.... [Unintelligible Whisper] You didn't know, did you? *cough* Ha-ha-ha. Remember my dying words. *cough* Remember them whenever *cough*... whenever you look at those you thought were your friends!» [K1/Bridge]

**[K1/Davik's_Estate]**
- (Admiral Saul Karath) «You summoned me, Lord Malak?» [K1/Davik's_Estate]
- (Admiral Saul Karath) «Th... the entire planet, Lord Malak? But... there are billions of people on Taris! We'd be slaughtering countless innocent civilians. Not to mention our own men still on the surface.» [K1/Davik's_Estate]
- (Admiral Saul Karath) «Of... of course not my Lord Malak. I will do as you command. But it will take several hours to position our fleet.» [K1/Davik's_Estate]
- (Admiral Saul Karath) «Yes, Lord Malak.» [K1/Davik's_Estate]
- (Admiral Saul Karath) «Taris is defenceless against our assault, Lord Malak. They are offering no resistance. The city is in ruins.» [K1/Davik's_Estate]

**[K1/Leviathan]**
- «Carth, it has been far too long since we last spoke. I see the recent months have not been kind in your case. I barely recognized you.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «Did you learn nothing in your time under me? As a soldier you should understand that casualties were unavoidable. This was an act of war.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «In war even the innocent must die. The Sith would not accept me until I proved I had truly turned my back on the Republic by bombing the planet.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «You used to be a man of action, not of empty words. Cling to your lust for revenge if you must, but spare me your tired threats. I've heard them all before. You are an insignificant part of these events, anyway. Lord Malak is far more interested in your Jedi companions. He has great plans for them.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «Your words are brave, Bastila, but the lure of the dark side is hard to resist - or so I've been told. I wonder if your companion is as devoted to the light as you are?» [K1/Leviathan]
- «You're defiant. I'm certain Malak will find your loyalty to the Jedi amusing.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «Your loyalty is as fickle as ever, I see. Malak will find that amusing, though I seriously doubt he will want you of all people at his side.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «The Dark Lord would probably reward me if I just killed you once and for all. But he may want to question you given the trouble you've caused him... and the history between you.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «You mean... oh, this can't be true, can it? You really don't know what's going on here, do you? Well, I won't be the one to deprive Malak of the pleasure of telling you himself.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «The Dark Lord will no doubt torture you for information and for his own twisted pleasure. Eventually you will tell him everything. The Sith can be very persuasive. However, Lord Malak is in another sector. It may be some time before he arrives, so I suppose I will have to fill in for him until then. Activate the torture fields.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «Enough! I don't want them to pass out before I question them. Malak will appreciate any information I can give him when he arrives.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «I'm sure you won't. However, we both know your friend's loyalties have proven in the past to be somewhat... flexible.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «I am interrogating you, not the other way around. You will answer questions, not ask them.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «It is time to put your loyalty to the test. I doubt torturing you will gain me your true cooperation. Your will is too strong to be broken that way. However, even the strongest of heroes has trouble watching those they care about suffering.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «The interrogation will begin now. Each time you refuse to answer or give me a false answer, Bastila will suffer.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «The interrogation will begin now. Each time you refuse to answer or give me a false answer, Carth will suffer.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «You expect me to fall for such a transparent ploy. You travel half way across the galaxy with someone and you expect me to believe you feel nothing for each other?» [K1/Leviathan]
- «I tire of these games - now I want answers! On what planet is the Jedi Academy at which you were trained?» [K1/Leviathan]
- «[Failure] Alderaan is nothing but a planet of artisans and philosophers! There is no training academy there! You must think this is a game.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «Very well. This is the price of your resistance.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «Enough! You see what happens when you try to defy me?» [K1/Leviathan]
- «An excellent beginning. I am glad you are being reasonable.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «This first question was a test. Obviously Malak knew the Academy was on Dantooine, and it has since been destroyed by our fleet! Dantooine is an empty graveyard now. Nothing remains but a smoking ruin and the charred remains of your former Masters!» [K1/Leviathan]
- «It doesn't matter whether you believe me or not. The fact remains that the Jedi on Dantooine have been eradicated, along with any hope of someone coming to rescue you!» [K1/Leviathan]
- «More empty threats. We Sith prefer to let our actions speak for us. Perhaps that is why we are winning this war.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «A brave front, but your feigned indifference does not fool me. Your masters are eradicated, along with any hope of someone rescuing you!» [K1/Leviathan]
- «Now... tell me your mission. How were the Jedi planning on using you to stop Lord Malak and our Sith armada?» [K1/Leviathan]
- «[Failure] Do you take me for a fool? The Jedi are not assassins - they would never devise such a plan!» [K1/Leviathan]
- «Perhaps you need a reminder of the consequences of refusing to cooperate.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «Listen, can you not hear her suffering? You can spare her further pain by simply answering my questions.» [K1/Leviathan] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Now, I will ask again - on what mission did the Jedi Council send you?» [K1/Leviathan]
- «You should be grateful, Bastila. He is sparing you agony beyond comprehension.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «You should be grateful, Carth. She is sparing you agony beyond comprehension.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «Tell me, how did you find out about the Star Forge?» [K1/Leviathan]
- «[Failure] A diary? This is not the time for jokes! I will only ask you one more time: How did you find out about the Star Forge?» [K1/Leviathan]
- «Don't listen to her. Tell me what I want to know or watch Bastila suffer!» [K1/Leviathan]
- «Don't listen to him! Tell me what I want to know or watch Carth suffer!» [K1/Leviathan]
- «I find your willingness to cooperate pleasing, though it seems your answers are quite upsetting to Bastila. Not to worry - we are almost finished here.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «I find your willingness to cooperate pleasing, though it seems your answers are quite upsetting to Carth. Not to worry, we are almost finished here.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «The information you have given me has been most useful, but I fear our session is over. Lord Malak will want to continue with this line of questioning himself.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «I had hoped you would continue to cooperate, but you seem to have changed your position.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «Perhaps another lesson is in order?» [K1/Leviathan]
- «I am surprised she did not pass out sooner. Rarely have I seen someone withstand such punishment and remain conscious.» [K1/Leviathan] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «I see I am wasting my time here. When Malak arrives you will learn my interrogation techniques are considered merciful among the Sith.» [K1/Leviathan]
- «I will leave you here in your cell with a small taste of the horrors you will suffer when Lord Malak arrives.» [K1/Leviathan]

**[K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]**
- (Admiral Saul Karath) «Lord Malak, the Star Forge is operating at 200% capacity – far beyond our expectations.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- (Admiral Saul Karath) «She was aided by Carth Onasi, a decorated war hero of the Republic and a legendary soldier. During the Mandalore Wars he was honored many times for his bravery.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- (Admiral Saul Karath) «Yes, Lord Malak. He served under me when I still followed the Republic. You could say I was his mentor.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- (Admiral Saul Karath) «An eye witness, Lord Malak.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- (Admiral Saul Karath) «Calo Nord, a bounty hunter, was there when Bastila and Carth escaped the planet. Apparently they left him for dead.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- (Admiral Saul Karath) «Calo has agreed to help us capture the young Bastila – for a very hefty fee, of course. But I assure you he is well worth the price. His reputation as a bounty hunter is well earned.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- (Admiral Saul Karath) «Lord Malak, forgive me. There is something else. May we have a private audience away from the ears of the common soldiers?» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- (Admiral Saul Karath) «I promise you will be very interested in what Calo has to tell you about Bastila's other companion, Lord Malak.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- (Admiral Saul Karath) «Calo Nord is dead, Lord Malak. He has failed in his mission. Forgive me.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- (Admiral Saul Karath) «Shall I hire another bounty hunter, Lord Malak?» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]

### Quote bank B: what others say about Admiral Saul Karath (by name, alias or epithet), by scene

**[K1/Bridge]**
- Carth: «The only thing you taught me was betrayal and death, Saul.» [K1/Bridge]
- Bastila: «The Admiral, he's still alive!» [K1/Bridge]
- Carth: «He's gone. He said... it can't be true, can it? No. No... no - it can't! Damn you, Saul! Damn you!» [K1/Bridge]

**[K1/Carth]**
- Carth: «When I think of all the men who have betrayed us, the one that stands out above them all is the one I respected the most. Saul.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «Admiral Saul Karath is the commander of the entire Sith fleet. He's half the reason Malak has done so well in the war. Saul was my commanding officer back when the Mandalorian Wars first began. He taught me everything about being a soldier... and I looked up to him. Saul approached me before he left. He talked to me about how the Republic was on the losing side... and about how I should start thinking of my survival. I know now that he was trying to recruit me into the Sith, but I couldn't have conceived of it back then. I argued with him and he got angry and he left. I never saw him again.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «Saul was my mentor... he led us to so many victories against the Mandalorians, even when things looked to be at their worst. I just... I couldn't conceive of it. He... he couldn't be serious. I was wrong, of course... he not only left us for the Sith, he... he gave them the codes to bypass our scanners. I remember waking up as the first of the Sith bombers snuck past our defenses and began destroying half of our docked ships. I knew right away what had happened. I... could have stopped him. I could have stopped it all.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «I blame Saul, not myself. I was... I was stupid and I ignored the danger. He nearly destroyed us all.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «Don't even start with that! I don't feel sorry for myself. I feel angry... at Saul and all those others. It's all I can think about sometimes!» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «I've fought Saul for years, now, and if I ever catch up to him... he will regret what he's done. He will regret it.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «Look, I didn't mean it that way. I want to get to Saul, not... no... no, forget it. It seems all I can do is insult you, isn't it? Just forget I said anything. Let's... let's... just get on with what we were doing.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «I, ahhh... I'm not very good at this. I... I know I owe you an apology. Uh, more than one, probably. I was just so desperate to finally face Saul directly in the battle over Taris, and now the Jedi have us looking for these... these Star Maps. I know this mission is important, it's just... I feel a bit useless. I can fight, sure, but I'm no Jedi... all this feels completely out of my league.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «It's just that I... don't talk about it very much. Ok? I told you about my homeworld. Telos. Four years ago, Saul led the Sith fleet there and demanded its surrender. The planet refused and Saul proceeded to devastate its entire surface. Millions died. I had... a wife and son on Telos. I thought they would be safe there. But my task force arrived too late to be of much help. We didn't have enough medical supplies. The colony was burning and the dying were everywhere. I remember holding my wife and screaming for the medics. They... didn't come in time.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «Of course not. How could you? I... had nothing left after that, really. I devoted myself to the fleet. Hunting Saul was my only purpose.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «I had... had nothing left after that, really. I devoted myself to the fleet. Hunting Saul was my only purpose.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «I... miss them. I know killing Saul won't bring them back, and it won't make me happy again... but I have to do it. I don't expect you to understand. I have to pay him back for what he's done... I have to. It's all I have left.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «Gotten over it? Look - my family was everything to me. Once they were gone... I had nothing. Hunting Saul became my only purpose.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «Yes. I can't forgive Saul for this. He took everything from me, even... even my trust. I hope you can understand that.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «She isn't a ghost to me. I still remember holding her as she died... and I doubt I'll forget until I've paid Saul back in kind. That's all I have left: finding Saul and killing the scum. Don't worry... I won't waste any more time until then.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «The only thing that's kept me going since she died has been the need to find Saul and kill him. It's better to think about that than... anything else. If I can do that, then maybe I can let her go. Let it all go. If we encounter Saul... if we ever have the chance, promise me that I will be the one to kill him. I have to settle this... I need to.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «I... never thought about it. I suppose I always assumed that I would be dead once Saul was.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «Understand that whenever I envisioned taking on Saul in the past, it was always as the captain of my own ship... or alone. In those cases I would risk everything.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «That's never occurred to me. I've always been the captain of my own ship, after all... or thought about going after Saul on my own.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «I would like to think that I wouldn't put you in danger. That I wouldn't forget that there's more behind Saul that needs to be stopped.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «If I saw Saul, however... if I had the chance... I don't know what I would do. I really don't. I mean his death has been my entire focus for so long.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «If, uh... if I'm going to find some purpose beyond taking revenge on Saul, then it's going to have to be in protecting you. I don't know why, but I think some terrible fate is waiting for you. I think the Jedi Council knows it, too. And I don't want that to come to pass.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «If I'm going to live past Saul, I need you to, as well. Let me protect you... from yourself, from the Sith... you have to let me try.» [K1/Carth]
- Carth: «Because... because I never got the chance to save my wife and son. Because I didn't stop Saul when I had the chance. Because I finally have the chance to do it right. You are an extraordinary woman... you make me think that maybe I might have some purpose beyond revenge. I don't know whether it means anything to you... but it does to me.» [K1/Carth]

**[K1/Command_Center]**
- Bastila: «The Star Forge is under assault from the entire Republic fleet! If I don't use my Battle Meditation to turn the tide, Admiral Dodonna's capital ships will destroy the Star Forge. I have to stay here in the command center so that I can best use my Battle Meditation against our enemy. If I don't the Star Forge will be lost! You must go to face Malak without me. Find him, kill him and reclaim the title of Dark Lord of the Sith!» [K1/Command_Center]
- Vandar: «Not anymore, Admiral. I sensed something different about Bastila during her transmission, but I thought I was only feeling the evil presence of the Star Forge itself. I see now I was wrong. Bastila has turned to the dark side, Admiral. You have to give the order to retreat!» [K1/Command_Center]

**[K1/Davik's_Estate]**
- Darth Malak: «Your predecessor once made the mistake of questioning my orders, Admiral. Surely you are not so foolish as to make the same mistake?» [K1/Davik's_Estate]
- Darth Malak: «Then I suggest you begin immediately. You are dismissed, Admiral.» [K1/Davik's_Estate]

**[K1/Hangar]**
- Carth: «I got the revenge I always wanted when Saul died, but it hasn't brought me the peace that I thought it would.» [K1/Hangar]
- Carth: «No, it's no joke. The Jedi Council captured Revan and erased the Dark Lord's mind, programming in a new identity. Saul Karath told me on the Leviathan and Bastila confirmed it!» [K1/Hangar]

**[K1/Leviathan]**
- Carth: «It's the Leviathan. Saul Karath's vessel. My old mentor.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Carth: «Admiral Karath taught me everything I know about being a soldier. He was a legend in the Republic fleet, and a hero to me. Until he betrayed us. When the Sith attacked my home world, the Leviathan - which is Saul Karath's flagship - was at the head of the fleet. My family was destroyed that day and my wife died in the Sith bombardment.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Carth: «I'm not going to do anything stupid. I won't throw our lives away in some mad quest for vengeance. But if I get a chance to kill Saul during our escape, nobody better get in my way.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Carth: «I'll admit it won't be easy. Saul's no fool and he won't underestimate us either. You can count on plenty of guards watching every move we make.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Bastila: «Maybe Admiral Karath doesn't know how many of us there are on board. We all have special talents; talents we could exploit so that one of us could stage a rescue. We just have to figure out who has the best chance to avoid capture so that they can come and rescue us later. It's a long shot, but it's our only hope.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Sith Commander: «Excellent. Have you searched the ship thoroughly? Admiral Karath warned me to be on alert for any kind of treachery.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Sith Trooper: «She tried to bite me through my armor! And you should hear what she said about my mother. Admiral Karath needs to teach her the proper respect for the Sith!» [K1/Leviathan]
- Sith Commander: «Admiral Karath wants us to question all the prisoners. Did you get any information from him?» [K1/Leviathan]
- Sith Commander: «The Admiral doesn't have time to bother with some Twi'lek girl! Take her away to solitary confinement. I'll leave it up to you to teach her the proper respect for the Sith!» [K1/Leviathan]
- Sith Commander: «Dump the Mandalorian in the medical bay. Let them take the blame if he dies before the Admiral has a chance to interrogate him.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Sith Commander: «Very well. The Admiral is probably too busy to bother with this old man anyway. Take him to solitary confinement for interrogation. Report back to me if you learn anything.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Sith Commander: «Well done. Return to your posts, and I will tell the Admiral of this.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Carth: «But I recognized you, Saul. I see your face every night even as I promise myself I will kill you for what you did to my home world.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Carth: «My wife died in that attack, Saul. And for that, I swear I'll kill you.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Bastila: «We will never serve Malak or the dark side! The Sith will be destroyed, Admiral Karath... as will you if you don't turn away from this path.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Bastila: «Don't waste your breath, Saul! We won't answer any of your questions.» [K1/Leviathan] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Bastila: «Don't try to move too quickly, you might not be fully recovered yet. Admiral Karath had his guards continue to torture you even after you passed out.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Carth: «They tortured all of us, though you got the worst of it by far. Saul wanted them to make us suffer. He's become some sort of sadistic monster.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Bastila: «This is not a matter to joke about! If there is one thing we can learn from Saul it is how the power of the dark side can corrupt even the bravest of heroes! I'm sorry. Forgive me. Snapping at you like that won't help our situation.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Bastila: «I'd like to believe that Saul was lying to us, but even as he said the words I knew they were true. The Academy is gone.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Carth: «None of this will matter if we don't get out of this prison before Saul gets back!» [K1/Leviathan]
- Carth: «Saul mentioned that Lord Malak was on his way. I think the Admiral left to prepare for his arrival... and to report the results of our interrogation.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Bastila: «It is fortunate you were able to resist the Admiral's questioning. The fate of the galaxy could be changed by revealing the slightest piece of vital information.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Bastila: «I only wish you had been able to resist the Admiral's questioning. I hope the information you revealed does not bring the entire galaxy under the dominion of the Sith.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Bastila: «Your efforts were well intentioned but poorly thought out; Saul tortured us all anyway. There is too much at stake to let reckless passion rule our actions.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Carth: «Don't be so hard on yourself. I've known the Admiral a long time. I could see that he already knew the answers to the questions he was asking.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Carth: «Stop it, both of you! Bastila, you... you can't honestly say how you would have reacted in that situation - nobody can! Besides, Saul already had all the information. I could tell.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Carth: «Forget about it, Bastila. It doesn't matter anyway. I've known the Admiral a long time. I could see that he already knew the answers to the questions he was asking.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Carth: «I've known Admiral Karath a long time and I think you're right.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Carth: «The interrogation was a sham. Saul was toying with us. He didn't care what we told him. I think it was just an excuse to torture us before Malak arrived.» [K1/Leviathan]
- Bastila: «Did you feel that? A disturbance in the Force. The Admiral has sent his message, the Dark Lord knows we are here now. Malak is coming.» [K1/Leviathan]

**[K1/Prison_Block]**
- Jolee: «You shouldn't have let me out, sonny. That was wrong. Admiral Karath won't be too happy with you disobeying his orders.» [K1/Prison_Block]
- Carth: «Count me in, then. I've got a score to settle with the Admiral before we get off this ship, and I have a feeling I'm gonna find him on the Leviathan's bridge!» [K1/Prison_Block]
- Bastila: «That's true, Carth. You can come, but don't let your hatred of Saul Karath jeopardize our true mission: getting the Ebon Hawk safely off the Leviathan.» [K1/Prison_Block]

**[K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]**
- Darth Malak: «Interesting. How did you acquire this information, Admiral?» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Darth Malak: «I trust you are not wasting my time, Admiral Karath.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]
- Darth Malak: «The penalty for failure is death, Admiral Karath. But the failure was Calo's, not yours. You may rise.» [K1/Quest_for_the_Star_Forge]

**[K1/Star_Forge]**
- Admiral Dodonna: «This is Admiral Forn Dodonna to the Ebon Hawk, do you read us?» [K1/Star_Forge] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- Carth: «Admiral Dodonna, this is Carth Onasi. We are receiving your transmission.» [K1/Star_Forge]
- Carth: «The Star Forge wasn't constructed by the Sith, Admiral. We don't have time for me to fully explain it, but that space station is far older than you can imagine.» [K1/Star_Forge]
- Carth: «You can't do that, Admiral. The Star Forge is a factory of immense power. It's been churning out the capital ships, snub fighters and assault droids that have powered the Sith war effort. You have to destroy the Star Forge now or you'll be fighting an unending wave of reinforcements.» [K1/Star_Forge]
- Carth: «It's because of Bastila, Admiral. She turned to the dark side and become Malak's apprentice. We suspect she's somewhere on that space station right now, using her Battle Meditation against you and your fleet.» [K1/Star_Forge]
- Carth: «Don't worry, Admiral. The Ebon Hawk and her crew are going to see this through to the end!» [K1/Star_Forge]
- Bastila: «Admiral Dodonna, this is Bastila Shan of the Jedi Order. We are receiving your transmission.» [K1/Star_Forge]
- Bastila: «Don't pull back, Admiral. You have to attack the Star Forge now, otherwise the Sith will use its power to destroy the Republic.» [K1/Star_Forge]
- Vandar: «Bastila's power could help you win this battle, Admiral, but it will mean nothing if Malak escapes. Destroying the Star Forge is secondary to stopping the Sith threat once and for all.» [K1/Star_Forge]

**[K1/Star_Forge_System]**
- Carth: «The Star Forge... I've never seen anything like it. I'm transmitting these coordinates to Admiral Dodonna.  Maybe a quick strike by the Republic can cripple the Sith fleet. Message is away. Now we can just wait for the Republic to show up. We should be safe here - we're outside their sensor range.» [K1/Star_Forge_System]
- Carth: «I got the revenge I always wanted when Saul died, but it hasn't brought me the peace that I thought it would. All I can think of now is the promise I made to protect you from what's going to come. It's given me a reason to look past simple revenge.» [K1/Star_Forge_System]

**[K1/Temple_Summit]**
- Carth: «You don't have to help the Sith, Mission. Not while I'm here. I see now it was a mistake to let you go into that Temple. I of all people should have seen this coming. First Saul Karath, and now you... I mean, I should be an expert on betrayal by now! But nothing you can say or do can make me betray the Republic. I won't join you, and I won't just stand aside and let you become ruler of the Sith again, Revan!» [K1/Temple_Summit]

**[K1/Viewing_Platform]**
- Republic Officer: «Admiral Dodonna - we've hit their main orbital stabilizers!» [K1/Viewing_Platform]
- Vandar: «But at what cost, Admiral? Where is the Ebon Hawk and her crew?» [K1/Viewing_Platform]
- Carth: «We couldn't let you start the victory party without us, Admiral!» [K1/Viewing_Platform]
- Vandar: «It is too late to retreat, Admiral. The Sith armada has cut us off. There is no escape for us now.» [K1/Viewing_Platform]

## Ajunta Pall
*Corpus coverage: 47 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 14 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 2 pronoun references resolved from context · 5 lines addressed to them · 7 narration lines · 4 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
Ajunta Pall is the spirit of one of the first Sith Lords, trapped in his tomb: «We were the first. The first to rebel, to betray...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall].
### Personality and motivations
Regret.
### Arc (scene by scene)
- Asks the player about his sword. Resolutions include letting him go or fighting.
### Relationships
- None.
### Abilities
- Ghostly.
### Feats
- Lore only.
### Power-scaling statements
- «We destroyed each other.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
### Duels and fights
- A possible fight with his spirit.
### Verdict implications
- History only.
### Open questions
- None.

### Abilities: game mechanics (FLAGGED: gameplay stats, not lore)
- [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall] Spirit of Ajunta Pall: Class Jedi Consular, Level 18, Vitality 219, Force 1244, Defense 24. Powers: Drain Life, FR=28, Force Storm, Force Whirlwind, Horror. Feats: DC=21, Master Sense.

- None found on the RCM-tagged lines for this character.

### Quote bank A: Ajunta Pall's own lines, by scene (page)

**[K1/Hangar]**
- «A... Jedi? Here? Why have you come to this dark place, Jedi? Why... disturb my sleepless rest?» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Do... I know you? It... seems as if we have met before...» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «[Success] I do... and yet I do not. The Force is so strong with you, human... yet the face, the soul... it has been so very long...» [K1/Hangar] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*

**[K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]**
- «Too long... too long in the cold and the dark. I am disturbed again? A human...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Are you certain? You... the Force is with you. So strong, so bright. You remind me of... someone I once knew, so long ago...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «I... had a name, once. Ajunta Pall. Yes, that was my name. I was one of many. We were servants of the dark side... Sith Lords, we called ourselves. So proud. In the end we were not so proud. We hid... hid from those we had betrayed. We fell... and I knew it would be so...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Ancient? Has it been so long that you use the word 'ancient'? I have... been here so long, so lost, I cannot... cannot remember... We were the first. The first to rebel, to betray... to surrender ourselves to the dark side. So strong, we thought... so wrong...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Our Jedi Masters. Those who had taught us to use the Force... who warned us against the dark side. Yet we embraced it in secret, reveled in its power. We were... discovered? Or did we act? I can no longer remember. But here is where we came... to hide, to grow. And here we fell.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Our temple... our tomb. Built far from our enemies. We revered power and threw off the teachings of our old masters. It... we were not the first to fall to the dark side. But we... had more power than those before us. It... came from elsewhere...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Our oldest secret. Only... only we would know, we lords. Only we would know where our power came from...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «[Failure] I cannot, human. It is a secret of so long ago... I no longer remember. You who bristle with the Force... you must find this place...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «[Failure] The Force no longer touches my mind, human. And... even if it did I, I no longer remember our secret. The Force bristles in you... you must find this place with it...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Or... have you? Or did you? Or... will you? Oh, so... many images...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «I... see your heart, human Jedi. I see your power, your pride. You... you will find the old place, the dark place... and you will regret it.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «I... see your dark heart, human. Dark as mine was, proud as mine was. You... you will find the old place... and you will regret it.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «So much power... it is blinding. I...I remember so little...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «We... hid from the Jedi, but it was not they who destroyed us. Is it not... obvious what we did? We destroyed each other. We desired the secrets of each other, to increase our power... we battled until finally our fortress rained down on top of us.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «And so here our old secret is buried and none of us hold it any more... is that not right? Our power fled. Oh, what became of us? Do... do the Sith still thrive? Did they ever return?» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «So much... so much time has passed. And yet we have learned nothing... nothing...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Remain? Do I... remain? I... have regretted for so long... all that I have done.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «I... I do not remember. A map? Perhaps it is a map. But it has not been buried with me. All I have now is my sword.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «My sword... I filled it with my pride. And it is... buried with me, now. A corpse as I am a corpse. I am dead, as my faith... is dead. And I shall remain here... surrounded by blackness in death as in life...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Most of my brethren would desire... only to take what power I have left. Even if it would destroy them.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Of course you do. You... seek my power. Will it destroy you... as it did me?» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «I... cannot allow you to leave. Not until you have... done what I will ask of you.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «I wish my sword to be... taken away from here. I do not wish it to rot away as I have. I command this of you.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «If... if you are wise, you will not keep it. In the end, it is what destroyed me...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Then you shall not leave this place. I... have power, still. I can... still command!» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «There are... three blades within my sarcophagus. Only one is truly my sword, but it has been so long... I do not remember which. Find the sword that is mine and... place it on the statue. If it is truly mine, then it is yours.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Then you must... die. This is how it... must be.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «I do remember one thing of my sword... listen to me carefully... "I am that which grips the heart in fright, hearkens night and silences the light." It was... written of my sword long... long ago. Go, then... find my sword and place it on the statue...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Yes... that is the one. That is the blade that destroyed me. Take it... take it and the other blades, even... take them and go. My darkness awaits me...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «No... need? What choice have I?» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Return? But... I betrayed my old masters. They would never let me return to the light side. It is too late... too late...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «[Success] If... if I could return. Oh, my Master... it has been... so long... and I regret so much...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «[Failure] No... I... do not think so. I have the eternity... I deserved. I made my choice long ago.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «No, I cannot. Here... here is where I rest. Here is where my eternity awaits.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Then I have... the eternity I deserved. It has taken me... so long to realize that. I made my choice long ago.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Be at peace...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall] *(+1 exact-duplicate / he-she variant line(s) merged)*
- «Our... oldest secret is buried, but not here. I... remember nothing else. I cannot help you.» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Do not... do not say such things! I... still have power! Go! Go from my... ancient tomb!» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «I shall not tell you... our oldest secret! I shall not! Begone!» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Do not let the dark flame guide you, young one...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Take the blades and leave me to my darkness...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Then feel my power! You shall... die here, with me!!» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «Never! I shall... destroy you first! Die here... remain forever with me!» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]
- «No... that is not the one. My ancient sword is black as the night sky... I remember now. I... I am sorry...» [K1/Tomb_of_Ajunta_Pall]

### Quote bank A: Belaya's own lines, by scene (page)

**[K1/Grove]**
- «You have done a great thing. One of our own had strayed, but you have returned Juhani to the Order. For this you deserve the highest praise.» [K1/Grove]
- «Juhani was a... a dear companion to me for many years. We spent many nights together alone under the stars. That will never happen again, thanks to you. Curse the Council for sending you, of all people, to speak to her! Could you have done nothing else?!» [K1/Grove]
- «She had it coming, did she? She had it coming?!» [K1/Grove]
- «Do not presume to tell me how I should speak of anything! They should have known better than to entrust such a task to you!» [K1/Grove]
- «Pardon me if I find your apology rather disingenuous. Juhani meant nothing to you, and the Council should have known better than to entrust her life to you.» [K1/Grove]
- «Why? Because the Council believed that my closeness to her might do more harm than good... and yet they send *you*, instead? And look what has happened.» [K1/Grove]
- «The Juhani I knew deserved more than this! She deserved more from the Council, and more from you! You... you did not even try, did you? Did it even occur to you that Juhani might have been saved? That she might be worth saving? I should kill you for what you've done! And yet I can't, can I? I am a Jedi, and we are sworn to protect all... even the likes of you. Get... get out of my sight! The very thought of the Council... and of you... makes me ill.» [K1/Grove]
- «Master Zhar tells me you have successfully completed the tests, and I even see you have constructed your own lightsaber. You should be proud of your accomplishment… but never forget that you still have much to learn. As your power grows you will constantly face temptations to slip to the dark side. This is the eternal struggle of the Jedi.» [K1/Grove]

**[K1/Jedi_Enclave]**
- «You there! Padawan! Why are you not wearing the customary robes of the Jedi? Do you mock the honored traditions of our Order?» [K1/Jedi_Enclave]
- «My name is Belaya. I have come here to further my learning in the ways of the Force. Many Jedi come here to train under Master Zhar. Any who belong to the Order should surely know this. You must be neglecting your studies, Padawan.» [K1/Jedi_Enclave]
- «Bastila? I have heard of her. They say she has already mastered the art of Battle Meditation, remarkable in one so young. Though I have heard she has a foolish pride in her own talents. But as for you... you claim you are not a Padawan? I find this hard to believe. The Force is strong within you. I can feel its presence. If this is some type of jest, it is in very poor taste. The Jedi Order is not a subject for jokes.» [K1/Jedi_Enclave]
- «No, I suppose you did not. I apologize.» [K1/Jedi_Enclave]
- «Please forgive the abruptness with which I first greeted you. It was harsh, and perhaps unfair. My Master often warns me I must learn to control my emotions. I see I have much left to learn.» [K1/Jedi_Enclave]
- «I wish you a pleasant stay here on Dantooine. May the Force be with you.» [K1/Jedi_Enclave]
- «Greetings, apprentice... though I understand you shall not have that lowly title much longer. Master Zhar tells me your progress has been remarkable. Soon, he feels, you will attain the rank of Padawan. Then you will truly be a full-fledged Jedi. How are you enjoying your training?» [K1/Jedi_Enclave]
- «[[../Grove#Jedi Council» [K1/Jedi_Enclave]
- «You speak as if this were some foolish game! Have you no idea what is at stake?» [K1/Jedi_Enclave]
- «With power comes responsibility, and only by learning discipline and sacrifice can we truly learn to master our potential. I wish you luck in your training, apprentice. There is much you must yet learn. May the Force be with you.» [K1/Jedi_Enclave]
- «There are caves on Dantooine that hold those rare crystals. But be warned, apprentice: they are dangerous. You should not meddle in things beyond you.» [K1/Jedi_Enclave]
- «I wish you luck in your training, apprentice. There is much you must yet learn. May the Force be with you.» [K1/Jedi_Enclave]
- «All Jedi must know the code. Its tenets are the fundamental teachings of our Order.» [K1/Jedi_Enclave]
- «Think and meditate on these truths, apprentice, if you ever wish to become a Padawan.» [K1/Jedi_Enclave]

**[K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]**
- «Shen and Rahasia have been welcomed here in the compound for as long as they wish to stay. I am glad that your help enabled them to remain together.» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]
- «A battle took place earlier to the south of the Enclave. A lot of people died and many droids were destroyed. I wonder what dark force could have caused this to happen...» [K1/Sandral-Matale_Feud]

**[K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]**
- «You! I remember you! What are you doing here?! Did the Jedi Council send you? I swear I will expose you if they have!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «So you do not even have the decency to remember me? I have cursed your name every moment since we last met on Dantooine.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «But you will! You pretend to be a Sith, but I know who you are! I have cursed your name every moment since we last met on Dantooine!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «[Failure] You do not fool me for a second! The Council fawns over you and places all their hope in you to save them... you wouldn't turn on them!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «[Success] I see. So you have come to join the Sith as I have? I should have known that would happen, as should the Council.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «I am Belaya, once of the Jedi but now of the Sith... it was your murder of Juhani that placed this bitter blackness in my heart!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «You are a spy... and a murderer. It was you who killed Juhani and placed this bitter blackness in my heart that I cannot get rid of!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «The Council is nothing but a slow and doddering farce, placing all their trust in you... and you failed them, just as you failed Juhani.» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «I couldn't touch you on Dantooine, but I can, here! The Sith have taught me the power of freedom... and revenge! FOR JUHANI!!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]
- «Do you even remember me? I am Belaya, and it is your murder of Juhani that brought me here. Fortunately, I have learned the power of revenge!» [K1/Sith_Academy_Entrance]

- Vandar Tokare: «Heed her example: all are susceptible to the lure of the dark side. We must always remain vigilant against it. Go to Master Zhar and inform him that the Grove has been cleansed. I think you may be nearing the end of your apprenticeship.» [K1/Grove]
- Vrook Lamar: «For good or ill, you are now a true Padawan. The time has come for you and Bastila to investigate the dream you shared. The secrets to stopping Malak may lie hidden within the ancient Dantooine ruins you both saw in your visions.» [K1/Grove]
- Deesra: «I hear that Master Zhar feels you are now ready to become a full fledged member of our Order. Congratulations, apprentice. Or should I say Padawan? But I am certain you will not be one of those. So, Padawan – what can I do for you?» [K1/Grove]

**[K1/Jedi_Enclave]**

## Keeper Orsaa and The One (Rakata)
*Corpus coverage: 126 own lines (every line, no length filter) · 36 lines naming them by name, alias or epithet · 0 pronoun references resolved from context · 1 lines addressed to them · 3 narration lines · 9 duplicate/variant lines merged (noted inline). Every «quote» is script-verified.*
### Background and history
The Rakata were the ancient empire that built the Star Forge: «Long ago the Rakata ruled all the known galaxy» [K1/Elder_Settlement]. «Our ships combined technology and the power of the Force» [K1/Elder_Settlement]. The Elders (Keeper Orsaa) and The One's tribe are its degraded descendants.
### Personality and motivations
The Elders are penitent; The One is a would-be conqueror.
### Arc (scene by scene)
- Unknown World: the Elders vs The One. The player sides with one of them.

- Revan, who visited before the war.

- Empire-scale.
