<!-- 023-kotor-and-revan/kotor-2-notes.md (verbatim copy) -->

# Star Wars Knights of the Old Republic II: The Sith Lords (KOTOR 2): power-scaling notes (Oct 19, 2026)

**Method:** the source is a game walkthrough wiki (StrategyWiki, 148 K2 content pages fetched via api.php on Oct 19, 2026; raw copies in `raw/kotor/`). That makes it intermediate depth: it transcribes most dialog and describes cutscenes, but it is **not the game itself**. Gameplay stats are mechanics, low weight. The citation `K2/<Page>` means the StrategyWiki subpage `Star Wars Knights of the Old Republic II: The Sith Lords/<Page>`. Many lines exist in he/she variants; one is quoted. The wiki also covers Restored Content Mod material on a separate page. Nothing below relies on it unless marked.

## 1. Plot by planet
- **Prologue / Peragus II** (Administration Level, Mining Tunnels, Dormitories, Fuel Depot, Asteroid Exterior, Harbinger): the Exile wakes in a dead mining facility with Kreia and Atton.
  - On the Harbinger, Kreia confronts Darth Sion: «This battle is mine alone. I am not defenseless.»
  - Sion: «I sense you, my Master. Faint... weak.» Kreia: «Your senses betray you. As you betrayed me.»
  - Then «He spins and severs her hand with his lightsaber, and you clutch your own hand». This is the Exile–Kreia bond (K2/Engine_Deck).
  - Peragus is destroyed as the Ebon Hawk escapes.
- **Telos: Citadel Station** (Entertainment/Residential modules, Restoration Zone, Czerka): HK-50 droids, the Exchange, Ithorian conflicts. On the **Polar Plateau** the party reaches Atris's Secret Academy. Atris tasks the Exile with finding the lost Jedi Masters.
- **The four Master worlds** (any order):
  - **Nar Shaddaa**: Refugee Sector, Jekk'Jekk Tarr, Goto's Yacht. Zez-Kai Ell is found. Visas returns. Goto is revealed as G0-T0.
  - **Dantooine**: Khoonda, Crystal Cave, Enclave Sublevel. Vrook is found. Defense of Khoonda.
  - **Dxun/Onderon**: Mandalore's camp, Battle Circle, Iziz, Royal Palace. The civil war against General Vaklu. Kavar is found.
  - **Korriban**: Sith Academy, Shyrack Cave, Secret Tomb. Sion appears at the academy hub. The Exile fights him. Sion «restores himself to maximum», Kreia orders a retreat, and the party flees (K2/Sith_Academy). The Secret Tomb holds visions of Malak, Bastila and others (K2/Secret_Tomb).
  - **Visas Marr** ambushes the Ebon Hawk early on, loses, and yields (K2/Visas).
- **Rebuilt Jedi Enclave (Dantooine)** (K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave, K2/Lost_Jedi): the three surviving Masters explain the exile («You are a breach that must be closed»), call the Exile's bonds the template for the new Sith hunger, and try to cut the Exile from the Force. Kreia intervenes, drains all three, and leaves for Telos.
- **Telos return / Atris** (K2/Telos): Atris has fallen («She said you were the last obstacle to my enlightenment»). The Exile defeats her (her Handmaiden sisters fall first). The Sith fleet attacks Telos and Kreia becomes Darth Traya.
- **Ravager** (Command Deck, Bridge): Nihilus incapacitates the whole party, Visas disrupts the link, and Nihilus is defeated and dies (K2/Bridge). The Ravager is destroyed.
- **Malachor V** (Depths, Surface, Trayus Academy, Trayus Crescent/Proving Grounds, Trayus Core):
  - The Ebon Hawk crashes.
  - Sion is beaten four times and lets himself die (K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)).
  - Traya is defeated in the Core, falls, then dies after her prophecies (K2/Trayus_Core).
  - Bao-Dur's remote and the mass shadow generator destroy Malachor again (K2/Depths).

## 2. Key quotes (verified)
- Kreia on the three Lords: «One bathes in pain, feeds on it for sustenance. The other has ceased being a living being, so consumed by hunger than he has forgotten his own flesh. And the last is a creature of betrayals» (K2/Kreia)
- Kreia on Nihilus: «his mere presence kills all around him, slowly, feeding him. He is already dead, it is simply a question of how many he kills before he falls.» Also: «He is more of a hole in the Force than a living thing... Katarr called out as a beacon to him, and he could not resist it.» (K2/Kreia)
- Kreia on her own fall: «There are techniques within the Force against which there is no defense. I was cast down. Stripped of my power. Exiled.» (K2/Kreia)
- Kreia's "techniques" claim from before her return as Traya: «perhaps what the greatest of the Sith Lords knew of evil, they learned from me?» (K2/Kreia)
- Kreia at the Enclave: «Die? No - became stronger, yes.» and «Let me show you - you, who have forever seen the galaxy through the Force. See it through the eyes of the exile.» [narration: «Kreia drains them, and they collapse»]. On examining a body afterwards: «[This master is dead... drained of life. His body is worse than lifeless, it's like an absence in the Force.]» (K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave)
- Vrook to the Exile: «You are a cipher, forming bonds, leeching the life of others, siphoning their will and dominating them... You are a breach that must be closed.» (K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave)
- Zez-Kai Ell: «You feed on it, and you grow stronger. You're like Malachor... killing hundreds - only to become more and more powerful.» (K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave)
- Vrook: «we have the power to cut you off from the Force, and it must be done.» (K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave)
- Kavar: «When we felt Katarr die, there is something we felt... An echo in the Force. We'd felt it before when you stood before us.» (K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave)
- Traya: «I have used you so that you might become strong, stronger than I.» Also: «You are greater than any I have ever trained.» (K2/Trayus_Core)
- Traya on the Exile: «You are beautiful to me, exile. A dead spot in the Force» (K2/Trayus_Core)
- Atris: «the greatest of her apprentices comes to Telos. And he will destroy everything, just as he destroyed Katarr and all the Jedi gathered there.» (K2/Telos)
- Sion: «I have come farther than any of her apprentices since Revan... even you... and I am stronger for it.» (K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top))
- Sion: «And you have not bested her in battle, as I have.» (K2/Sith_Academy)
- Sion: «As long as the dark places of this world flow through the cracks of my flesh, I cannot be killed.» Also: «I can die a hundred times, exile, and still I will rise again, as strong as before.» (K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top))
- Sion: «You are strong... I cannot see as she does, but I know, that in time, you shall surpass her power. But not here, not in this place.» (K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top))
- Sion, after his last defeat: «You... have defeated me... flesh and belief, both cast down.» (K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top))
- Kreia on Korriban: «Sion. He cannot be defeated... he is not a beast of flesh and blood. This is not a battle that can be won. Flee.» and «There will be another time. But it is not now, not here, while Korriban runs through him.» (K2/Sith_Academy)
- Visas to Nihilus: «You... you are the darkness in which all life dies, milord. All life... exists to feed your power» (K2/Visas)
- Visas: «As he is a seam in the fabric of the Force, he senses fractures and weaknesses around him.» (K2/Iziz_Cantina)
- Mandalore: «Only Revan was worthy of our respect. We swept through the Outer Rim without any opposition - until Revan assumed command of the Republic forces.» (K2/Mandalore)
- HK-47: «Revan was a human capable of subtle, intelligent cruelties... His meatbag apprentice was more like an angry club.» (K2/HK-47)
- Handmaiden: «Only Revan ever demonstrated such skill in war. And even as he slaughtered us, the Echani still respected him.» (K2/Handmaiden)
- Kreia: «Was it the Republic that defeated Malak, or was it Revan? If it was Revan, then the Republic was never truly tested.» (K2/Hangar_Bay)
- Kreia on the Sith assassins: «They feed... and grow stronger... when they are near Force Sensitives.» (K2/Hangar_Bay)

## 3. Boss fights and duels (outcomes)
Gameplay stats from the wiki's NPC blocks: L = level, VP = Vitality, FP = Force points, Def = Defense.

| Fight | Page | Stats | Outcome |
|---|---|---|---|
| Kreia vs Sion (Harbinger) | K2/Engine_Deck | — | Sion severs Kreia's hand and the Exile feels it. Kreia sends the Exile away. Both survive. Edge: Sion at the time. Kreia is post-exile and weakened. |
| Visas vs Exile (Ebon Hawk) | K2/Visas | Visas L14 | Exile wins: «My lightsaber... you have destroyed it. I yield» |
| Sion vs Exile (Korriban) | K2/Sith_Academy | Sion L20, VP 322, FP 180, Def 43 | At <50% Sion "restores himself to maximum". Kreia says flee and the party runs. Sion: «Do not harm her. I command it.» Draw/retreat. |
| Kavar vs Exile (Onderon, optional dark path) | K2/Royal_Palace | Kavar L18, VP 261 | Exile wins, then «you subconsciously drain Force power from him». |
| Kreia vs Vrook, Kavar and Zez-Kai Ell | K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave | Masters L21 | **Kreia drains all three at once.** They die (bodies are "like an absence in the Force"). The Masters had just begun severing the Exile. |
| Atris vs Exile | K2/Telos | Atris L21, VP 252, FP 513, Def 43 | Exile wins. Her powers include Drain Life, Horror, Shock and Force Suppression. |
| **Nihilus vs Exile (+Visas, party)** | K2/Bridge | Nihilus L21, VP 1004, FP 615, Def 48; Slow, Force Scream, Wound, Drain Life/Force, Shock | Nihilus «incapacitates the entire party». When he moves to drain the Exile he «suddenly reels back, collapsing». Visas disrupts the link, and if she sacrifices herself he is cut to 20% Vitality. **Exile wins, assisted.** |
| **Sion vs Exile (Malachor, Trayus Academy)** | K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top) | Sion L23, VP 488, FP 415, Def 47 | Exile must «defeat him four times». Persuasion erodes his will, and he finally drops and dies willingly. **Exile wins, decisively.** |
| **Traya vs Exile (Trayus Core)** | K2/Trayus_Core | Traya L23, VP 1180, FP 1221, Def 51; 12 powers incl. Drain Force, Force Suppression, Force Deflection | Two phases, then **three floating lightsabers**. Traya «drops from the platform into Malachor's... core». She then dies after the final dialog: «You are greater than any I have ever trained.» **Exile wins.** |
| Kreia vs Sion (Trayus Core intro) | K2/Malachor_V | — | Seven Sith assassins «drop dead as she approaches the core». Sion: «I spared you once, I will not do so again.» Kreia re-asserts mastership. The wiki doesn't show a fight. |

## 4. Force powers and abilities shown
- Playable powers (K2/Force_powers):
  - KOTOR 1's set plus Heal, Force Barrier/Body, Mind Trick, Force Deflection, Force Scream, Revitalize, Battle Meditation, Drain Force.
  - Prestige-only powers: Force Sight, Beast Trick, Breath Control, Inspire Followers, Force Enlightenment, Crush Opposition, Fury, Force Crush, Precognition and Battle Precognition, Force Camouflage.
  - Lightsaber forms are listed on K2/Forms.
- Story-level abilities:
  - **Force bonds.** Exile and Kreia share pain, as with the severed hand. The Masters call the Exile's bonding a form of domination: «siphoning their will».
  - **Force drain on a group.** Kreia kills three Masters at once.
  - **Severing from the Force.** The Masters claim they can do it («we have the power to cut you off from the Force»). Kreia was "stripped of my power" by her apprentices.
  - **Planet-scale hunger.** Katarr died to Nihilus: the Masters (K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave) and Atris (K2/Telos) both say so. The mechanism is told, not shown.
  - **Party-wide incapacitation.** Nihilus disables the whole party on sight (K2/Bridge).
  - **Dark-side undeath.** Sion heals at will on dark-side worlds. Kreia ties it to the location: «while Korriban runs through him».
  - **Telekinetic multi-saber.** Traya's three floating lightsabers.
  - **Mind-wipe.** Kreia to the Disciple: «All thoughts of me will slide from your vision, from your mind, like water.» (K2/Disciple)


> **SUPER-DEEP CHARACTER WRITE-UPS (Oct 19, 2026):** all 24 analyses for this game were rewritten in full. Each has background, personality, a scene-by-scene arc with light/dark variants, relationships, abilities (lore, plus mechanics flagged as such), feats, statements, duels, cut content (RCM), verdict implications, gaps and complete verbatim quote banks. They are in **[kotor-2-characters/](kotor-2-characters.md)**, split out for size. The shorter analyses below are kept as summaries.

## 5. Character analyses
### The Exile (player character)
- **Feats:**
  - Beats Visas, Atris, Sion (four times on Malachor) and Traya.
  - Defeats Nihilus with Visas's help. Nihilus's drain fails on the Exile and "reels back".
  - Optionally drains Kavar's Force.
- **Statements:**
  - Traya: "stronger than I", "greater than any I have ever trained".
  - Sion: "in time, you shall surpass her power".
  - The Masters: "a breach", grows stronger through death.
  - Mandalore: «You turned a demoralized, defeated mess into a coordinated army» (K2/Mandalore).
  - Atris, earlier: «He is no Jedi - he walked Revan's path, but he was not strong enough.» (K2/Lost_Jedi). This is an early-game assessment.
- **Arc:** cut off from the Force after Malachor → reconnects, grows by forming bonds → kills the three Lords.
- **Verdict impact:** endgame Exile > Sion (high), > Traya (in-game result, medium-high), > Nihilus (assisted, with a specific counter; medium). The Exile's wins come with a hard-counter caveat: Nihilus can't drain a "dead spot", and the Exile is "beautiful" to Sion.

### Kreia / Darth Traya
- **Feats:**
  - Drains three Jedi Masters at once.
  - Seven assassins die as she walks past.
  - Fights Sion on the Harbinger while weakened, losing a hand.
  - Mind-wipes the Disciple.
  - The final boss: three floating sabers and the game's highest stats.
- **Statements:**
  - Taught Revan and implies that the greatest Sith learned evil from her (K2/Kreia).
  - Was overthrown by Sion and Nihilus: "techniques within the Force against which there is no defense".
- **Arc:** mentor → Traya → wants to kill the Force through the Exile → dies at the Exile's hands, satisfied.
- **Verdict impact:**
  - Traya > Sion in a fair fight: she is his master, and Sion admits «She... cannot be defeated» (K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)). Caveat: «And you have not bested her in battle, as I have».
  - Traya vs Nihilus is complicated by the sever-Force technique that already beat her once.
  - Vs Revan: the teacher claim doesn't make her stronger.

### Darth Nihilus
- **Feats:**
  - Incapacitates the whole party.
  - Hurls Visas across the room.
  - Chokes Visas through the Force (K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck).
  - Destroyed Katarr: told, not shown (Masters, Atris).
- **Statements:**
  - Kreia: "hole in the Force", "already dead", "not something he can direct or focus".
  - Visas: "darkness in which all life dies".
  - Atris: "the greatest of her apprentices".
- **Arc:** a cameo boss. His hunger collapses on him when he tries to drain the Exile.
- **Verdict impact:** the highest destructive scale of the three Lords on statements (planet drain). His duel showing is weak because he is hard-countered. Kreia frames the hunger as uncontrolled, which caps his use of it in a versus match.

### Darth Sion
- **Feats:**
  - Severs Kreia's hand.
  - Fully heals mid-fight.
  - Survives four defeats.
  - Commands assassin squads.
- **Statements:**
  - «farther than any of her apprentices since Revan».
  - Admits that Traya can't be beaten and that the Exile will surpass her.
  - «I fear no one, for while the Force is with me I yet live.»
- **Arc:** pain-bound undead → falls in love with the Exile → «I am glad to leave this place... at last.»
- **Verdict impact:** confirms the current tier-7 placement. The wiki adds the location dependence ("while Korriban runs through him", "the dark places of this world") to the immortality caveat.

### Atton Rand
- **Feats:** a former Sith Jedi-hunter. On a captured Jedi: «I hurt her. I hurt her a lot. And then... she showed me the Force.» (K2/Atton)
- **Statements:** Revan trained «elite Sith units into assassination squads» (K2/Atton).
- **Arc:** pazaak cynic → latent Force user.
- **Verdict impact:** low (Knight at best). A useful witness on Revan's anti-Jedi doctrine.

### Visas Marr
- **Feats:** ambushes and loses to the Exile. Disrupts Nihilus's link, and her sacrifice is the decisive move in that fight.
- **Statements:** Nihilus "senses fractures and weaknesses"; the Exile's "greatness... does not stem from the Force".
- **Arc:** Nihilus's slave → the Exile's student → frees herself.
- **Verdict impact:** her bond to Nihilus is why Nihilus loses. The Exile-vs-Nihilus result therefore can't be read straight.

### Mandalore (Canderous)
- **Statements:**
  - «Only Revan was worthy of our respect».
  - «Were it not for Revan's strength, the Republic would already be dead.»
  - «I have reclaimed Mandalore's helmet, lost after our defeat at Revan's hands» (K2/Enclave_Courtyard).
- **Feats:** reunites the clans on Dxun. A party fighter (non-Force).
- **Verdict impact:** strategic hype for Revan and the Exile.

### The Handmaiden (Brianna)
- **Feats:** Echani unarmed duels with the Exile ("I have already learned much in our duels"). Sides against Atris.
- **Statements:** Revan defeated the Echani; Revan "speaking to Malak" through the final battle; "Only Revan ever demonstrated such skill in war".
- **Verdict impact:** low (martial artist). Revan hype.

### The Jedi Masters (Vrook, Kavar, Zez-Kai Ell)
- **Feats:**
  - Survived the Sith purge by dispersing.
  - Kavar kills two Vaklu soldiers on entry and heals (K2/Royal_Palace).
  - Together they begin severing the Exile from the Force.
- **Their end:** all three are drained by Kreia at once.
- **Statements:** they felt the Katarr echo and the Exile echo; Zez: «I, too, lost a Padawan on Malachor» (K2/Goto's_Yacht).
- **Verdict impact:** sets Traya's floor. Three council-level Masters fall to one drain from Kreia, who was still unrevealed as Traya. (They were mid-ritual and distracted. Low stats are not lore.)

### Atris
- **Feats:** commands a Sith holocron library; lightning-kills the Handmaiden. Loses to the Exile.
- **Statements:** set the Exile up as bait for the Sith; calls Nihilus the greatest of Traya's apprentices.
- **Verdict impact:** a Master-tier benchmark below the Exile.

### HK-47, G0-T0, Bao-Dur, Mira, Hanharr, Disciple (brief)
- **HK-47:** a Revan intel witness (Malachor V was "cleaning house"; Malak an "angry club").
- **G0-T0:** controls the mass shadow generator plot (K2/Depths).
- **Bao-Dur:** built the mass shadow generator: «what I did at Malachor» (K2/Bao-Dur).
- **Mira and Hanharr:** low Force scale.
- **Disciple:** «There was no Sith leader since Revan who has survived long enough to rule».

## 6. Gaps
- The wiki has no scene of the Nihilus/Sion betrayal of Traya and no Kreia-vs-Sion fight at the Trayus Core. The Restored Content Mod page was fetched but not mined.
- The Peragus and Telos pages were scanned by keyword, not read line by line. Influence-dependent dialogs may hold more Kreia lore.
- Gameplay stats (VP, FP) are not lore and should not drive verdicts.

---

# DEEP PASS (Oct 19, 2026, second read)

Method: second pass over the same 148 saved K2 content pages. Nothing was re-fetched. The pages were parsed into a full ordered digest (about 2.5 MB), a narration-only plot file (which was read in full), and 25 per-character dossiers (the character's own top lines plus lines that mention them). These were cross-checked with targeted greps of dialog/ALL.tsv. Every «quote» below was machine-checked as a substring of the saved wiki text. Citations use the form [K2/<Page>]. "(RCM)" marks cut content restored by the Restored Content Mod.

## D1. Backstory and history (as told in game)
- **Mandalorian Wars.** Revan and Malak defy the Council: «Two Jedi Knights, Revan and Malak, defied the Jedi Council.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]. «It was Revan who drove the Mandalorians back into the Unknown Regions.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]. Mandalore on the pre-Revan war: «We swept through the Outer Rim without any opposition - until Revan assumed command of the Republic forces.» [K2/Mandalore]. Atton recalls the atrocities, including Serroco and Duro: «The Mandalorians were slaughtering us by the millions.» [K2/Atton].
- **Malachor V.** Bao-Dur built the mass shadow generator and owns it: «undo the damage that was done in Malachor V... the damage I did» [K2/Depths]. He also says: «When I built weapons to destroy them, the Mandalorians were destroyed.» [K2/Bao-Dur]. The Exile was the General who gave the command, and Kavar describes the cost: «it is a wonder that you did not die there when thousands perished» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]. Survival came by cutting off the Force: «You cut yourself off, because you had to if you were to survive. You had hints of it on the war on Dxun. Malachor was simply the final blow.» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave].
- **The Exile's trial.** Kavar: «it was as if the Force had died within you, leaving you hollow» [K2/Royal_Palace]. Vash, in the Korriban vision: «The dark side is not what I sensed in the exile.» [K2/Lost_Jedi]. Zez-Kai Ell left the Order the day of the exile [K2/Goto's_Yacht]. Atris admits she later leaked the Exile's location as bait: «So I arranged for you to return to the Republic, leaked information of your past, and then waited for the Sith to come.» [K2/Telos].
- **Jedi Civil War.** «Revan was ambushed by the Jedi and captured. Malak continued to wage war in his Master's place» [K2/Hangar_Bay]. «By the war's end, barely a hundred Jedi remained.» [K2/Hangar_Bay]. G0-T0 adds a secret: «It is unknown to all but a few, but the Republic lost the Jedi Civil War.» This is the variant for a light-side Revan; the Sith-victory variant reads «the Sith won the Jedi Civil War» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]. According to Atton, Revan ran Jedi-hunting units: «So Revan trained elite Sith units into assassination squads» [K2/Atton].
- **Aftermath and Katarr.** The Jedi call a secret conclave: «Before the meeting could even begin, all the Jedi on the planet were killed, along with the Miralukas and all other life on the planet.» [K2/Royal_Palace]. The Masters then scatter as bait [K2/Goto's_Yacht].
- **Kreia's history and the Triumvirate.** Kreia trained Revan: «At one time, Revan was my Padawan.» [K2/Kreia]. Revan's other masters were «Zhar, Dorak, Master Kae before Kae left for the Wars» [K2/Enclave_Sublevel]. Kreia then fell at Malachor's academy: «There are techniques within the Force against which there is no defense. I was cast down. Stripped of my power. Exiled.» [K2/Kreia]. The three Lords are described as: «One bathes in pain, feeds on it for sustenance. The other has ceased being a living being, so consumed by hunger than he has forgotten his own flesh. And the last is a creature of betrayals» [K2/Kreia]. The Trayus Academy sits at «the threshold of the borders of an ancient empire» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)], and Traya says Malachor and Korriban «brushes the edges of the empire that waits in the dark» [K2/Trayus_Core].
- **The Handmaiden's parentage.** Her mother was Arren Kae, a Jedi Master exiled for having a child, who later joined Revan [K2/Handmaiden]. Her father was killed by Revan: «He was slain by Revan in the Jedi Civil War when Revan sought to destabilize the Echani worlds.» [K2/Handmaiden].

## D2. Scene-by-scene plot (expanded)
- **Peragus.** The Exile wakes in the medbay. The miners were killed by their own droids, steered by HK-50, the "maintenance officer". Atton is found locked up in the security block. In the dormitories the Exile hears Kreia's voice through the bond. The asteroid explodes after the escape on the Ebon Hawk. Earlier, aboard the Harbinger, Kreia confronts Sion, loses a hand, and the Exile feels her pain through the bond: «The pain, however, was unexpected» [K2/Hangar_Bay].
- **Telos Citadel Station.** The Exchange (Luxa, Loppak Slusk) and Czerka's Jana Lorso set up a side choice between the Ithorians (Chodo Habat) and Czerka over the restoration contract. TSF Lt. Grenn and Admiral Onasi appear. The Exile is held by the TSF; the party's gear is recovered from "TSF detainee lockers" (walkthrough narration), and the Ebon Hawk is later reclaimed. Bao-Dur repairs the shuttle. On the Polar Plateau the party fights HK-50s. At the Secret Academy the Exile meets Atris and her Handmaidens. Atris turns down the Exile's offer of help and sends them to find the remaining Masters. Depending on the Exile's gender, the Handmaiden (male Exile) or the Disciple (female Exile) joins the crew.
- **Nar Shaddaa.** Mira (light side) or Hanharr (dark side) follows the Exile. The Exchange puts a bounty on Jedi. Key scenes: the Jekk'Jekk Tarr gas den, Visquis, and Kreia binding Hanharr to hunt Mira [K2/Jekk'Jekk_Tarr_Tunnels]. Zez-Kai Ell turns out to have been in hiding as a refugee; Mira says he «hired me to watch out for you» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]. Goto's yacht: G0-T0 is revealed as "Goto" and explains his Republic-stability directive. Possible endings for Goto are capture or death.
- **Dxun and Onderon.** The new Mandalore (Canderous) holds the Mandalorian ruins. His tasks include the battle circle, where Bralor says «You always had hidden strengths» [K2/Battle_Circle]. Sith assassins attack Freedon Nadd's tomb. Onderon's civil war pits Queen Talia against General Vaklu; Mandalore pushes for Vaklu [K2/Iziz_Cantina]. Kavar is at the palace. On the dark path, Kavar is killed and Colonel Tobin says «Master Kavar is no match for you» [K2/Royal_Palace]. In the Sith tomb the Exile faces Nadd's echo and the Sith there.
- **Dantooine.** Khoonda vs Azkul's mercenaries. Vrook is either rescued or killed in the Crystal Cave, and teaches a Force form. The Enclave sublevel holds Jorran. Vandar appears as a hologram on the Khoonda plains [K2/Khoonda_Plains].
- **Korriban.** The academy is deserted. Lonna Vash lies dead [K2/Sith_Academy]. Sion confronts the Exile: «I know the paths you walked in exile. I know your teacher.» [K2/Sith_Academy]. In the Secret Tomb, visions show the Exile's trial (the "Lost Jedi" scene). A Kreia-versus-Atton argument also plays out there (partly RCM, see D5).
- **Rebuilt Jedi Enclave (the Masters' gathering).** The surviving Masters (Vrook, Kavar, Zez-Kai Ell) decide to sever the Exile from the Force. Kavar's verdict: «In you, we saw a wound in the Force.» Vrook calls the Exile «a cipher, forming bonds, leeching the life of others» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]. Kreia arrives, pushes the Council back with the Force, and "Kreia drains them, and they collapse" (narration) [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]. There is an alternative path if the Masters were killed earlier.
- **Telos assault.** Nihilus's Ravager arrives over Telos. Atris, surrounded by Sith holocrons («These holocrons hold much of the Sith», Kreia [K2/Telos]), fights the Exile [K2/Telos]. She reveals: «She seeks the death of all Jedi, all Sith... and the death of the Force.» [K2/Telos]. Kreia openly takes the name Darth Traya: «There must always be a Darth Traya.» [K2/Telos].
- **Ravager.** Mandalore's assault and the bridge: Visas (if a companion), the Exile and the party fight Nihilus, who dies. Tobin's line about the ship: «He tore it from the mass shadows of Malachor, along with his fleet... that is a measure of his power.» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck].
- **Malachor V.** The Ebon Hawk crashes. The party splits up: Mira vs Hanharr in the Depths, Bao-Dur's remote vs G0-T0, HK-47 vs HK-50s. In the Trayus Academy Sion is beaten four times. After the fourth defeat "he drops to his knees" (narration); the persuasion lines break his will. In the Trayus Core the Exile faces Kreia, who gives prophecies for each companion, then fights her with three floating lightsabers and kills her. The remote fires the mass shadow generator. The ending branches on alignment (the walkthrough uses an Alignment-40 threshold). Exact ending-text wording was not verified line by line.

## D3. Lore and power-scaling statements compilation
**Revan**
- «Revan was power. It was like staring into the heart of the Force. Even then, you could see the Jedi he would slay etched on his soul.» [K2/Kreia]
- «Only Revan was worthy of our respect.» [K2/Mandalore]. «Were it not for Revan's strength, the Republic would already be dead.» [K2/Mandalore]
- Revan studied Force bonds: «It was rumored that Revan studied such bonding deeply» [K2/Goto's_Yacht]
- Strategic foresight (G0-T0): «I believe that Revan saw a war on another front that we did not» [K2/G0-T0]
- Sion: «I have come farther than any of her apprentices since Revan» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]. This implicitly places Revan above Sion.

**The Exile**
- Atris: «You were strong enough to withstand it once - but few have your strength in such matters» [K2/Telos]
- HK-47: «Your single life changed the face of the galaxy, of history itself.» [K2/HK-47]
- Kavar: «You always did form connections to others, strong ones, even when you were a student.» [K2/Royal_Palace]
- Zez-Kai Ell: «You feed on it, and you grow stronger. You're like Malachor...» [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]
- Traya: «no Jedi ever made the choice you did. To sever ties so completely, so utterly, that it leaves a wound in the Force.» [K2/Trayus_Core]
- Tobin, on the dark-side Onderon path: «Your leadership and skill towers over your companions'.» [K2/Master_of_the_Palace]
- Caveat on the Exile's abilities: the Exile's power grows through bonds and through feeding on death, so it is circumstantial and does not set a fixed baseline.

**Nihilus**
- «He is more of a hole in the Force than a living thing.» [K2/Kreia]
- «perhaps he will grow strong enough to eradicate all life, merely with his presence.» [K2/Visas]
- «You... you are the darkness in which all life dies, milord.» [K2/Visas]
- Atris: «he will destroy everything, just as he destroyed Katarr and all the Jedi gathered there.» [K2/Telos]
- On the Ravager's fleet and hull (Tobin, above): «a measure of his power».
- KEY FEAT AND LIMITATION (narration, Ravager bridge): "Nihilus raises his hand to drain you, but suddenly reels back, collapsing on the ground". Visas can disrupt the link so that "Nihilus is incapacitated for one combat round". His drain backfires on the Exile.
- Limitation (Mandalore): «If he's so powerful, why hasn't he stopped us, then?» [K2/Ravager_-_Command_Deck]. Kreia: «Do not think of his power as one would a weapon» [K2/Kreia]

**Traya / Kreia**
- «Revan was my Padawan» [K2/Kreia]. She has her own account of Sion's loyalty to her: «Show him every respect when he arrives in these halls, Lord Sion. This I command you.» [K2/Trayus_Academy]
- Sion: «She... cannot be defeated.» This is on the [Success] branch, which is persuasion and partly framed rhetoric [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Atris: «She seeks the death of all Jedi, all Sith... and the death of the Force.» [K2/Telos]
- How she was cast down: «There are techniques within the Force against which there is no defense.» [K2/Kreia]
- Feat: she drains the three Masters at the Enclave ("Kreia drains them, and they collapse") [K2/Rebuilt_Jedi_Enclave]. On Malachor, "seven Sith assassins decloak... only to drop dead as she approaches" (narration) [K2/Trayus_Core area].

**Sion**
- «As long as the dark places of this world flow through the cracks of my flesh, I cannot be killed.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- «The power of the dark side is what holds me together.» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]
- Kreia: «of pain, he has learned much. Of knowledge, of teaching, he knows nothing.» [K2/Kreia]

**Malak (as seen in K2)**
- Revan is ranked above him: Kreia, «Was it the Republic that defeated Malak, or was it Revan?» [K2/Hangar_Bay]
- Mandalore says of Kavar, «I thought you were killed fighting Malak during the Jedi Civil War» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]. This is rumor only.


> **SUPER-DEEP CHARACTER WRITE-UPS (Oct 19, 2026):** all 24 analyses for this game were rewritten in full. Each has background, personality, a scene-by-scene arc with light/dark variants, relationships, abilities (lore, plus mechanics flagged as such), feats, statements, duels, cut content (RCM), verdict implications, gaps and complete verbatim quote banks. They are in **[kotor-2-characters/](kotor-2-characters.md)**, split out for size. The shorter analyses below are kept as summaries.

## D4. Character analyses (deep). 24 entries; the earlier analyses above remain valid.
1. **The Exile (Meetra Surik)**. Feats: in-game, kills Nihilus with the party, beats Sion repeatedly and then breaks his will, defeats Atris, defeats Traya, and (on the dark path) kills Kavar, Vrook and Zez. Statements: see D3. Arc: from a hollow outcast to someone reconnected to the Force; "a wound in the Force" that feeds through bonds. Verdict implications: the in-game record is the strongest of any K2 character, but it comes with party and bond assistance. Medium confidence for a neutral-ground ranking.
2. **Kreia / Darth Traya**. Feats: survives Sion's attack, minus a hand; drains three Masters; telepathically commands Hanharr and Atton («my punishment will make you beg for the death that has long hounded you» [K2/Polar_Plateau]); has a three-lightsaber telekinetic duel. She trained Revan and was cast down by Sion and Nihilus. Verdict: the best knowledge-based scaling of anyone in K2. Her duel record is mixed.
3. **Darth Nihilus**. Feats: destroyed Katarr; the Ravager and its fleet. Weakness: Visas and Kreia indicate he can be undone by the Exile's bonds and "echo". His single on-screen fight is a loss. Verdict: hax/AoE power at the top, duel credentials unproven.
4. **Darth Sion**. Feats: severs Kreia's hand; survives countless deaths through pain. «Many times have I been near death» [K2/Trayus_Academy_(Top)]. He loses each duel with the Exile and dies when his will breaks. Verdict: a durability specialist, below Traya and the Exile.
5. **Atton Rand**. Backstory: a Jedi-killer in Revan's assassination squads. «People say killing Jedi is hard. It's not, you just have to be smart about it.» [K2/Atton]. Methods include gas and drugs and wounding the Padawan first. A Jedi woman showed him the Force and died to protect his secret: «She wasted her life to save me.» [K2/Atton]. Traya's prophecy: «Atton shall keep his murderer's heart.» [K2/Trayus_Core] (variant). Verdict: a hunter of Jedi through tactics, with no raw-power feats.
6. **Visas Marr**. The sole survivor of Katarr and Nihilus's emissary, who yields to the Exile: «My lightsaber... you have destroyed it. I yield... master.» [K2/Visas]. A Miraluka who sees through the Force. Verdict: low-to-mid tier; important because she is the source of the main Nihilus statements.
7. **Mandalore (Canderous Ordo)**. Rallies the clans by reclaiming the helmet [K2/Enclave_Courtyard]. «I was at Malachor V.» [K2/Mandalorian_Ruins]. He relies on armor and cunning: «now I've got to rely more on my armor, my weapons, and my cunning.» [K2/Mandalore]. Verdict: a non-Force veteran whose main value is as a source of statements on Revan.
8. **The Handmaiden (Brianna)**. Echani, daughter of Arren Kae. Spars with the Exile: «I have already learned much in our duels» [K2/Handmaiden]. Atris strikes her with lightning when she defects (Telos). Verdict: elite unarmed combatant, low Force tier.
9. **The Disciple (Mical)**. A Republic scholar and former Jedi student who wanted the Exile as his master [K2/Disciple]. Keeps Jedi lore (Ossus, Arca, Nomi). Verdict: no meaningful feats.
10. **Mira**. Bounty hunter and a Mandalorian War refugee: «After Revan crushed the Mandalorians, planets throughout the Republic were flooded with refugees.» [K2/Mira]. Has the light-path fight with Hanharr in the Depths. Verdict: street level.
11. **Hanharr**. A Wookiee hunter exiled to the Shadowlands, enslaved by Czerka, bound by a life-debt to Mira. Kreia empowers him to hunt her. «I could crush you with one hand» [K2/Docks]. Verdict: a peak physical brute.
12. **G0-T0 (Goto)**. A droid crime lord, driven by a Republic-preservation directive: «preserve the Republic, or preserve the laws of the Republic» [K2/G0-T0]. Has data on Revan's production facilities. Verdict: an intelligence source rather than a combatant.
13. **Bao-Dur**. Iridonian engineer who built the Malachor mass shadow generator and the planetary shields. His remote fires the MSG again in the ending. Verdict: engineering hax; defined by the MSG's scale.
14. **HK-47**. Assassin droid that rebuilds itself in K2. Tallies Jedi kills: «it is difficult to determine who was a Jedi and who was not» [K2/HK-47]. Fights HK-50s (the HK factory page is missing from the wiki, so no factory feat is recorded). Verdict: elite non-Force combatant.
15. **T3-M4**. Utility droid; logs the Masters' deaths in its records (e.g. «Master Kavar: Onderon: Deceased.» [K2/T3-M4]). No combat scaling.
16. **Atris**. The last Jedi historian, corrupted by Sith holocrons. Admits she baited the Sith using the Exile [K2/Telos]. Fights the Exile and loses. Kreia: «I never destroyed Atris - she had destroyed herself.» [K2/Trayus_Core]. Verdict: mid-tier Master, defeated.
17. **Vrook Lamar**. Teaches a form at Khoonda, is killed or spared, and leads the severance attempt. His view of the Exile: «your ability to form such connections, to influence others, was always a subject of discussion» [K2/Crystal_Cave]. Verdict: a Master-level fodder victim of Traya.
18. **Kavar**. A famed guardian: «Kavar, huh? The famed Jedi guardian?» [K2/Iziz_Cantina]. The Mandalorians expected him to lead the Jedi. Dies to the dark Exile or to Traya. Verdict: a strong Master who never beats a protagonist on screen.
19. **Zez-Kai Ell**. Left the Order after the Exile's trial and hid on Nar Shaddaa [K2/Goto's_Yacht]. Has a "one-sided encounter" with the Exile on the dark path. Verdict: low for a Master.
20. **Vandar Tokare (K2 cameo)**. Appears only as a hologram in the Khoonda plains area [K2/Khoonda_Plains]. No feats in K2.
21. **Lonna Vash**. Found dead at the Korriban academy, killed by Sion [K2/Sith_Academy]. In the trial vision she objects to the exile ruling. Verdict: fodder for Sion.
22. **Colonel Tobin / General Vaklu**. Onderon military. Tobin is a source of statements about Nihilus and Kavar. No Force scaling.
23. **Azkul / Visquis / Loppak Slusk**. Mercenary and Exchange bosses, all defeated. Street tier.
24. **Revan (absent)**. Has no on-screen role in K2, but Kreia, Mandalore, G0-T0, Atton and the Handmaiden all scale Revan to the top. Revan left known space for "a war on another front". The scenes of Bastila joining Carth and Lt. Grenn depend on the Revan choices.

## D5. Restored Content Mod (cut content), clearly labeled
- (RCM) Additional party banter: Kreia's «Do not use the Force as a crutch» lesson, Atton's «Just like a Jedi.», and the Secret Tomb exchange in which several companions echo «Apathy is death.» There are also extra Hanharr dark lines and some RCM-only items.
- (RCM) No cut content changes the power scaling: the RCM pages have no Nihilus-Sion meeting, no HK factory battle, and no extra Revan scene. RCM therefore does not affect any verdict.

## D6. Deep-pass gaps
- Pages missing on the wiki: K2 Force, HK Manufacturing Plant, Lightsaber, Melee, Military Base Sub-Level, Ranged, Turret encounters.
- Combat outcomes come from walkthrough narration, not from cutscene scripts. Stats in the NPC templates are gameplay mechanics, not lore.
- The Exile is explicitly gender-variable (Meetra Surik is the Legends name; the game itself does not name the Exile). Several key lines have he/she variants; quotes use whichever variant the wiki page gives first.

# KOTOR 2: super-deep character write-ups (Oct 19, 2026)

Method: for every character, a script pulled ALL of their own lines and every line by others that names them, from raw/kotor/dialog/ALL2.tsv (45,051 transcribed lines, including 19,180 player dialogue options). It also pulled every walkthrough-narration line naming them, from the saved page text (digest/K2-narr.txt), and the gameplay stat blocks from the raw .wiki pages. Near-duplicate he/she variants are collapsed. ALL own lines are included regardless of length. Only exact duplicates and he/she variants are merged, and each merge is noted inline. 'About' lines cover names, aliases and epithets (chargen/aliases.py) plus pronoun references resolved from context (scene-wide, every candidate hand-reviewed and flagged [context-resolved] with its anchor line). A separate bank lists lines addressed to the character. The hand-written synthesis comes first. The quote banks follow it, verbatim and grouped by scene (page). Every «quote» is checked by verify_kotor_quotes.py. Game mechanics are flagged as mechanics. (RCM) marks Restored Content Mod material.

Characters (24): The Exile (player character); Kreia / Darth Traya; Darth Nihilus; Darth Sion; Atton Rand; Visas Marr; Mandalore (Canderous Ordo); The Handmaiden (Brianna); The Disciple (Mical); Mira; Hanharr; G0-T0 (Goto); Bao-Dur; HK-47 (KOTOR 2); T3-M4 (KOTOR 2); Atris; Master Vrook Lamar; Master Kavar; Master Zez-Kai Ell; Vandar Tokare (KOTOR 2 cameo); Master Lonna Vash; Colonel Tobin and General Vaklu; Azkul, Visquis and Loppak Slusk; Revan (absent, KOTOR 2)
